




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

vinayapiṭakai


pārājikapāḷi


vairañ‍jakaṇḍaṃ

1. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ viharati naḷairupucimandamūlai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| assausi khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau vairañ‍jāyaṃ viharati naḷairupucimandamūlai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā bhagavāti (syā॰), dī॰ ni॰ 1.157, abbhuggatākāraina pana samaiti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ  sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti; sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’’ti|

2. itau paraṃ yāva pārā॰ 15-16 padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmīti pāṭhau a॰ ni॰ 8.11 atha  khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama – ‘na samaṇau gautamau  brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuḍḍhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaitī’ti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, tathaiva? na hi bhavaṃ gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuḍḍhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaiti? tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, na sampan‍namaivā’’ti|

‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, brāhmaṇa, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yamahaṃ abhivādaiyyaṃ vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiyyaṃ vā āsanaina vā nimantaiyyaṃ| yañhi, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau abhivādaiyya vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiyya vā āsanaina vā nimantaiyya, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’’ti|

3. ‘‘arasarūpau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya  – ‘arasarūpau samaṇau gautamau’ti| yai tai, brāhmaṇa, rūparasā saddarasā gandharasā rasarasā phauṭṭhabbarasā tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā anabhāvakatā (sī॰) anabhāvaṃgatā (syā॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘arasarūpau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

4. ‘‘nibbhaugau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘nibbhaugau samaṇau gautamau’ti| yai tai, brāhmaṇa, rūpabhaugā saddabhaugā gandhabhaugā rasabhaugā phauṭṭhabbabhaugā tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘nibbhaugau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

5. ‘‘akiriyavādau  bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, akiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyaduc‍caritassa  vacīduc‍caritassa manauduc‍caritassa| anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ akiriyaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

6. ‘‘ucchaidavādau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, ucchaidaṃ vadāmi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa| anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ucchaidaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau  vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau  ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

7. ‘‘jaigucchī bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, jigucchāmi kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina| anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā jigucchāmi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

8. ‘‘vainayikau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa| anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

9. ‘‘tapassī bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau’ti| tapanīyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, pāpakai akusalai dhammai vadāmi, kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| yassa khau, brāhmaṇa, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā tamahaṃ tapassīti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, brāhmaṇa, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

10. ‘‘apagabbhau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau’ti| yassa khau, brāhmaṇa, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā tamahaṃ apagabbhauti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, brāhmaṇa, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisi’’|

11. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni sammā parisaiditāni sammā paribhāvitāni| yau nu khau taisaṃ kuk‍kuṭacchāpakānaṃ paṭhamataraṃ pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyya, kinti svāssa vacanīyau – ‘‘jaiṭṭhau  vā kaniṭṭhau vā’’ti? ‘‘jaiṭṭhautissa, bhau gautama, vacanīyau| sau hi naisaṃ jaiṭṭhau hautī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, avij‍jāgatāya pajāya aṇḍabhūtāya pariyaunaddhāya  avij‍jaṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā aikauva laukai anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau| svāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, jaiṭṭhau saiṭṭhau laukassa’’|

‘‘āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, brāhmaṇa, vīriyaṃ viriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) ahausi asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā appamuṭṭhā (sī॰ syā॰), passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| sau  khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca vihāsiṃ satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaisiṃ, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

12. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi, jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau; sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau; sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, rattiyā paṭhamai yāmai paṭhamā vij‍jā adhigatā, avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā, tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau – yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, paṭhamābhinibbhidā ahausi kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā|

13. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina atik‍kantamānussakaina (ka॰) sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai  suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai| sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina  saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai  hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai| sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, rattiyā majjhimai yāmai dutiyā vij‍jā adhigatā, avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā, tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau – yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, dutiyābhinibbhidā ahausi kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā|

14. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ  abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tassa mai aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahausi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tatiyā vij‍jā adhigatā, avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā, tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau – yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, tatiyābhinibbhidā  ahausi – kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā’’ti|

15. aivaṃ  vuttai, vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘jaiṭṭhau bhavaṃ gautamau, saiṭṭhau bhavaṃ gautamau! abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama!! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti, aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| adhivāsaitu ca mai bhavaṃ gautamau vairañ‍jāyaṃ vassāvāsaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

16. taina  khau pana samayaina vairañ‍jā dubbhikkhā hauti dvīhitikā saitaṭṭhikā salākāvuttā na sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| taina khau pana samayaina uttarāpathakā uttarāhakā (sī॰) assavāṇijā assavaṇijā (ka॰) pañ‍camattaihi assasataihi vairañ‍jaṃ vassāvāsaṃ upagatā haunti| taihi assamaṇḍalikāsu bhikkhūnaṃ patthapatthapulakaṃ patthapatthamūlakaṃ (ka॰) pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti| bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vairañ‍jaṃ piṇḍāya pavisitvā piṇḍaṃ alabhamānā assamaṇḍalikāsu piṇḍāya caritvā patthapatthapulakaṃ ārāmaṃ āharitvā udukkhalai kauṭṭaitvā kauṭṭaitvā paribhuñ‍janti| āyasmā panānandau patthapulakaṃ silāyaṃ pisitvā bhagavatau upanāmaiti| taṃ bhagavā paribhuñ‍jati|

assausi khau bhagavā udukkhalasaddaṃ| jānantāpi tathāgatā pucchanti, jānantāpi na pucchanti; kālaṃ viditvā pucchanti, kālaṃ viditvā na pucchanti; atthasaṃhitaṃ  tathāgatā pucchanti, nau anatthasaṃhitaṃ| anatthasaṃhitai saitughātau tathāgatānaṃ| dvīhi ākāraihi buddhā bhagavantau bhikkhū paṭipucchanti – dhammaṃ vā daisaissāma, sāvakānaṃ vā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmāti pañ‍ñāpaissāmāti (sī॰ syā॰)| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, udukkhalasaddau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, ānanda! tumhaihi, ānanda sappurisaihi vijitaṃ| pacchimā janatā sālimaṃsaudanaṃ atimañ‍ñissatī’’ti|

17. atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, vairañ‍jā dubbhikkhā dvīhitikā saitaṭṭhikā salākāvuttā| na sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| imissā, bhantai, mahāpathaviyā haiṭṭhimatalaṃ sampan‍naṃ – saiyyathāpi khuddamadhuṃ anīlakaṃ aivamassādaṃ| sādhāhaṃ, bhantai, pathaviṃ parivattaiyyaṃ| bhikkhū pappaṭakaujaṃ paribhuñ‍jissantī’’ti| ‘‘yai pana tai, mauggal‍lāna, pathavinissitā pāṇā tai kathaṃ karissasī’’ti? ‘‘aikāhaṃ, bhantai, pāṇiṃ abhinimminissāmi – saiyyathāpi mahāpathavī| yai pathavinissitā pāṇā tai tattha saṅkāmaissāmi| aikaina hatthaina pathaviṃ parivattaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, mā  tai ruc‍ci pathaviṃ parivattaituṃ| vipal‍lāsampi sattā paṭilabhaiyyu’’nti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, sabbau bhikkhusaṅghau uttarakuruṃ piṇḍāya gacchaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, mā tai ruc‍ci sabbassa bhikkhusaṅghassa uttarakuruṃ piṇḍāya gamana’’nti|

18. atha  khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘katamaisānaṃ khau buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausi; katamaisānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’ti? atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ sāyaṇhasamayaṃ (sī॰) paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘katamaisānaṃ khau buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausi, katamaisānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’ti| ‘katamaisānaṃ nu khau, bhantai, buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausi, katamaisānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’’ti?

‘‘bhagavatau ca, sāriputta, vipassissa bhagavatau ca sikhissa bhagavatau ca vaissabhussa brahmacariyaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausi| bhagavatau ca, sāriputta, kakusandhassa bhagavatau ca kauṇāgamanassa bhagavatau ca kassapassa brahmacariyaṃ  ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’ti|

19. ‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina bhagavatau ca vipassissa bhagavatau ca sikhissa bhagavatau ca vaissabhussa brahmacariyaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’ti? ‘‘bhagavā ca, sāriputta, vipassī bhagavā ca sikhī bhagavā ca vaissabhū kilāsunau ahaisuṃ sāvakānaṃ vitthāraina dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| appakañ‍ca naisaṃ ahausi suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| apañ‍ñattaṃ sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ| anuddiṭṭhaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ| taisaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ antaradhānaina buddhānubuddhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ antaradhānaina yai tai pacchimā sāvakā nānānāmā nānāgauttā nānājac‍cā nānākulā pabbajitā tai taṃ brahmacariyaṃ khippañ‍ñaiva antaradhāpaisuṃ| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, nānāpupphāni phalakai nikkhittāni suttaina asaṅgahitāni tāni vātau vikirati vidhamati viddhaṃsaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ suttaina asaṅgahitattā| aivamaiva khau, sāriputta, taisaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ antaradhānaina buddhānubuddhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ antaradhānaina yai tai pacchimā sāvakā nānānāmā nānāgauttā nānājac‍cā nānākulā pabbajitā tai taṃ brahmacariyaṃ khippañ‍ñaiva antaradhāpaisuṃ|

‘‘akilāsunau ca tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ sāvakai caitasā caitau paric‍ca auvadituṃ| bhūtapubbaṃ,  sāriputta, vaissabhū bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ bhiṃsanakai bhīsanakai (ka॰) vanasaṇḍai sahassaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ caitasā  caitau paric‍ca auvadati anusāsati – ‘aivaṃ vitak‍kaitha, mā aivaṃ vitak‍kayittha; aivaṃ manasikarautha, mā aivaṃ manasākattha manasākarittha (ka॰); idaṃ pajahatha, idaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti| atha khau, sāriputta, tassa bhikkhusahassassa vaissabhunā bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aivaṃ auvadiyamānānaṃ aivaṃ anusāsiyamānānaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu| tatra sudaṃ, sāriputta, bhiṃsanakassa vanasaṇḍassa bhiṃsanakatasmiṃ hauti – yau kauci avītarāgau taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ pavisati, yaibhuyyaina laumāni haṃsanti| ayaṃ khau, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina bhagavatau ca vipassissa bhagavatau ca sikhissa bhagavatau ca vaissabhussa brahmacariyaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’ti|

20. ‘‘kau  pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina bhagavatau ca kakusandhassa bhagavatau ca kauṇāgamanassa bhagavatau ca kassapassa brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’ti? ‘‘bhagavā  ca, sāriputta, kakusandhau bhagavā ca kauṇāgamanau bhagavā ca kassapau akilāsunau ahaisuṃ sāvakānaṃ vitthāraina dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| bahuñ‍ca naisaṃ ahausi suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ, pañ‍ñattaṃ sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ, uddiṭṭhaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ| taisaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ antaradhānaina buddhānubuddhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ antaradhānaina yai tai pacchimā sāvakā nānānāmā nānāgauttā nānājac‍cā nānākulā pabbajitā tai taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṃ  dīghamaddhānaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, nānāpupphāni phalakai nikkhittāni suttaina susaṅgahitāni tāni vātau na vikirati na vidhamati na viddhaṃsaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ suttaina susaṅgahitattā| aivamaiva khau, sāriputta, taisaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ antaradhānaina buddhānubuddhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ antaradhānaina yai tai pacchimā sāvakā nānānāmā nānāgauttā nānājac‍cā nānākulā pabbajitā tai taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ| ayaṃ khau, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina bhagavatau ca kakusandhassa bhagavatau ca kauṇāgamanassa bhagavatau ca kassapassa brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ ahausī’’ti|

21. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau! aitassa, sugata, kālau! yaṃ bhagavā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya pañ‍ñāpaiyya (sī॰ syā॰), uddisaiyya pātimaukkhaṃ, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitika’’nti| ‘‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, sāriputta! āgamaihi tvaṃ, sāriputta! tathāgatauva tattha kālaṃ jānissati| na tāva, sāriputta, satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti uddisati na uddisati (sī॰) pātimaukkhaṃ yāva na idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti| yatau ca khau, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ  pañ‍ñapaiti uddissati pātimaukkhaṃ taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| na tāva, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti yāva na saṅghau rattañ‍ñumahattaṃ pattau hauti| yatau ca khau, sāriputta, saṅghau rattañ‍ñumahattaṃ pattau hauti atha idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā  dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha, satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti  uddisati pātimaukkhaṃ taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| na tāva, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, yāva na saṅghau vaipul‍lamahattaṃ pattau hauti| yatau ca khau, sāriputta, saṅghau vaipul‍lamahattaṃ pattau hauti, atha idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti uddisati pātimaukkhaṃ taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| na tāva, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, yāva na saṅghau lābhaggamahattaṃ pattau hauti| yatau ca khau, sāriputta, saṅghau lābhaggamahattaṃ pattau hauti, atha idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti uddisati pātimaukkhaṃ taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| na tāva, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, yāva na saṅghau bāhusac‍camahattaṃ pattau hauti| yatau ca khau, sāriputta, saṅghau bāhusac‍camahattaṃ pattau hauti, atha idhaikac‍cai āsavaṭṭhānīyā dhammā saṅghai pātubhavanti, atha satthā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti uddisati pātimaukkhaṃ taisaṃyaiva āsavaṭṭhānīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya| nirabbudau hi, sāriputta, bhikkhusaṅghau nirādīnavau apagatakāḷakau suddhau sārai patiṭṭhitau| imaisañhi, sāriputta, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ yau pacchimakau bhikkhu  sau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti|

22. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ānanda, tathāgatānaṃ yaihi nimantitā vassaṃ vasanti, na tai anapalaukaitvā janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamanti| āyāmānanda, vairañ‍jaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ apalaukaissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina yaina vairañ‍jassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau vairañ‍jau  brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau vairañ‍jaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘nimantitamha tayā, brāhmaṇa, vassaṃvuṭṭhā vassaṃvutthā (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), apalaukaima taṃ, icchāma mayaṃ janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamitu’’nti| ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhau gautama, nimantitattha mayā vassaṃvuṭṭhā; api ca, yau daiyyadhammau sau na din‍nau| tañ‍ca khau nau asantaṃ, naupi adātukamyatā, taṃ kutaittha labbhā bahukic‍cā gharāvāsā bahukaraṇīyā| adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau bhagavā vairañ‍jaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  pak‍kāmi| atha khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

23. atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina vairañ‍jassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ auṇītapattapāṇiṃ (ka॰) ticīvaraina acchādaisi, aikamaikañ‍ca bhikkhuṃ aikamaikaina dussayugaina acchādaisi| atha khau bhagavā vairañ‍jaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā anupagamma sauraiyyaṃ saṅkassaṃ kaṇṇakuj‍jaṃ yaina payāgapatiṭṭhānaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā payāgapatiṭṭhānai gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttaritvā yaina bārāṇasī tadavasari| atha khau bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyanti|

vairañ‍jabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|



1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

1. paṭhamapārājikaṃ

sudin‍nabhāṇavārau

24. taina  khau pana samayaina vaisāliyā avidūrai kalandagāmau nāma atthi kalandagāmau nāma hauti (sī॰), kalandagāmau hauti (syā॰)| tattha sudin‍nau nāma kalandaputtau saiṭṭhiputtau hauti| atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau sambahulaihi sampahūlaihi (sī॰) sahāyakaihi saddhiṃ vaisāliṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau nisin‍nau hauti| addasa khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau bhagavantaṃ mahatiyā parisāya parivutaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintaṃ nisin‍naṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhampi dhammaṃ suṇaiyya’’nti| atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau yaina sā parisā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yathā yathā khau ahaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’’nti| atha khau sā parisā bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

25. atha  khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau aciravuṭṭhitāya parisāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā yathāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| pabbājaitu maṃ bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anuñ‍ñātausi pana tvaṃ, sudinna, mātāpitūhi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, anuñ‍ñātau mātāpitūhi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| ‘‘na khau, sudin‍na, tathāgatā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ mātāpitūhi puttaṃ pabbājaintī’’ti| ‘‘sauhaṃ, bhantai, tathā karissāmi yathā maṃ mātāpitarau anujānissanti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti|

26. atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau vaisāliyaṃ taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā yaina kalandagāmau yaina mātāpitarau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mātāpitarau aitadavauca – ‘‘ammatātā, yathā yathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ  saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; icchāmahaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajituṃ| anujānātha maṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, tāta sudin‍na, amhākaṃ aikaputtakau piyau manāpau sukhaidhitau sukhaparihatau| na tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kiñ‍ci dukkhassa jānāsi| maraṇainapi mayaṃ tai akāmakā vinā bhavissāma, kiṃ pana mayaṃ taṃ jīvantaṃ anujānissāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau mātāpitarau aitadavauca – ‘‘ammatātā, yathā yathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; icchāmahaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| anujānātha maṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, tāta sudin‍na, amhākaṃ aikaputtakau piyau manāpau sukhaidhitau sukhaparihatau| na tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kiñ‍ci dukkhassa jānāsi| maraṇainapi mayaṃ tai akāmakā vinā bhavissāma, kiṃ pana mayaṃ taṃ jīvantaṃ anujānissāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti! tatiyampi khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau mātāpitarau aitadavauca – ‘‘ammatātā, yathā yathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; icchāmahaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| anujānātha maṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| tatiyampi  khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, tāta sudin‍na, amhākaṃ aikaputtakau piyau manāpau sukhaidhitau sukhaparihatau| na tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kiñ‍ci dukkhassa jānāsi| maraṇainapi mayaṃ tai akāmakā vinā bhavissāma, kiṃ pana mayaṃ taṃ jīvantaṃ anujānissāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti!

27. atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau – ‘‘na maṃ mātāpitarau anujānanti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti, tatthaiva anantarahitāya bhūmiyā nipaj‍ji – idhaiva mai maraṇaṃ bhavissati pabbaj‍jā vāti| atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau aikampi bhattaṃ na bhuñ‍ji, dvaipi bhattāni na bhuñ‍ji, tīṇipi bhattāni na bhuñ‍ji, cattāripi bhattāni na bhuñ‍ji, pañ‍capi bhattāni na bhuñ‍ji, chapi bhattāni na bhuñ‍ji, sattapi bhattāni na bhuñ‍ji|

28. atha khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, tāta sudin‍na, amhākaṃ aikaputtakau  piyau manāpau sukhaidhitau sukhaparihatau| na tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kiñ‍ci dukkhassa jānāsi| maraṇainapi mayaṃ tai akāmakā vinā bhavissāma, kiṃ pana mayaṃ taṃ jīvantaṃ anujānissāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya? uṭṭhaihi, tāta sudin‍na, bhuñ‍ja ca piva ca paricāraihi ca, bhuñ‍jantau pivantau paricāraintau kāmai paribhuñ‍jantau puñ‍ñāni karauntau abhiramassu| na taṃ mayaṃ anujānāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai sudin‍nau kalandaputtau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, tāta sudin‍na, amhākaṃ aikaputtakau piyau manāpau sukhaidhitau sukhaparihatau| na tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kiñ‍ci dukkhassa jānāsi| maraṇainapi mayaṃ tai akāmakā vinā bhavissāma, kiṃ pana mayaṃ taṃ jīvantaṃ anujānissāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya! uṭṭhaihi, tāta sudin‍na, bhuñ‍ja ca piva ca paricāraihi ca, bhuñ‍jantau pivantau paricāraintau kāmai paribhuñ‍jantau puñ‍ñāni karauntau abhiramassu| na taṃ mayaṃ anujānāma agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

atha khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa sahāyakā yaina sudin‍nau kalandaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā  sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ  – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, samma sudin‍na, mātāpitūnaṃ  aikaputtakau piyau manāpau  sukhaidhitau sukhaparihatau| na tvaṃ, samma sudin‍na, kiñ‍ci dukkhassa jānāsi| maraṇainapi tai mātāpitarau akāmakā vinā bhavissanti, kiṃ pana taṃ jīvantaṃ anujānissanti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajāya! uṭṭhaihi, samma sudin‍na, bhuñ‍ja ca piva ca paricāraihi ca, bhuñ‍jantau pivantau paricāraintau kāmai paribhuñ‍jantau puñ‍ñāni karauntau abhiramassu, na taṃ mātāpitarau anujānissanti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, sudin‍nau kalandaputtau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa sahāyakā sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, samma sudin‍na…pai॰… tatiyampi khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

29. atha khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa sahāyakā yaina sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa mātāpitarau aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ammatātā, aisau sudin‍nau anantarahitāya bhūmiyā nipan‍nau – ‘idhaiva mai maraṇaṃ bhavissati pabbaj‍jā vā’ti| sacai tumhai sudin‍naṃ nānujānissatha agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya, tatthaiva maraṇaṃ āgamissati| sacai pana tumhai sudin‍naṃ anujānissatha agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya, pabbajitampi naṃ dakkhissatha| sacai sudin‍nau nābhiramissati agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya, kā tassa añ‍ñā gati bhavissati, idhaiva pac‍cāgamissati| anujānātha sudin‍naṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| ‘‘anujānāma, tātā, sudin‍naṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| atha khau sudin‍nassa kalandaputtassa sahāyakā yaina sudin‍nau kalandaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘uṭṭhaihi, samma sudin‍na, anuñ‍ñātausi mātāpitūhi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti|

30. atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau – ‘‘anuñ‍ñātaumhi kira mātāpitūhi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti, haṭṭhau udaggau pāṇinā gattāni paripuñchantau vuṭṭhāsi| atha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau katipāhaṃ balaṃ gāhaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisan‍nau khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘anuñ‍ñātau anuñ‍ñātaumhi (sī॰ syā॰) ahaṃ, bhantai, mātāpitūhi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya| pabbājaitu maṃ bhagavā’’ti| alattha khau sudin‍nau kalandaputtau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau ca panāyasmā sudin‍nau aivarūpai dhutaguṇai samādāya vattati, ārañ‍ñikau  hauti piṇḍapātikau paṃsukūlikau sapadānacārikau, añ‍ñataraṃ vaj‍jigāmaṃ upanissāya viharati|

taina khau pana samayaina vaj‍jī dubbhikkhā hauti dvīhitikā saitaṭṭhikā salākāvuttā, na sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa aitadahausi – ‘‘aitarahi khau vaj‍jī dubbhikkhā  dvīhitikā saitaṭṭhikā salākāvuttā, na sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| bahū khau pana mai vaisāliyaṃ ñātī aḍḍhā mahaddhanā mahābhaugā pahūtajātarūparajatā pahūtavittūpakaraṇā pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ ñātī upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ! ñātī maṃ ñātakāpi maṃ (syā॰) nissāya dānāni dassanti puñ‍ñāni karissanti, bhikkhū ca lābhaṃ lacchanti, ahañ‍ca piṇḍakaina na kilamissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina vaisālī taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā sudin‍nau vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| assausuṃ khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa ñātakā – ‘‘sudin‍nau kira kalandaputtau vaisāliṃ anuppattau’’ti| tai āyasmatau sudin‍nassa saṭṭhimattai thālipākai bhattābhihāraṃ abhihariṃsu| atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau tai saṭṭhimattai thālipākai bhikkhūnaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kalandagāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kalandagāmai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina sakapitu nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami|

31. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau sudin‍nassa ñātidāsī ābhidausikaṃ kummāsaṃ chaḍḍaitukāmā chaṭṭaitukāmā (ka॰) hauti| atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau taṃ ñātidāsiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai taṃ, bhagini, chaḍḍanīyadhammaṃ, idha mai pattai ākirā’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa ñātidāsī taṃ ābhidausikaṃ  kummāsaṃ āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pattai ākirantī hatthānañ‍ca pādānañ‍ca sarassa ca nimittaṃ aggahaisi| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa ñātidāsī yaināyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yagghaiyyai, jānaiyyāsi, ayyaputtau sudin‍nau anuppattau’’ti| ‘‘sacai, jai, tvaṃ sac‍caṃ bhaṇasi, adāsiṃ taṃ karaumī’’ti|

32. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sudin‍nau taṃ ābhidausikaṃ kummāsaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ kuṭṭamūlaṃ kuḍḍamūlaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) nissāya paribhuñ‍jati| pitāpi khau āyasmatau  sudin‍nassa kammantā āgacchantau addasa āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ taṃ ābhidausikaṃ kummāsaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ kuṭṭamūlaṃ nissāya paribhuñ‍jantaṃ| disvāna yaināyasmā sudin‍nau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nāma, tāta sudin‍na, ābhidausikaṃ kummāsaṃ paribhuñ‍jissasi! nanu nāma, tāta sudin‍na, sakaṃ gaihaṃ gantabba’’nti? ‘‘agamimha agamamhā (ka॰) khau tai gahapati, gaihaṃ| tatauyaṃ ābhidausikau kummāsau’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pitā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi, tāta sudin‍na, gharaṃ gamissāmā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau yaina sakapitu nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pitā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhuñ‍ja, tāta sudin‍nā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati, kataṃ mai aj‍ja bhattakic‍ca’’nti| ‘‘adhivāsaihi, tāta sudin‍na, svātanāya bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā sudin‍nau tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

33. atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina haritaina gaumayaina pathaviṃ aupuñ‍jāpaitvā aupucchāpaitvā (sī॰ syā॰) dvai puñ‍jai kārāpaisi – aikaṃ hirañ‍ñassa, aikaṃ suvaṇṇassa| tāva mahantā puñ‍jā ahaisuṃ, auratau ṭhitau purisau pāratau ṭhitaṃ purisaṃ na passati; pāratau ṭhitau purisau auratau ṭhitaṃ purisaṃ na passati| tai puñ‍jai kilañ‍jaihi paṭicchādāpaitvā majjhai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā tiraukaraṇīyaṃ parikkhipitvā āyasmatau  sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, vadhu, yaina alaṅkāraina alaṅkatā puttassa mai sudin‍nassa piyā ahausi manāpā taina alaṅkāraina alaṅkarā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti, khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātuyā pac‍cassausi|

34. atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sakapitu nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa  pitā yaināyasmā sudin‍nau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai puñ‍jai vivarāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ tai, tāta sudin‍na, mātu mattikaṃ itthikāya itthidhanaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ paittikaṃ añ‍ñaṃ pitāmahaṃ| labbhā, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugā ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| aihi tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’’ti| ‘‘tāta, na ussahāmi na visahāmi, abhiratau ahaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carāmī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pitā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ tai, tāta sudin‍na, mātu mattikaṃ, itthikāya itthidhanaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ paittikaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ pitāmahaṃ| labbhā, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugā ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| aihi tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’’ti| ‘‘vadaiyyāma khau taṃ, gahapati, sacai tvaṃ nātikaḍḍhaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘vadaihi, tāta sudin‍nā’’ti| taina hi tvaṃ, gahapati, mahantai mahantai sāṇipasibbakai kārāpaitvā hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇassa pūrāpaitvā sakaṭaihi nibbāhāpaitvā majjhai gaṅgāya sautai aupātaihi ausādaihi (sī॰ syā॰)| taṃ kissa haitu? yañhi tai, gahapati, bhavissati tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā ārakkhau vā sau tai na bhavissatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pitā anattamanau  ahausi – ‘‘kathañhi nāma puttau sudin‍nau aivaṃ vakkhatī’’ti!

35. atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pitā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, vadhu, tvaṃ piyā ca manāpā ca tvampi yāca (sī॰)| appaiva  nāma puttau sudin‍nau tuyhampi vacanaṃ karaiyyā’’ti! atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa pādaisu gahaitvā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīdisā nāma tā, ayyaputta, accharāyau yāsaṃ tvaṃ haitu brahmacariyaṃ carasī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhagini, accharānaṃ haitu brahmacariyaṃ carāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā – ‘‘aj‍jataggai maṃ ayyaputtau sudin‍nau bhaginivādaina samudācaratī’’ti, tatthaiva mucchitā papatā|

atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau pitaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai, gahapati, bhaujanaṃ dātabbaṃ daitha, mā nau vihaiṭhayitthā’’ti| ‘‘bhuñ‍ja, tāta sudin‍nā’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā ca pitā ca āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisuṃ sampavāraisuṃ| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kulaṃ aḍḍhaṃ mahaddhanaṃ mahābhaugaṃ pahūtajātarūparajataṃ pahūtavittūpakaraṇaṃ pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñaṃ| labbhā, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugā ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| aihi tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’’ti| ‘‘amma, na ussahāmi na visahāmi, abhiratau  ahaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carāmī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kulaṃ aḍḍhaṃ mahaddhanaṃ mahābhaugaṃ pahūtajātarūparajataṃ pahūtavittūpakaraṇaṃ pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñaṃ pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñaṃ (pa॰ carāmīti) itipāṭhau sabbattha natthi, ūnau mañ‍ñai| taina hi, tāta sudin‍na, bījakampi daihi – mā nau aputtakaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ licchavayau atiharāpaisu’’nti| ‘‘aitaṃ khau mai, amma, sak‍kā  kātu’’nti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana, tāta sudin‍na, aitarahi viharasī’’ti? ‘‘mahāvanai, ammā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sudin‍nau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

36. atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, vadhu, yadā utunī hausi, pupphaṃ tai uppan‍naṃ hauti, atha mai āraucaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ ayyai’’ti khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātuyā pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā nacirassaiva  utunī ahausi, pupphaṃsā uppaj‍ji| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘utunīmhi, ayyai, pupphaṃ mai uppan‍na’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, vadhu, yaina alaṅkāraina alaṅkatā puttassa sudin‍nassa piyā ahausi manāpā taina alaṅkāraina alaṅkarā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ ayyai’’ti khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātuyā pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmatau  sudin‍nassa mātā āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikaṃ ādāya yaina mahāvanaṃ yaināyasmā sudin‍nau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kulaṃ aḍḍhaṃ mahaddhanaṃ mahābhaugaṃ pahūtajātarūparajataṃ pahūtavittūpakaraṇaṃ pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñaṃ| labbhā, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugā ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| aihi tvaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’’ti| ‘‘amma, na ussahāmi na visahāmi, abhiratau ahaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carāmī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa mātā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, tāta sudin‍na, kulaṃ aḍḍhaṃ mahaddhanaṃ mahābhaugaṃ pahūtajātarūparajataṃ pahūtavittūpakaraṇaṃ pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñaṃ| taina hi, tāta sudin‍na, bījakampi daihi – mā nau aputtakaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ licchavayau atiharāpaisu’’nti| ‘‘aitaṃ khau mai, amma, sak‍kā kātu’’nti, purāṇadutiyikāya bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā apañ‍ñattai sikkhāpadai anādīnavadassau purāṇadutiyikāya tikkhattuṃ maithunaṃ dhammaṃ abhiviñ‍ñāpaisi| sā taina gabbhaṃ gaṇhi| bhummā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘nirabbudau vata, bhau, bhikkhusaṅghau nirādīnavau; sudin‍naina kalandaputtaina abbudaṃ uppāditaṃ, ādīnavau uppāditau’’ti| bhummānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā cātumahārājikā cātummahārājikā (sī॰ syā॰) daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ…pai॰… tāvatiṃsā daivā… yāmā daivā … tusitā daivā… nimmānaratī daivā… paranimmitavasavattī  daivā… brahmakāyikā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘nirabbudau vata, bhau, bhikkhusaṅghau nirādīnavau; sudin‍naina kalandaputtaina abbudaṃ  uppāditaṃ, ādīnavau uppāditau’’ti| itiha taina khaṇaina taina muhuttaina yāva brahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchi|

atha  khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikā tassa gabbhassa paripākamanvāya puttaṃ vijāyi| atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa sahāyakā tassa dārakassa ‘bījakau’ti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| āyasmatau sudin‍nassa purāṇadutiyikāya bījakamātāti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| āyasmatau sudin‍nassa bījakapitāti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| tai aparaina samayaina ubhau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā arahattaṃ sacchākaṃsu|

37. atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, ahu vippaṭisārau – ‘‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā! dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ! yauhaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā nāsakkhiṃ yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti| sau tainaiva kuk‍kuc‍caina taina vippaṭisāraina kisau ahausi lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau antaumanau līnamanau dukkhī dummanau vippaṭisārī pajjhāyi|

38. atha khau āyasmatau sudin‍nassa sahāyakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pubbai khau tvaṃ, āvusau sudin‍na, vaṇṇavā ahausi pīṇindriyau pasan‍namukhavaṇṇau vippasan‍nachavivaṇṇau; sau dāni tvaṃ aitarahi kisau  lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau antaumanau līnamanau dukkhī dummanau vippaṭisārī pajjhāyasi| kac‍ci nau tvaṃ, āvusau sudin‍na, anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carasī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carāmi| atthi mai pāpakammaṃ kataṃ; purāṇadutiyikāya maithunau dhammau paṭisaivitau; tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahu vippaṭisārau – ‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yauhaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā nāsakkhiṃ yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti| ‘‘alañhi tai, āvusau sudin‍na, kuk‍kuc‍cāya alaṃ vippaṭisārāya yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā na sakkhissasi yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| nanu, āvusau, bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau, nau sarāgāya; visaṃyaugāya dhammau daisitau, nau saṃyaugāya; anupādānāya dhammau daisitau, nau saupādānāya| tattha  nāma tvaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā  virāgāya dhammai daisitai sarāgāya caitaissasi, visaṃyaugāya dhammai daisitai  saṃyaugāya caitaissasi, anupādānāya dhammai daisitai saupādānāya caitaissasi! nanu, āvusau, bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina rāgavirāgāya dhammau daisitau, madanimmadanāya pipāsavinayāya ālayasamugghātāya vaṭṭupacchaidāya taṇhākkhayāya  virāgāya niraudhāya nibbānāya dhammau daisitau! nanu, āvusau, bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina kāmānaṃ pahānaṃ akkhātaṃ, kāmasañ‍ñānaṃ pariñ‍ñā akkhātā, kāmapipāsānaṃ paṭivinayau akkhātau, kāmavitak‍kānaṃ samugghātau akkhātau, kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau! naitaṃ, āvusau, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya| atha khvaitaṃ, āvusau, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti|

39. atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, sudin‍na, purāṇadutiyikāya maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ ananucchaviyaṃ (sī॰), maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appaṭirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā na sakkhissasi yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau, nau sarāgāya; visaṃyaugāya dhammau daisitau, nau saṃyaugāya; anupādānāya dhammau daisitau, nau saupādānāya! tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā virāgāya dhammai daisitai sarāgāya caitaissasi, visaṃyaugāya dhammai daisitai saṃyaugāya caitaissasi, anupādānāya dhammai daisitai saupādānāya caitaissasi! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina rāgavirāgāya dhammau daisitau! madanimmadanāya pipāsavinayāya ālayasamugghātāya vaṭṭupacchaidāya taṇhākkhayāya virāgāya niraudhāya nibbānāya dhammau daisitau! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina kāmānaṃ  pahānaṃ akkhātaṃ, kāmasañ‍ñānaṃ pariñ‍ñā akkhātā, kāmapipāsānaṃ paṭivinayau akkhātau, kāmavitak‍kānaṃ samugghātau akkhātau, kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau! varaṃ tai, maughapurisa, āsivisassa āsīvisassa (sī॰ syā॰) ghauravisassa mukhai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ, na tvaiva mātugāmassa aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ| varaṃ tai, maughapurisa, kaṇhasappassa mukhai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ, na tvaiva mātugāmassa aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ| varaṃ tai, maughapurisa, aṅgārakāsuyā ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya aṅgajātaṃ  pakkhittaṃ, na tvaiva mātugāmassa aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi, maughapurisa, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| itaunidānañ‍ca khau, maughapurisa, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ  vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, yaṃ tvaṃ asaddhammaṃ gāmadhammaṃ  vasaladhammaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ audakantikaṃ rahassaṃ dvayaṃdvayasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jissasi, bahūnaṃ khau tvaṃ, maughapurisa, akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādikattā pubbaṅgamau| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya; atha khvaitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya, pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sudin‍naṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya duppausatāya mahicchatāya asantuṭṭhitāya asantuṭṭhatāya (syā॰) saṅgaṇikāya kausaj‍jassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā anaikapariyāyaina subharatāya supausatāya appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vīriyārabbhassa (ka॰) vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā bhikkhūnaṃ tadanucchavikaṃ tadanulaumikaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi pañ‍ñāpaissāmi (sī॰ syā॰) dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaiyya, pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

sudin‍nabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

mak‍kaṭīvatthu

40. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ mahāvanai mak‍kaṭiṃ āmisaina upalāpaitvā tassā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati| atha khau sau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaraṃ  ādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā yaina tassa bhikkhunau vihārau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasa khau sā mak‍kaṭī tai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ puratau kaṭimpi cālaisi chaippampi cālaisi, kaṭimpi  auḍḍi, nimittampi akāsi| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhikkhu imissā mak‍kaṭiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivatī’’ti| aikamantaṃ nilīyiṃsu| atha khau sau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā piṇḍapātaṃ ādāya paṭik‍kami|

41. atha khau sā mak‍kaṭī yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sau bhikkhu taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ aikadaisaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā aikadaisaṃ tassā mak‍kaṭiyā adāsi| atha khau sā mak‍kaṭī taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tassa bhikkhunau kaṭiṃ auḍḍi| atha khau sau bhikkhu tassā mak‍kaṭiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati| atha khau tai bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘nanu, āvusau, bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ; kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, mak‍kaṭiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ; tañ‍ca khau  manussitthiyā, nau tiracchānagatāyā’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, tathaiva taṃ hauti| ananucchavikaṃ, āvusau, ananulaumikaṃ appaṭirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā na sakkhissasi yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ! nanu, āvusau, bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau, nau sarāgāya…pai॰… kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau! naitaṃ, āvusau, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya| atha khvaitaṃ, āvusau, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya, pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

42. atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mak‍kaṭiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā na sakkhissasi yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau, nau sarāgāya  …pai॰… kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau! varaṃ tai, maughapurisa, āsīvisassa ghauravisassa mukhai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ, na tvaiva mak‍kaṭiyā aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ| varaṃ tai, maughapurisa, kaṇhasappassa mukhai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ, na tvaiva mak‍kaṭiyā aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ| varaṃ  tai, maughapurisa, aṅgārakāsuyā ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ, na tvaiva mak‍kaṭiyā aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pakkhittaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi, maughapurisa, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ; na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| itaunidānañ‍ca khau, maughapurisa, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, yaṃ tvaṃ asaddhammaṃ gāmadhammaṃ vasaladhammaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ audakantikaṃ rahassaṃ dvayaṃdvayasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaiyya antamasau tiracchānagatāyapi, pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

mak‍kaṭīvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

santhatabhāṇavārau

43. taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, yāvadatthaṃ supiṃsu, yāvadatthaṃ nhāyiṃsu| yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā yāvadatthaṃ supitvā yāvadatthaṃ nhāyitvā ayaunisau manasi karitvā sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaiviṃsu| tai aparaina samayaina ñātibyasanainapi phuṭṭhā bhaugabyasanainapi phuṭṭhā raugabyasanainapi phuṭṭhā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘na mayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, buddhagarahinau na dhammagarahinau na saṅghagarahinau; attagarahinau mayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, anañ‍ñagarahinau| mayamaivamhā alakkhikā mayaṃ appapuñ‍ñā, yai mayaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitvā nāsakkhimhā yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| idāni caipi  idānipi cai (syā॰) mayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, labhaiyyāma bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ labhaiyyāma upasampadaṃ, idānipi mayaṃ vipassakā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharaiyyāma| sādhu, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau vaisālikānaṃ vaj‍jiputtakānaṃ paṭissuṇitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi|

‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, ānanda, anavakāsau yaṃ tathāgatau vaj‍jīnaṃ vā vaj‍jiputtakānaṃ vā kāraṇā sāvakānaṃ pārājikaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ samūhanaiyyā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai yau pana bhikkhavai bhikkhu (sī॰) yau khau bhikkhavai bhikkhu (syā॰), sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati sau āgatau na upasampādaitabbau; yau ca khau, bhikkhavai yau ca khau bhikkhavai bhikkhu (sī॰ syā॰), sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ āvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati sau āgatau upasampādaitabbau| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

44. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaiyya antamasau tiracchānagatāyapi, pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau’’ti|

45. yau panāti yau yādisau yathāyuttau yathājac‍cau yathānāmau yathāgauttau  yathāsīlau yathāvihārī yathāgaucarau thairau vā navau vā majjhimau vā| aisau vuc‍cati ‘yau panā’ti|

vibha॰ 510, jhānavibhaṅgaipi bhikkhūti bhikkhakauti bhikkhu, bhikkhācariyaṃ ajjhupagatauti bhikkhu, bhin‍napaṭadharauti bhikkhu, samañ‍ñāya bhikkhu, paṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhu, aihi bhikkhūti bhikkhu, tīhi saraṇagamanaihi upasampan‍nauti bhikkhu, bhadrau bhikkhu, sārau bhikkhu, saikhau bhikkhu, asaikhau bhikkhu, samaggaina saṅghaina ñatticatutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nauti bhikkhu| tatra yvāyaṃ bhikkhu samaggaina saṅghaina ñatticatutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nau, ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

dī॰ ni॰ 3.305 sikkhāti  tissau sikkhā – adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| tatra yāyaṃ adhisīlasikkhā, ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitā sikkhāti|

sājīvaṃ nāma yaṃ bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ, aitaṃ sājīvaṃ nāma| tasmiṃ sikkhati, taina vuc‍cati sājīvasamāpan‍nauti|

sikkhaṃ  apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvāti atthi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca pac‍cakkhātā|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā| idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau upāsakabhāvaṃ patthayamānau ārāmikabhāvaṃ patthayamānau sāmaṇairabhāvaṃ patthayamānau titthiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau titthiyasāvakabhāvaṃ patthayamānau assamaṇabhāvaṃ patthayamānau asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ sikkhaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ  vinayaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ uddaisaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ  upajjhāyaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ ācariyaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ saddhivihārikaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ antaivāsikaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ samānupajjhāyakaṃ… yaṃnūnāhaṃ samānācariyakaṃ yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ gihī assa’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ upāsakau assa’nti… ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ ārāmikau assa’nti… ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sāmaṇairau assa’nti… ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ titthiyau assa’nti… ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ titthiyasāvakau assa’nti… ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ assamaṇau assa’nti… ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ asakyaputtiyau assa’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

46. ‘‘atha  vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘yadi panāhaṃ buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati  viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘yadi panāhaṃ asakyaputtiyau assa’nti  vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘apāhaṃ buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘apāhaṃ asakyaputtiyau assa’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘handāhaṃ buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘handāhaṃ asakyaputtiyau assa’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘hauti mai buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhaiyya’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘hauti mai asakyaputtiyau assa’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

47. ‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau ‘mātaraṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pitaraṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘bhātaraṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘bhaginiṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘puttaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘dhītaraṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pajāpatiṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘ñātakai sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘mittai sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘gāmaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘nigamaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘khaittaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘vatthuṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘hirañ‍ñaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘suvaṇṇaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘sippaṃ sarāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pubbai hasitaṃ lapitaṃ kīḷitaṃ samanussarāmī’ti  vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva  hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

48. ‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘mātā mai atthi, sā mayā pausaitabbā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pitā mai atthi, sau mayā pausaitabbau’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘bhātā mai atthi, sau mayā pausaitabbau’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘bhaginī mai atthi, sā mayā pausaitabbā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘puttau mai atthi, sau mayā pausaitabbau’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘dhītā mai atthi, sā mayā pausaitabbā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pajāpati mai atthi, sā mayā pausaitabbā’ti  vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti … ‘ñātakā mai atthi, tai mayā pausaitabbā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘mittā mai atthi, tai mayā pausaitabbā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

49. ‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘mātā mai atthi, sā maṃ pausaissatī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pitā mai atthi, sau maṃ pausaissatī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘bhātā mai atthi, sau maṃ pausaissatī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘bhaginī mai atthi, sā maṃ pausaissatī’ti  vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘puttau mai atthi, sau maṃ pausaissatī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘dhītā mai atthi, sā maṃ pausaissatī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pajāpati mai atthi, sā maṃ pausaissatī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘ñātakā mai atthi, tai maṃ pausaissantī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘mittā mai atthi, tai maṃ pausaissantī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘gāmau mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘nigamau mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘khaittaṃ mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘vatthu mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘hirañ‍ñaṃ mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘suvaṇṇaṃ mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘sippaṃ mai atthi, taināhaṃ jīvissāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

50. ‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau ‘duk‍kara’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘na sukara’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘duc‍cara’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘na sucara’nti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘na ussahāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘na visahāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘na ramāmī’ti vadati  viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘nābhiramāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca apac‍cakkhātā|

51. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca pac‍cakkhātā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca pac‍cakkhātā|

‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘dhammaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘saṅghaṃ  pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘vinayaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘pātimaukkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘uddaisaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘upajjhāyaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘ācariyaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘saddhivihārikaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘antaivāsikaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘samānupajjhāyakaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘samānācariyakaṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘sabrahmacāriṃ pac‍cakkhāmī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘gihīti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘upāsakauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti  vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘ārāmikauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘sāmaṇairauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘titthiyauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘titthiyasāvakauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘assamaṇauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘asakyaputtiyauti maṃ dhāraihī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca pac‍cakkhātā|

52. ‘‘atha vā pana uk‍kaṇṭhitau anabhiratau sāmañ‍ñā cavitukāmau bhikkhubhāvaṃ aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau gihibhāvaṃ patthayamānau…pai॰… asakyaputtiyabhāvaṃ patthayamānau – ‘alaṃ mai buddhainā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘alaṃ mai sabrahmacārīhī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi…pai॰… atha vā pana…pai॰… ‘kiṃ nu mai buddhainā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘kiṃ nu mai sabrahmacārīhī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘na mamatthau buddhainā’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘na mamatthau sabrahmacārīhī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti… ‘sumuttāhaṃ buddhainā’ti  vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… ‘sumuttāhaṃ sabrahmacārīhī’ti vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca pac‍cakkhātā|

53. ‘‘yāni  vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi buddhavaivacanāni vā dhammavaivacanāni vā saṅghavaivacanāni vā sikkhāvaivacanāni vā vinayavaivacanāni vā pātimaukkhavaivacanāni vā uddaisavaivacanāni vā upajjhāyavaivacanāni vā  ācariyavaivacanāni vā saddhivihārikavaivacanāni vā antaivāsikavaivacanāni vā samānupajjhāyakavaivacanāni vā samānācariyakavaivacanāni vā sabrahmacārivaivacanāni vā gihivaivacanāni vā upāsakavaivacanāni vā ārāmikavaivacanāni vā sāmaṇairavaivacanāni vā titthiyavaivacanāni vā titthiyasāvakavaivacanāni vā assamaṇavaivacanāni vā asakyaputtiyavaivacanāni vā, taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi vadati viñ‍ñāpaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dubbalyāvikammañ‍caiva hauti sikkhā ca pac‍cakkhātā|

54. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi sikkhā pac‍cakkhātā hauti taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi ummattakau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| ummattakassa santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| khittacittau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| khittacittassa santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| vaidanāṭṭau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| vaidanāṭṭassa santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| daivatāya santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| tiracchānagatassa santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā  hauti sikkhā| ariyakaina milakkhassa milakkhakassa (sī॰ syā॰) milakkhussa (ka॰) santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, sau ca na paṭivijānāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| milakkhakaina ariyakassa santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, sau ca na paṭivijānāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| ariyakaina ariyassa santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, sau ca na paṭivijānāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| milakkhakaina milakkhassa  santikai sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, sau ca na paṭivijānāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| davāya sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| ravāya sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| asāvaitukāmau sāvaiti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| sāvaitukāmau na sāvaiti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| aviñ‍ñussa sāvaiti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| viñ‍ñussa na sāvaiti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| sabbasau  vā pana na sāvaiti, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, apac‍cakkhātā hauti sikkhā’’|

55. mahāni॰ 49, 50, 51 maithunadhammau nāma yau sau asaddhammau gāmadhammau vasaladhammau duṭṭhul‍laṃ audakantikaṃ rahassaṃ dvayaṃdvayasamāpatti, aisau maithunadhammau nāma|

paṭisaivati nāma yau nimittaina nimittaṃ aṅgajātaina aṅgajātaṃ antamasau tilaphalamattampi pavaisaiti, aisau paṭisaivati nāma|

antamasau  tiracchānagatāyapīti tiracchānagatitthiyāpi maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau, pagaiva manussitthiyā| taina vuc‍cati – ‘antamasau tiracchānagatāyapī’ti|

pārājikau hautīti saiyyathāpi nāma purisau sīsacchin‍nau abhabbau taina sarīrabandhanaina jīvituṃ, aivamaiva bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘pārājikau hautī’ti|

asaṃvāsauti saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau samasikkhatā – aisau saṃvāsau nāma| sau taina saddhiṃ natthi| taina vuc‍cati – ‘asaṃvāsau’ti|

56. tissau itthiyau – manussitthī, amanussitthī, tiracchānagatitthī| tayau ubhataubyañ‍janakā – manussubhataubyañ‍janakau, amanussubhataubyañ‍janakau, tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakau| tayau paṇḍakā – manussapaṇḍakau, amanussapaṇḍakau, tiracchānagatapaṇḍakau| tayau purisā – manussapurisau, amanussapurisau, tiracchānagatapurisau|

manussitthiyā tayau maggai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpatti pārājikassa – vac‍camaggai, passāvamaggai, mukhai| amanussitthiyā…pai॰… tiracchānagatitthiyā tayau maggai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpatti pārājikassa – vac‍camaggai, passāvamaggai, mukhai| manussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… amanussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakassa tayau  maggai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpatti pārājikassa – vac‍camaggai, passāvamaggai, mukhai| manussapaṇḍakassa dvai maggai  maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpatti pārājikassa – vac‍camaggai, mukhai| amanussapaṇḍakassa… tiracchānagatapaṇḍakassa… manussapurisassa… amanussapurisassa… tiracchānagatapurisassa dvai maggai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpatti pārājikassa – vac‍camaggai, mukhai|

57. bhikkhussa  saivanacittaṃ upaṭṭhitai manussitthiyā vac‍camaggaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhussa saivanacittaṃ upaṭṭhitai manussitthiyā passāvamaggaṃ… mukhaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhussa saivanacittaṃ upaṭṭhitai amanussitthiyā… tiracchānagatitthiyā… manussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… amanussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakassa… vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ mukhaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhussa saivanacittaṃ upaṭṭhitai manussapaṇḍakassa vac‍camaggaṃ mukhaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhussa saivanacittaṃ upaṭṭhitai amanussapaṇḍakassa… tiracchānagatapaṇḍakassa… manussapurisassa… amanussapurisassa… tiracchānagatapurisassa vac‍camaggaṃ mukhaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa āpatti pārājikassa|

58. bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina  aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati pavisanaṃ sādayati (ka॰), paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ na sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa santikai  ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ na sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ na sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ na sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ na sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ na sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati āpatti pārājikassa| bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ na sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ na sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ na sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

59. bhikkhupac‍catthikā  manussitthiṃ jāgarantiṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ… mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai  ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ  sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā amanussitthiṃ… tiracchānagatitthiṃ… manussubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ… amanussubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ… tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ jāgarantaṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ… mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussapaṇḍakaṃ… amanussapaṇḍakaṃ… tiracchānagatapaṇḍakaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā tiracchānagatapaṇḍakaṃ jāgarantaṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ … mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina  khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

60. bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussapurisaṃ… amanussapurisaṃ… tiracchānagatapurisaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā tiracchānagatapurisaṃ jāgarantaṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ… mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

61. bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussitthiṃ bhikkhussa  santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatāya asanthatassa, asanthatāya santhatassa, santhatāya santhatassa, asanthatāya asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā  manussitthiṃ jāgarantiṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ… mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti, santhatāya asanthatassa, asanthatāya santhatassa, santhatāya santhatassa, asanthatāya asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā amanussitthiṃ… tiracchānagatitthiṃ… manussubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ… amanussubhataubyañjanakaṃ  … tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatassa asanthatassa, asanthatassa santhatassa, santhatassa santhatassa, asanthatassa asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ jāgarantaṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ… mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… passāvamaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti, santhatassa asanthatassa, asanthatassa santhatassa, santhatassa santhatassa, asanthatassa asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

62. bhikkhupac‍catthikā manussapaṇḍakaṃ… amanussapaṇḍakaṃ… tiracchānagatapaṇḍakaṃ… manussapurisaṃ… amanussapurisaṃ… tiracchānagatapurisaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatassa asanthatassa, asanthatassa santhatassa, santhatassa santhatassa, asanthatassa asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā  tiracchānagatapurisaṃ jāgarantaṃ… suttaṃ… mattaṃ… ummattaṃ… pamattaṃ… mataṃ akkhāyitaṃ… mataṃ yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| mataṃ yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ bhikkhussa santikai ānaitvā vac‍camaggaina… mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ abhinisīdainti, santhatassa asanthatassa, asanthatassa santhatassa, santhatassa santhatassa, asanthatassa asanthatassa| sau ca pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

63. bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhuṃ manussitthiyā santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina vac‍camaggaṃ… passāvamaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhuṃ manussitthiyā jāgarantiyā… suttāya… mattāya… ummattāya… pamattāya … matāya akkhāyitāya… matāya yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitāya…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| matāya yaibhuyyaina khāyitāya santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina vac‍camaggaṃ… passāvamaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhuṃ amanussitthiyā… tiracchānagatitthiyā… manussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… amanussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakassa… manussapaṇḍakassa… amanussapaṇḍakassa… tiracchānagatapaṇḍakassa… manussapurisassa… amanussapurisassa… tiracchānagatapurisassa santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina vac‍camaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhuṃ tiracchānagatapurisassa jāgarantassa… suttassa… mattassa… ummattassa… pamattassa… matassa akkhāyitassa… matassa yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitassa…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| matassa yaibhuyyaina khāyitassa santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina vac‍camaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti| sau  cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

64. bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhuṃ manussitthiyā santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina  vac‍camaggaṃ… passāvamaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatassa asanthatāya, asanthatassa santhatāya, santhatassa santhatāya, asanthatassa asanthatāya| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā  bhikkhuṃ manussitthiyā jāgarantiyā… suttāya… mattāya… ummattāya… pamattāya… matāya akkhāyitāya… matāya yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitāya…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| matāya yaibhuyyaina khāyitāya santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina  vac‍camaggaṃ… passāvamaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatassa asanthatāya, asanthatassa santhatāya, santhatassa santhatāya, asanthatassa asanthatāya| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhuṃ amanussitthiyā… tiracchānagatitthiyā… manussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… amanussubhataubyañ‍janakassa… tiracchānagatubhataubyañ‍janakassa… manussapaṇḍakassa… amanussapaṇḍakassa… tiracchānagatapaṇḍakassa… manussapurisassa… amanussapurisassa… tiracchānagatapurisassa santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina vac‍camaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatassa asanthatassa, asanthatassa santhatassa, santhatassa santhatassa, asanthatassa asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|
