65. bhikkhupac‍catthikā  bhikkhuṃ tiracchānagatapurisassa jāgarantassa… suttassa… mattassa… ummattassa… pamattassa… matassa akkhāyitassa… matassa yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitassa…pai॰… āpatti pārājikassa| matassa yaibhuyyaina khāyitassa santikai ānaitvā aṅgajātaina vac‍camaggaṃ… mukhaṃ abhinisīdainti santhatassa asanthatassa, asanthatassa santhatassa, santhatassa  santhatassa, asanthatassa asanthatassa| sau cai pavaisanaṃ sādiyati, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyati, ṭhitaṃ sādiyati, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… na sādiyati, anāpatti|

yathā bhikkhupac‍catthikā vitthāritā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbā|

rājapac‍catthikā… caurapac‍catthikā… dhuttapac‍catthikā… uppaḷagandhapac‍catthikā| saṃkhittaṃ|

66. maggaina maggaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| maggaina amaggaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| amaggaina maggaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti  pārājikassa| amaggaina amaggaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

bhikkhu suttabhikkhumhi vippaṭipaj‍jati; paṭibuddhau sādiyati, ubhau nāsaitabbā| paṭibuddhau na sādiyati, dūsakau nāsaitabbau| bhikkhu suttasāmaṇairamhi vippaṭipaj‍jati; paṭibuddhau sādiyati, ubhau nāsaitabbā| paṭibuddhau na sādiyati, dūsakau nāsaitabbau| sāmaṇairau suttabhikkhumhi vippaṭipaj‍jati; paṭibuddhau sādiyati, ubhau nāsaitabbā| paṭibuddhau na sādiyati, dūsakau nāsaitabbau| sāmaṇairau suttasāmaṇairamhi vippaṭipaj‍jati; paṭibuddhau sādiyati, ubhau nāsaitabbā| paṭibuddhau na sādiyati, dūsakau nāsaitabbau|

anāpatti ajānantassa, asādiyantassa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

santhatabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

mak‍kaṭī vaj‍jiputtā ca, gihī naggau ca titthiyā|

dārikuppalavaṇṇā ca, byañ‍janaihiparai duvai||

mātā  dhītā bhaginī ca, jāyā ca mudu lambinā|

dvai  vaṇā laipacittañ‍ca, dārudhītalikāya ca||

sundaraina saha pañ‍ca, pañ‍ca sivathikaṭṭhikā|

nāgī yakkhī ca paitī ca, paṇḍakaupahatau chupai||

bhaddiyai arahaṃ suttau, sāvatthiyā caturau parai|

vaisāliyā tayau mālā, supinai bhārukacchakau||

supabbā  saddhā bhikkhunī, sikkhamānā sāmaṇairī ca|

vaisiyā paṇḍakau gihī, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vuḍḍhapabbajitau migauti||

vinītavatthu

67. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu mak‍kaṭiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū sikkhaṃ apac‍cakkhāya dubbalyaṃ anāvikatvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaiviṃsu| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau mayaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘aivaṃ mai anāpatti bhavissatī’ti, gihiliṅgaina maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘aivaṃ mai anāpatti bhavissatī’ti, naggau hutvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘aivaṃ mai anāpatti bhavissatī’ti, kusacīraṃ nivāsaitvā… vākacīraṃ nivāsaitvā… phalakacīraṃ nivāsaitvā… kaisakambalaṃ nivāsaitvā… vālakambalaṃ nivāsaitvā… ulūkapakkhikaṃ nivāsaitvā… ajinakkhipaṃ nivāsaitvā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu pīṭhakai nipan‍naṃ dārikaṃ passitvā sārattau aṅguṭṭhaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaisi| sā kālamakāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti saṅghādisaisassā’’ti|

68. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau māṇavakau uppalavaṇṇāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibaddhacittau hauti| atha khau sau māṇavakau uppalavaṇṇāya bhikkhuniyā gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhāya kuṭikaṃ pavisitvā nilīnau acchi| uppalavaṇṇā bhikkhunī pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā pādai pakkhālaitvā kuṭikaṃ pavisitvā mañ‍cakai nisīdi| atha khau sau māṇavakau uppalavaṇṇaṃ bhikkhuniṃ uggahaitvā dūsaisi| uppalavaṇṇā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, asādiyantiyā’’ti|

69. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau itthiliṅgaṃ pātubhūtaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, taṃyaiva upajjhaṃ tamaiva upasampadaṃ tāniyaiva tāni (sī॰ syā॰) vassāni bhikkhunīhi saṅgamituṃ saṅkamituṃ (sī॰ syā॰)| yā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi sādhāraṇā tā āpattiyau bhikkhunīnaṃ santikai vuṭṭhātuṃ| yā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi asādhāraṇā tāhi āpattīhi anāpattī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarissā bhikkhuniyā purisaliṅgaṃ pātubhūtaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, taṃyaiva upajjhaṃ tamaiva upasampadaṃ tāniyaiva tāni (sī॰ syā॰) vassāni bhikkhūhi saṅgamituṃ saṅkamituṃ (sī॰ syā॰)| yā āpattiyau  bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūhi sādhāraṇā tā āpattiyau bhikkhūnaṃ santikai vuṭṭhātuṃ| yā āpattiyau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇā  tāhi āpattīhi anāpattī’’ti|

70. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘aivaṃ mai anāpatti bhavissatī’ti, mātuyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi… dhītuyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi… bhaginiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi… tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu purāṇadutiyikāya maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

71. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu mudupiṭṭhikau hauti| sau anabhiratiyā pīḷitau attanau aṅgajātaṃ mukhaina aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau parājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu lambī hauti| sau anabhiratiyā pīḷitau attanau aṅgajātaṃ attanau vac‍camaggaṃ pavaisaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu matasarīraṃ passi| tasmiñ‍ca sarīrai aṅgajātasāmantā vaṇau hauti| sau – ‘aivaṃ mai anāpatti bhavissatī’ti, aṅgajātai aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaitvā vaṇaina nīhari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu matasarīraṃ passi| tasmiñ‍ca sarīrai aṅgajātasāmantā vaṇau hauti| sau – ‘aivaṃ  mai anāpatti bhavissatī’ti, vaṇai aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaitvā aṅgajātaina nīhari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau laipacittassa nimittaṃ aṅgajātaina chupi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau dārudhītalikāya nimittaṃ aṅgajātaina chupi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

72. taina  khau pana samayaina sundarau nāma bhikkhu rājagahā pabbajitau rathikāya rathiyāya (ka॰) gacchati| añ‍ñatarā itthī – ‘muhuttaṃ itthī taṃ passitvā aitadavauca muhuttaṃ (syā॰), bhantai, āgamaihi, vandissāmī’ti sā vandantī antaravāsakaṃ ukkhipitvā mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘sādiyi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, sādiyi’’nti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, asādiyantassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagini, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, ahaṃ vāyamissāmi, tvaṃ mā vāyami, aivaṃ tai anāpatti bhavissatī’’ti| sau bhikkhu tathā akāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagini, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| ‘‘aihi bhantai, tvaṃ vāyama, ahaṃ na vāyamissāmi, aivaṃ tai anāpatti bhavissatī’’ti| sau bhikkhu tathā akāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagini, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, abbhantaraṃ ghaṭṭaitvā bahi maucaihi…pai॰… bahi ghaṭṭaitvā abbhantaraṃ maucaihi, aivaṃ tai anāpatti bhavissatī’’ti| sau bhikkhu tathā akāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

73. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sivathikaṃ gantvā akkhāyitaṃ sarīraṃ passitvā tasmiṃ maithunaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sivathikaṃ gantvā yaibhuyyaina akkhāyitaṃ sarīraṃ  passitvā tasmiṃ maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sivathikaṃ gantvā yaibhuyyaina khāyitaṃ  sarīraṃ passitvā tasmiṃ maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sivathikaṃ gantvā chin‍nasīsaṃ passitvā vaṭṭakatai mukhai chupantaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sivathikaṃ gantvā chin‍nasīsaṃ passitvā vaṭṭakatai mukhai acchupantaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarissā itthiyā paṭibaddhacittau hauti| sā kālaṅkatā kālakatā (sī॰ syā॰) susānai chaḍḍitā| aṭṭhikāni vippakiṇṇāni haunti| atha khau sau bhikkhu sivathikaṃ gantvā aṭṭhikāni saṅkaḍḍhitvā nimittai aṅgajātaṃ paṭipādaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu nāgiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi… yakkhiniyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi… paitiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi … paṇḍakassa maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu upahatindriyau hauti| sau – ‘nāhaṃ vaidiyāmi vaidayāmi (ka॰) sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā, anāpatti mai bhavissatī’ti, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘vaidayi vā sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau na vā vaidayi, āpatti pārājikassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘itthiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivissāmī’ti, chupitamattai vippaṭisārī ahausi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti saṅghādisaisassā’’ti|

74. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhaddiyai jātiyāvanai divāvihāragatau nipan‍nau hauti| tassa aṅgamaṅgāni vātūpatthaddhāni haunti| añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīditvā yāvadatthaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| bhikkhū kilin‍naṃ passitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, ākāraihi aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti – rāgaina, vac‍caina, passāvaina, vātaina, uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakadaṭṭhaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahākāraihi aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau yaṃ tassa bhikkhunau rāgaina aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ assa| arahaṃ sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, tassa bhikkhunau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāvatthiyā andhavanai divāvihāragatau nipan‍nau hauti| añ‍ñatarā gaupālikā passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīdi| sau bhikkhu pavaisanaṃ sādiyi, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyi, ṭhitaṃ sādiyi, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāvatthiyā andhavanai divāvihāragatau nipan‍nau hauti| añ‍ñatarā ajapālikā passitvā… añ‍ñatarā kaṭṭhahārikā passitvā… añ‍ñatarā gaumayahārikā passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīdi| sau bhikkhu pavaisanaṃ sādiyi, paviṭṭhaṃ sādiyi, ṭhitaṃ sādiyi, uddharaṇaṃ sādiyi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

75. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ mahāvanai divāvihāragatau nipan‍nau hauti| añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīditvā yāvadatthaṃ katvā sāmantā hasamānā ṭhitā hauti| sau bhikkhu paṭibujjhitvā taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tuyhidaṃ kamma’’nti? ‘‘āma, mayhaṃ kamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘sādiyi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, jānāmī’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, ajānantassā’’ti|

76. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ mahāvanai divāvihāragatau rukkhaṃ apassāya nipan‍nau hauti| añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīdi| sau bhikkhu sahasā vuṭṭhāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘sādiyi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, sādiyi’’nti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, asādiyantassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ mahāvanai divāvihāragatau rukkhaṃ apassāya nipan‍nau hauti| añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīdi| sau bhikkhu ak‍kamitvā pavattaisi pavaṭṭaisi (sī॰ syā॰)| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘sādiyi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, sādiyi’’nti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, asādiyantassā’’ti|

77. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ divāvihāragatau dvāraṃ vivaritvā nipan‍nau hauti| tassa aṅgamaṅgāni vātūpatthaddhāni haunti| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā itthiyau gandhañ‍ca mālañ‍ca  ādāya ārāmaṃ āgamaṃsu vihārapaikkhikāyau| atha khau tā itthiyau taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aṅgajātai abhinisīditvā yāvadatthaṃ katvā, purisūsabhau vatāyanti vatvā gandhañ‍ca mālañ‍ca āraupaitvā pak‍kamiṃsu| bhikkhū kilin‍naṃ passitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, ākāraihi aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti – rāgaina, vac‍caina, passāvaina, vātaina, uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakadaṭṭhaina| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahākāraihi aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau, yaṃ tassa bhikkhunau rāgaina aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ assa| arahaṃ sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, tassa bhikkhunau| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, divā paṭisal‍līyantaina dvāraṃ saṃvaritvā paṭisal‍līyitu’’nti|

78. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhārukacchakau bhikkhu supinantai supinantaina (sī॰ syā॰) purāṇadutiyikāya maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitvā – ‘assamaṇau ahaṃ, vibbhamissāmī’ti, bhārukacchaṃ gacchantau antarāmaggai āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ passitvā aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| āyasmā upāli aivamāha – ‘‘anāpatti, āvusau, supinantainā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai supabbā nāma upāsikā mudhappasan‍nā muddhappasan‍nā (sī॰) hauti| sā aivaṃdiṭṭhikā hauti – ‘‘yā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ daiti sā aggadānaṃ daitī’’ti| sā bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca –  ‘‘aihi, bhantai, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagini, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, ūruntarikāya ūrantarikāya (sī॰) ghaṭṭaihi, aivaṃ tai anāpatti bhavissatī’’ti…pai॰…  aihi, bhantai, nābhiyaṃ ghaṭṭaihi… aihi, bhantai, udaravaṭṭiyaṃ ghaṭṭaihi… aihi, bhantai, upakacchakai ghaṭṭaihi… aihi, bhantai, gīvāyaṃ ghaṭṭaihi… aihi, bhantai, kaṇṇacchiddai ghaṭṭaihi… aihi, bhantai, kaisavaṭṭiyaṃ ghaṭṭaihi… aihi, bhantai, aṅgulantarikāya ghaṭṭaihi… ‘‘aihi, bhantai, hatthaina upak‍kamitvā maucaissāmi, aivaṃ tai anāpatti bhavissatī’’ti| sau bhikkhu tathā akāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti saṅghādisaisassā’’ti|

79. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ saddhā nāma upāsikā mudhappasan‍nā hauti| sā aivaṃdiṭṭhikā hauti – ‘‘yā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ daiti sā aggadānaṃ daitī’’ti| sā bhikkhuṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagini, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, ūruntarikāya ghaṭṭaihi…pai॰…  aihi, bhantai, hatthaina upak‍kamitvā maucaissāmi, aivaṃ tai anāpatti bhavissatī’’ti| sau bhikkhu tathā akāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti saṅghādisaisassā’’ti|

80. taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ licchavikumārakā bhikkhuṃ gahaitvā bhikkhuniyā vippaṭipādaisuṃ… sikkhamānāya vippaṭipādaisuṃ… sāmaṇairiyā vippaṭipādaisuṃ| ubhau  sādiyiṃsu| ubhau nāsaitabbā| ubhau na sādiyiṃsu| ubhin‍naṃ anāpatti|

81. taina  khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ licchavikumārakā bhikkhuṃ gahaitvā vaisiyā vippaṭipādaisuṃ… paṇḍakai vippaṭipādaisuṃ… gihiniyā  vippaṭipādaisuṃ| bhikkhu sādiyi| bhikkhu nāsaitabbau| bhikkhu na sādiyi| bhikkhussa anāpatti|

taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ licchavikumārakā bhikkhū gahaitvā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vippaṭipādaisuṃ| ubhau sādiyiṃsu| ubhau nāsaitabbā| ubhau na sādiyiṃsu| ubhin‍naṃ anāpatti|

82. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau vuḍḍhapabbajitau bhikkhu purāṇadutiyikāya dassanaṃ agamāsi| sā – ‘aihi, bhantai, vibbhamā’ti aggahaisi| sau bhikkhu paṭik‍kamantau uttānau paripati| sā ubbhajitvā ubbhujitvā (sī॰ syā॰) aṅgajātai aṅgajātaina (sī॰) abhinisīdi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi  …pai॰… ‘‘sādiyi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, sādiyi’’nti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, asādiyantassā’’ti|

83. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu arañ‍ñai viharati| migapautakau tassa passāvaṭṭhānaṃ āgantvā passāvaṃ pivantau mukhaina aṅgajātaṃ aggahaisi| sau bhikkhu sādiyi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

paṭhamapārājikaṃ samattaṃ|

2. dutiyapārājikaṃ

84. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā sandiṭṭhā sambhattā bhikkhū isigilipassai tiṇakuṭiyau karitvā vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu| āyasmāpi dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau tiṇakuṭikaṃ karitvā vassaṃ upagacchi| atha khau tai  bhikkhū vassaṃvuṭṭhā taimāsac‍cayaina tiṇakuṭiyau bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca paṭisāmaitvā janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu| āyasmā pana dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau tatthaiva vassaṃ vasi, tattha haimantaṃ, tattha gimhaṃ| atha khau āyasmatau dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa tiṇahāriyau kaṭṭhahāriyau tiṇakuṭikaṃ bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca ādāya agamaṃsu| dutiyampi khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkaḍḍhitvā tiṇakuṭikaṃ akāsi| dutiyampi khau āyasmatau dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa tiṇahāriyau kaṭṭhahāriyau tiṇakuṭikaṃ bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca ādāya agamaṃsu| tatiyampi khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca saṃkaḍḍhitvā tiṇakuṭikaṃ akāsi| tatiyampi khau āyasmatau dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa tiṇahāriyau kaṭṭhahāriyau tiṇakuṭikaṃ bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca ādāya agamaṃsu|

atha khau āyasmatau dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa  aitadahausi – ‘‘yāvatatiyakaṃ khau mai gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa tiṇahāriyau kaṭṭhahāriyau tiṇakuṭikaṃ bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca ādāya agamaṃsu| ahaṃ khau pana susikkhitau anavayau sakai ācariyakai kumbhakārakammai pariyaudātasippau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sāmaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ madditvā sabbamattikāmayaṃ kuṭikaṃ karaiyya’’nti! atha khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau sāmaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ madditvā sabbamattikāmayaṃ  kuṭikaṃ karitvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca gaumayañ‍ca saṃkaḍḍhitvā taṃ kuṭikaṃ paci| sā ahausi kuṭikā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā lauhitikā lauhitakā (syā॰), saiyyathāpi indagaupakau| saiyyathāpi nāma kiṅkaṇikasaddau kiṅkiṇikasaddau (sī॰ syā॰) aivamaivaṃ tassā kuṭikāya saddau ahausi|

85. atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasa taṃ kuṭikaṃ abhirūpaṃ dassanīyaṃ pāsādikaṃ lauhitikaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhirūpaṃ dassanīyaṃ pāsādikaṃ lauhitikaṃ, saiyyathāpi indagaupakau’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, tassa maughapurisassa ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ  akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau sabbamattikāmayaṃ kuṭikaṃ karissati! na hi nāma, bhikkhavai, tassa maughapurisassa pāṇaisu anuddayā anukampā avihaisā bhavissati! gacchathaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, kuṭikaṃ  bhindatha| mā pacchimā janatā pāṇaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍ji| na ca, bhikkhavai, sabbamattikāmayā kuṭikā kātabbā| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti, khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā yaina sā kuṭikā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ kuṭikaṃ bhindiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa mai tumhai, āvusau, kuṭikaṃ bhindathā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavā, āvusau, bhaidāpaitī’’ti| ‘‘bhindathāvusau, sacai dhammassāmī bhaidāpaitī’’ti|

86. atha khau āyasmatau dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yāvatatiyakaṃ khau mai gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa tiṇahāriyau kaṭṭhahāriyau tiṇakuṭikaṃ bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca ādāya agamaṃsu| yāpi mayā sabbamattikāmayā kuṭikā katā sāpi bhagavatā bhaidāpitā| atthi ca mai dārugahai gaṇakau sandiṭṭhau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ dārugahai gaṇakaṃ dārūni yācitvā dārukuṭikaṃ karaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau yaina dārugahai gaṇakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā dārugahai gaṇakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāvatatiyakaṃ khau mai, āvusau, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa tiṇahāriyau kaṭṭhahāriyau tiṇakuṭikaṃ bhinditvā tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca ādāya agamaṃsu| yāpi mayā sabbamattikāmayā kuṭikā katā sāpi bhagavatā bhaidāpitā| daihi mai, āvusau, dārūni| icchāmi dārukuṭikaṃ dārukuḍḍikaṃ kuṭikaṃ (sī॰) kātu’’nti| ‘‘natthi, bhantai, tādisāni dārūni yānāhaṃ  ayyassa dadaiyyaṃ| atthi, bhantai, daivagahadārūni nagarapaṭisaṅkhārikāni āpadatthāya nikkhittāni| sacai tāni dārūni rājā dāpaiti harāpaitha, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘din‍nāni, āvusau, rañ‍ñā’’ti| atha khau dārugahai gaṇakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhammacārinau samacārinau sammacārinau (ka॰) brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā| rājāpimaisaṃ abhippasan‍nau| nārahati adin‍naṃ din‍nanti vattu’’nti| atha khau dārugahai  gaṇakau āyasmantaṃ dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘harāpaitha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau tāni dārūni khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chaidāpaitvā sakaṭaihi nibbāhāpaitvā dārukuṭikaṃ akāsi|

87. atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau rājagahai kammantai anusañ‍ñāyamānau yaina dārugahai gaṇakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā dārugahai gaṇakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāni tāni, bhaṇai, daivagahadārūni nagarapaṭisaṅkhārikāni āpadatthāya nikkhittāni kahaṃ tāni dārūnī’’ti? ‘‘tāni, sāmi, dārūni daivaina ayyassa dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa din‍nānī’’ti| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau  anattamanau ahausi – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivau daivagahadārūni nagarapaṭisaṅkhārikāni āpadatthāya nikkhittāni dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa dassatī’’ti! atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, daivaina sac‍caṃ kira daiva daivaina (sī॰) daivagahadārūni nagarapaṭisaṅkhārikāni āpadatthāya nikkhittāni dhaniyassa kumbhakāraputtassa din‍nānī’’ti? ‘‘kau aivamāhā’’ti? ‘‘dārugahai gaṇakau, daivā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, dārugahai gaṇakaṃ āṇāpaihī’’ti| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau dārugahai gaṇakaṃ bandhaṃ baddhaṃ (sī॰) āṇāpaisi| addasa khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau dārugahai gaṇakaṃ bandhaṃ niyyamānaṃ| disvāna dārugahai gaṇakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, bandhau niyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘taisaṃ, bhantai, dārūnaṃ kic‍cā’’ti| ‘‘gacchāvusau, ahampi āgacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘aiyyāsi, bhantai, purāhaṃ hañ‍ñāmī’’ti|

88. atha khau āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau yaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau rājā māgadhau  sainiyau bimbisārau yaināyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ  dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmantaṃ dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira mayā, bhantai, daivagahadārūni nagarapaṭisaṅkhārikāni āpadatthāya nikkhittāni ayyassa din‍nānī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘mayaṃ khau, bhantai, rājānau nāma bahukic‍cā bahukaraṇīyā, datvāpi na saraiyyāma; iṅgha, bhantai, sarāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, mahārāja, paṭhamābhisittau aivarūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā – ‘‘din‍nañ‍ñaiva samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ tiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ paribhuñ‍jantū’’ti| ‘‘sarāmahaṃ, bhantai| santi, bhantai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā laj‍jinau kuk‍kuc‍cakā sikkhākāmā| taisaṃ appamattakaipi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| taisaṃ mayā sandhāya bhāsitaṃ, tañ‍ca khau arañ‍ñai apariggahitaṃ| sau tvaṃ, bhantai, taina laisaina dārūni adin‍naṃ harituṃ mañ‍ñasi! kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ hanaiyya vā bandhaiyya vā pabbājaiyya vā! gaccha, bhantai, laumaina tvaṃ muttausi| māssu punapi aivarūpaṃ akāsī’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘alaj‍jinau imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dussīlā musāvādinau| imai hi nāma dhammacārinau  samacārinau brāhmacārinau sac‍cavādinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā paṭijānissanti! natthi imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ, natthi imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ| naṭṭhaṃ imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ, naṭṭhaṃ imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ| kutau imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ, kutau imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ! apagatā imai sāmañ‍ñā, apagatā imai brahmañ‍ñā| rājānampi imai vañ‍cainti, kiṃ panañ‍ñai manussai’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā santuṭṭhā laj‍jinau kuk‍kuc‍cakā sikkhākāmā tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau rañ‍ñau dārūni adin‍naṃ ādiyissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, dhaniya, rañ‍ñau dārūni adin‍naṃ ādiyī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, rañ‍ñau dārūni adin‍naṃ ādiyissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya; athakhvaitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purāṇavauhārikau  mahāmattau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittakaina khau bhikkhu rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanati vā bandhati vā pabbājaiti vā’’ti? ‘‘pādaina vā, bhagavā, pādārahaina vā’’ti pādārahainavā atiraikapādainavāti (syā॰)| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai pañ‍camāsakau pādau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

89. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, yathārūpai adin‍nādānai rājānau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanaiyyuṃ vā bandhaiyyuṃ vā pabbājaiyyuṃ vā – ‘caurausi bālausi mūḷhausi thainausī’ti, tathārūpaṃ bhikkhu adin‍naṃ ādiyamānau ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

90. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ avaharitvā ārāmaṃ haritvā bhājaisuṃ| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘mahāpuñ‍ñattha tumhai, āvusau| bahuṃ tumhākaṃ cīvaraṃ uppan‍na’’nti| ‘‘kutau āvusau, amhākaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, idāni mayaṃ rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ avaharimhā’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kissa tumhai, āvusau, rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ avaharitthā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| tañ‍ca khau gāmai, nau arañ‍ñai’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, tathaivaitaṃ hauti| ananucchavikaṃ, āvusau, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tumhai, āvusau, rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ avaharissatha! naitaṃ, āvusau, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya; athakhvaitaṃ, āvusau, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ avaharitthā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisā, ananulaumikaṃ appaṭirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ  avaharissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya; atha khvaitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā bhikkhūnaṃ tadanucchavikaṃ tadanulaumikaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

91. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu gāmā vā arañ‍ñā vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, yathārūpai adin‍nādānai  rājānau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanaiyyuṃ vā bandhaiyyuṃ vā pabbājaiyyuṃ vā – ‘caurausi bālausi mūḷhausi thainausī’ti, tathārūpaṃ bhikkhu adin‍naṃ ādiyamānau ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau’’ti|

92. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

gāmau nāma aikakuṭikaupi gāmau, dvikuṭikaupi gāmau, tikuṭikaupi gāmau, catukuṭikaupi gāmau, samanussaupi gāmau, amanussaupi gāmau, parikkhittaupi gāmau, aparikkhittaupi gāmau, gaunisādiniviṭṭhaupi gāmau, yaupi satthau atiraikacatumāsaniviṭṭhau saupi vuc‍cati gāmau|

gāmūpacārau nāma parikkhittassa gāmassa indakhīlai indakhilai (ka॰) ṭhitassa majjhimassa purisassa laiḍḍupātau, aparikkhittassa gāmassa gharūpacārai ṭhitassa majjhimassa purisassa laiḍḍupātau|

arañ‍ñaṃ  nāma ṭhapaitvā gāmañ‍ca gāmūpacārañ‍ca avasaisaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ nāma|

adin‍naṃ nāmaṃ yaṃ adin‍naṃ anissaṭṭhaṃ aparic‍cattaṃ rakkhitaṃ gaupitaṃ mamāyitaṃ parapariggahitaṃ| aitaṃ adin‍naṃ nāma|

thaiyyasaṅkhātanti thaiyyacittau avaharaṇacittau|

ādiyaiyyāti  ādiyaiyya haraiyya avaharaiyya iriyāpathaṃ vikaupaiyya ṭhānā cāvaiyya saṅkaitaṃ vītināmaiyya|

yathārūpaṃ nāma pādaṃ vā pādārahaṃ vā atiraikapādaṃ vā|

rājānau  nāma pathabyārājā padaisarājā maṇḍalikā antarabhaugikā  akkhadassā mahāmattā, yai vā pana chaij‍jabhaij‍jaṃ karauntā anusāsanti| aitai rājānau nāma|

caurau nāma yau pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati| aisau caurau nāma|

hanaiyyuṃ vāti hatthaina vā pādaina vā kasāya vā vaittaina vā aḍḍhadaṇḍakaina vā chaij‍jāya vā hanaiyyuṃ|

bandhaiyyuṃ vāti raj‍jubandhanaina vā andubandhanaina vā saṅkhalikabandhanaina vā gharabandhanaina vā nagarabandhanaina vā gāmabandhanaina vā nigamabandhanaina vā bandhaiyyuṃ, purisaguttiṃ vā karaiyyuṃ|

pabbājaiyyuṃ vāti gāmā vā nigamā vā nagarā vā janapadā vā janapadapadaisā vā pabbājaiyyuṃ|

caurausi bālausi mūḷhausi thainausīti paribhāsau aisau|

tathārūpaṃ nāma pādaṃ vā pādārahaṃ vā atiraikapādaṃ vā|

ādiyamānauti ādiyamānau haramānau avaharamānau iriyāpathaṃ vikaupayamānau ṭhānā cāvayamānau saṅkaitaṃ vītināmayamānau|

ayampīti  purimaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati|

pārājikau  hautīti saiyyathāpi nāma paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pavuttau haritattāya (sī॰ syā॰) abhabbau haritatthāya haritattāya (sī॰ syā॰), aivamaiva bhikkhu pādaṃ vā pādārahaṃ vā atiraikapādaṃ vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘pārājikau hautī’ti|

asaṃvāsauti saṃvāsau nāma aikakammaṃ aikuddaisau  samasikkhatā| aisau saṃvāsau nāma| sau taina saddhiṃ natthi| taina vuc‍cati – ‘asaṃvāsau’ti|

93. bhūmaṭṭhaṃ thalaṭṭhaṃ ākāsaṭṭhaṃ vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ udakaṭṭhaṃ nāvaṭṭhaṃ yānaṭṭhaṃ bhāraṭṭhaṃ ārāmaṭṭhaṃ vihāraṭṭhaṃ khaittaṭṭhaṃ vatthuṭṭhaṃ gāmaṭṭhaṃ arañ‍ñaṭṭhaṃ udakaṃ dantapauṇaṃ dantapaunaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) vanappati haraṇakaṃ upanidhi suṅkaghātaṃ pāṇau apadaṃ dvipadaṃ catuppadaṃ bahuppadaṃ aucarakau auṇirakkhau saṃvidāvahārau saṅkaitakammaṃ nimittakammanti|

94. bhūmaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhittaṃ hauti nikhātaṃ paṭicchan‍naṃ| bhūmaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati kudālaṃ vā piṭakaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tattha jātakaṃ  kaṭṭhaṃ vā lataṃ vā chindati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tattha paṃsuṃ khaṇati vā byūhati viyūhati (syā॰) vā uddharati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kumbhiṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| attanau bhājanaṃ pavaisaitvā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| attanau bhājanagataṃ vā karauti muṭṭhiṃ vā chindati, āpatti pārājikassa| suttāruḷhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ pāmaṅgaṃ vā kaṇṭhasuttakaṃ  vā kaṭisuttakaṃ vā sāṭakaṃ vā vaiṭhanaṃ vā thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kauṭiyaṃ gahaitvā uc‍cāraiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ghaṃsantau nīharati, āpatti  thul‍lac‍cayassa| antamasau kaisaggamattampi kumbhimukhā maucaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| sappiṃ vā tailaṃ vā madhuṃ vā phāṇitaṃ vā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau aikaina payaugaina pivati, āpatti pārājikassa| tatthaiva bhindati vā chaḍḍaiti vā jhāpaiti vā aparibhaugaṃ vā karauti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

95. thalaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ thalai nikkhittaṃ hauti| thalaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau  dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

96. ākāsaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ ākāsagataṃ hauti| maurau vā kapiñ‍jarau vā tittirau vā vaṭṭakau vā, sāṭakaṃ vā vaiṭhanaṃ vā hirañ‍ñaṃ vā suvaṇṇaṃ vā chij‍jamānaṃ patati| ākāsaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| gamanaṃ upacchindati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

97. vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ vaihāsagataṃ hauti| mañ‍cai vā pīṭhai vā cīvaravaṃsai vā cīvararaj‍juyā vā bhittikhilai vā nāgadantai vā rukkhai vā laggitaṃ hauti, antamasau pattādhārakaipi| vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

98. udakaṭṭhaṃ  nāma bhaṇḍaṃ udakai nikkhittaṃ hauti| udakaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nimuj‍jati vā ummuj‍jati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| tattha jātakaṃ uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ vā bhisaṃ vā macchaṃ vā kacchapaṃ vā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

99. nāvā  nāma yāya tarati| nāvaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ nāvāya nikkhittaṃ hauti| ‘‘nāvaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmī’’ti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ  vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| nāvaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti  thul‍lac‍cayassa| bandhanaṃ maucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bandhanaṃ maucaitvā āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| uddhaṃ vā adhau vā tiriyaṃ vā antamasau kaisaggamattampi saṅkāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

100. yānaṃ nāma vayhaṃ rathau sakaṭaṃ sandamānikā| yānaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ yānai nikkhittaṃ hauti| yānaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| yānaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

101. bhārau nāma sīsabhārau khandhabhārau kaṭibhārau aulambakau| sīsai bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| khandhaṃ auraupaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| khandhai  bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| kaṭiṃ auraupaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| kaṭiyā bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| hatthaina gaṇhāti, āpatti pārājikassa| hatthai bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipati, āpatti pārājikassa| thaiyyacittau bhūmitau gaṇhāti, āpatti pārājikassa|

102. ārāmau nāma pupphārāmau phalārāmau| ārāmaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ ārāmai catūhi ṭhānaihi nikkhittaṃ hauti – bhūmaṭṭhaṃ thalaṭṭhaṃ, ākāsaṭṭhaṃ, vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ| ārāmaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti  thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati  gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| tattha jātakaṃ mūlaṃ vā tacaṃ vā pattaṃ vā pupphaṃ vā phalaṃ vā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| ārāmaṃ abhiyuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sāmikassa vimatiṃ uppādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| sāmikau na mayhaṃ bhavissatīti dhuraṃ nikkhipati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau sāmikaṃ parājaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau paraj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

103. vihāraṭṭhaṃ  nāma bhaṇḍaṃ vihārai catūhi ṭhānaihi nikkhittaṃ hauti – bhūmaṭṭhaṃ, thalaṭṭhaṃ, ākāsaṭṭhaṃ, vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ| vihāraṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| vihāraṃ abhiyuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sāmikassa vimatiṃ uppādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| sāmikau na mayhaṃ bhavissatīti dhuraṃ nikkhipati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau sāmikaṃ parājaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau paraj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

104. khaittaṃ nāma yattha pubbaṇṇaṃ vā aparaṇṇaṃ vā jāyati| khaittaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ khaittai catūhi ṭhānaihi nikkhittaṃ hauti – bhūmaṭṭhaṃ, thalaṭṭhaṃ, ākāsaṭṭhaṃ, vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ| khaittaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti, duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| tattha jātakaṃ pubbaṇṇaṃ vā aparaṇṇaṃ vā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| khaittaṃ abhiyuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sāmikassa vimatiṃ uppādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| sāmikau na  mayhaṃ bhavissatīti dhuraṃ nikkhipati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau sāmikaṃ parājaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau paraj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| khilaṃ vā raj‍juṃ  vā vatiṃ vā mariyādaṃ vā saṅkāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikaṃ payaugaṃ anāgatai, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| tasmiṃ payaugai āgatai, āpatti pārājikassa|

105. vatthu nāma ārāmavatthu vihāravatthu| vatthuṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ vatthusmiṃ catūhi ṭhānaihi nikkhittaṃ hauti – bhūmaṭṭhaṃ, thalaṭṭhaṃ, ākāsaṭṭhaṃ, vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ| vatthuṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| vatthuṃ abhiyuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sāmikassa vimatiṃ uppādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| sāmikau na mayhaṃ bhavissatīti dhuraṃ nikkhipati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau sāmikaṃ parājaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau paraj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| khīlaṃ  vā raj‍juṃ vā vatiṃ vā pākāraṃ vā saṅkāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikaṃ payaugaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| tasmiṃ payaugai āgatai āpatti pārājikassa|

106. gāmaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ gāmai catūhi ṭhānaihi nikkhittaṃ hauti  – bhūmaṭṭhaṃ, thalaṭṭhaṃ, ākāsaṭṭhaṃ, vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ| gāmaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

107. arañ‍ñaṃ  nāma yaṃ manussānaṃ pariggahitaṃ hauti, taṃ arañ‍ñaṃ| arañ‍ñaṭṭhaṃ nāma bhaṇḍaṃ arañ‍ñai catūhi ṭhānaihi nikkhittaṃ hauti – bhūmaṭṭhaṃ, thalaṭṭhaṃ, ākāsaṭṭhaṃ, vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ| arañ‍ñaṭṭhaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmīti thaiyyacittau dutiyaṃ vā pariyaisati gacchati vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| tattha jātakaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ vā lataṃ vā tiṇaṃ vā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

108. udakaṃ  nāma bhājanagataṃ vā hauti paukkharaṇiyā vā taḷākai vā| thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| attanau bhājanaṃ pavaisaitvā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ udakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| attanau bhājanagataṃ karauti, āpatti pārājikassa| mariyādaṃ bhindati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| mariyādaṃ bhinditvā pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ udakaṃ nikkhāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| atiraikamāsakaṃ vā ūnapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ udakaṃ nikkhāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| māsakaṃ vā ūnamāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ udakaṃ nikkhāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

109. dantapauṇaṃ nāma chin‍naṃ vā acchin‍naṃ vā| pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

110. vanappati  nāma yau manussānaṃ pariggahitau hauti rukkhau paribhaugau| thaiyyacittau chindati, pahārai pahārai āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikaṃ pahāraṃ anāgatai, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| tasmiṃ pahārai āgatai, āpatti pārājikassa|

111. haraṇakaṃ nāma añ‍ñassa haraṇakaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ| thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| sahabhaṇḍahārakaṃ padasā naissāmīti  paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| patitaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ gahaissāmīti pātāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| patitaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

112. upanidhi nāma upanikkhittaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ| daihi mai bhaṇḍanti vuc‍camānau nāhaṃ gaṇhāmīti bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sāmikassa vimatiṃ uppādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| sāmikau na mayhaṃ dassatīti dhuraṃ nikkhipati, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau sāmikaṃ parājaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| dhammaṃ carantau paraj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

113. suṅkaghātaṃ nāma rañ‍ñā ṭhapitaṃ hauti pabbatakhaṇḍai vā nadītitthai vā gāmadvārai vā – ‘atra paviṭṭhassa suṅkaṃ gaṇhantū’ti| tatra pavisitvā rājaggaṃ rājagghaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) bhaṇḍaṃ pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ suṅkaghātaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| antausuṅkaghātai ṭhitau bahisuṅkaghātaṃ pātaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| suṅkaṃ pariharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

114. pāṇau  nāma manussapāṇau vuc‍cati| thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| padasā naissāmīti paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

apadaṃ  nāma ahi macchā| pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

115. dvipadaṃ nāma manussā, pakkhajātā| thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| padasā naissāmīti paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

116. catuppadaṃ nāma – hatthī assā auṭṭhā gauṇā gadrabhā pasukā| thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| padasā naissāmīti paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti  thul‍lac‍cayassa| tatiyaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| catutthaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti  pārājikassa|

117. bahuppadaṃ nāma – vicchikā satapadī uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakā| pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa| padasā naissāmīti saṅkāmaiti, padai padai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| pacchimaṃ pādaṃ saṅkāmaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

118. aucarakau nāma bhaṇḍaṃ aucaritvā ācikkhati – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa|

auṇirakkhau  nāma āhaṭaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ gaupaintau pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

saṃvidāvahārau nāma sambahulā saṃvidahitvā aikau bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti sabbaisaṃ pārājikassa|

119. saṅkaitakammaṃ  nāma saṅkaitaṃ karauti – ‘‘puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā rattiṃ vā divā vā taina saṅkaitaina taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| taina saṅkaitaina taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa| taṃ saṅkaitaṃ purai vā pacchā  vā taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, mūlaṭṭhassa anāpatti| avahārakassa āpatti pārājikassa|

120. nimittakammaṃ nāma nimittaṃ karauti| akkhiṃ vā nikhaṇissāmi bhamukaṃ vā ukkhipissāmi sīsaṃ vā ukkhipissāmi, taina nimittaina taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| taina nimittaina taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa| taṃ nimittaṃ purai vā pacchā vā taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, mūlaṭṭhassa anāpatti| avahārakassa āpatti pārājikassa|

121. bhikkhu  bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau taṃ mañ‍ñamānau taṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau taṃ mañ‍ñamānau añ‍ñaṃ avaharati, mūlaṭṭhassa anāpatti| avahārakassa āpatti pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau añ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñamānau taṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau añ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñamānau añ‍ñaṃ avaharati, mūlaṭṭhassa anāpatti| avahārakassa āpatti pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmassa pāvada – ‘itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa pāvadatu – itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmaṃ  bhaṇḍaṃ avaharatū’’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau itarassa āraucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| avahārakau paṭiggaṇhāti, mūlaṭṭhassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| sau taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti sabbaisaṃ pārājikassa|

bhikkhu  bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmassa pāvada – ‘itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa pāvadatu – itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharatū’’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| avahārakau paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, mūlaṭṭhassa anāpatti| āṇāpakassa ca avahārakassa ca āpatti pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ  bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau gantvā puna pac‍cāgacchati – ‘‘nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharitu’’nti| sau puna āṇāpaiti – ‘‘yadā sak‍kausi tadā taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa|

bhikkhu  bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau āṇāpaitvā vippaṭisārī na sāvaiti – ‘‘mā avaharī’’ti| sau taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau āṇāpaitvā vippaṭisārī sāvaiti – ‘‘mā avaharī’’ti| sau ‘‘āṇattau ahaṃ tayā’’ti, taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, mūlaṭṭhassa anāpatti| avahārakassa  āpatti pārājikassa|

bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ avaharā’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau āṇāpaitvā vippaṭisārī sāvaiti – ‘‘mā avaharī’’ti| sau ‘‘sādhū’’ti suṭṭhūti (ka॰)? auramati, ubhin‍naṃ anāpatti|

122. pañ‍cahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti pārājikassa – parapariggahitañ‍ca hauti, parapariggahitasañ‍ñī ca, garukau ca hauti parikkhārau, pañ‍camāsakau vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

123. pañ‍cahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa – parapariggahitañ‍ca hauti, parapariggahitasañ‍ñī ca, lahukau ca hauti parikkhārau, atiraikamāsakau  vā ūnapañ‍camāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

124. pañ‍cahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| parapariggahitañ‍ca hauti, parapariggahitasañ‍ñī ca, lahukau ca hauti parikkhārau, māsakau vā ūnamāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

125. chahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti pārājikassa| na  ca sakasañ‍ñī, na ca vissāsaggāhī, na ca tāvakālikaṃ, garukau ca hauti  parikkhārau, pañ‍camāsakau vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti dukkaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti pārājikassa|

126. chahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| na ca sakasañ‍ñī, na ca vissāsaggāhī, na ca tāvakālikaṃ, lahukau ca hauti parikkhārau atiraikamāsakau  vā ūnapañ‍camāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

127. chahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca sakasañ‍ñī, na ca vissāsaggāhī, na ca tāvakālikaṃ, lahukau ca hauti parikkhārau, māsakau vā ūnamāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

128. pañ‍cahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca parapariggahitaṃ hauti, parapariggahitasañ‍ñī ca, garukau ca hauti parikkhārau, pañ‍camāsakau vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

129. pañ‍cahi  ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na  ca parapariggahitaṃ hauti, parapariggahitasañ‍ñī ca, lahukau ca hauti parikkhārau, atiraikamāsakau vā ūnapañ‍camāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

130. pañ‍cahi ākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca parapariggahitaṃ hauti, parapariggahitasañ‍ñī ca, lahukau ca hauti parikkhārau, māsakau vā ūnamāsakau vā, thaiyyacittañ‍ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti| āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| phandāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ṭhānā cāvaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

131. anāpatti  sasañ‍ñissa, vissāsaggāhai, tāvakālikai, paitapariggahai, tiracchānagatapariggahai, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissa, ummattakassa, (khittacittassa vaidanāṭṭassa) (khittacittassa vaidanāṭṭassa) katthaci natthi ādikammikassāti|

adin‍nādānamhi paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

rajakaihi pañ‍ca akkhātā, caturau attharaṇaihi ca|

andhakāraina vai pañ‍ca, pañ‍ca hāraṇakaina ca||

niruttiyā pañ‍ca akkhātā, vātaihi aparai duvai|

asambhin‍nai kusāpātau, jantaggaina jantāgharaina (syā॰) sahā dasa||

vighāsaihi pañ‍ca akkhātā, pañ‍ca caiva amūlakā|

dubbhikkhai kuramaṃsañ‍ca kūramaṃsañ‍ca (syā॰), pūvasakkhalimaudakā||

chaparikkhārathavikā, bhisivaṃsā na nikkhamai|

khādanīyañ‍ca vissāsaṃ, sasañ‍ñāyaparai duvai||

satta  nāvaharāmāti, satta caiva avāharuṃ|

saṅghassa avaharuṃ satta, pupphaihi aparai duvai||

tayau ca vuttavādinau, maṇi tīṇi atik‍kamai|

sūkarā ca migā macchā, yānañ‍cāpi pavattayi||

duvai paisī duvai dārū, paṃsukūlaṃ duvai dakā|

anupubbavidhānaina, tadañ‍ñau na paripūrayi||

sāvatthiyā  caturau muṭṭhī, dvai vighāsā duvai tiṇā|

saṅghassa bhājayuṃ satta, satta caiva assāmikā||

dārudakā mattikā dvai tiṇāni|

saṅghassa satta avahāsi saiyyaṃ|

sassāmikaṃ na cāpi nīharaiyya|

haraiyya sassāmikaṃ tāvakālikaṃ||

campā rājagahai caiva, vaisāliyā ca aj‍jukau|

bārāṇasī ca kausambī, sāgalā daḷhikaina cāti||

vinītavatthu

132. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā rajakabhaṇḍikaṃ avahariṃsu| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kac‍ci nu khau mayaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā mahagghaṃ dussaṃ passitvā thaiyyacittaṃ uppādaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau  ahaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, cittuppādai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā mahagghaṃ dussaṃ passitvā thaiyyacittau āmasi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā mahagghaṃ dussaṃ passitvā thaiyyacittau phandāpaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu rajakattharaṇaṃ gantvā mahagghaṃ dussaṃ passitvā thaiyyacittau ṭhānā cāvaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

133. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu mahagghaṃ uttarattharaṇaṃ passitvā thaiyyacittaṃ uppādaisi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau āmasi…pai॰…  thaiyyacittau phandāpaisi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau ṭhānā cāvaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

134. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu divā bhaṇḍaṃ passitvā nimittaṃ akāsi – ‘rattiṃ avaharissāmī’ti| sau taṃ mañ‍ñamānau taṃ avahari…pai॰… taṃ mañ‍ñamānau añ‍ñaṃ avahari…pai॰… añ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñamānau taṃ avahari…pai॰… añ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñamānau añ‍ñaṃ avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu divā bhaṇḍaṃ passitvā nimittaṃ akāsi – ‘‘rattiṃ avaharissāmī’’ti| sau taṃ mañ‍ñamānau attanau bhaṇḍaṃ avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñassa bhaṇḍaṃ harantau sīsai bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau āmasi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau phandāpaisi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau khandhaṃ auraupaisi…pai॰…  khandhai bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau āmasi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau phandāpaisi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau kaṭiṃ auraupaisi…pai॰… kaṭiyā bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau āmasi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau phandāpaisi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau hatthaina aggahaisi…pai॰… hatthai bhāraṃ thaiyyacittau bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipi…pai॰… thaiyyacittau bhūmitau aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

135. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ajjhaukāsai cīvaraṃ pattharitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘māyidaṃ cīvaraṃ nassī’ti, paṭisāmaisi| sau nikkhamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ pucchi – ‘‘āvusau, mayhaṃ cīvaraṃ kaina avahaṭa’’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘‘mayā avahaṭa’’nti| ‘‘sau taṃ ādiyi, assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰…| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘niruttipathau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, niruttipathai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pīṭhai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā| pīṭhai nisīdanaṃ nikkhipitvā… haiṭṭhāpīṭhai pattaṃ nikkhipitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu – ‘‘māyaṃ pattau nassī’’ti paṭisāmaisi| sau nikkhamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ pucchi – ‘‘āvusau, mayhaṃ pattau kaina avahaṭau’’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘‘mayā avahaṭau’’ti| ‘‘sau taṃ ādiyi, assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, niruttipathai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī vatiyā cīvaraṃ pattharitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī – ‘māyidaṃ  cīvaraṃ nassī’ti paṭisāmaisi| sā nikkhamitvā taṃ bhikkhuniṃ pucchi – ‘‘ayyai, mayhaṃ cīvaraṃ kaina avahaṭa’’nti? sā aivamāha – ‘‘mayā avahaṭa’’nti| ‘‘sā taṃ ādiyi, assamaṇīsi tva’’nti| tassā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, niruttipathai’’ti|

136. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vātamaṇḍalikāya ukkhittaṃ sāṭakaṃ passitvā sāmikānaṃ dassāmīti, aggahaisi| sāmikā taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘athaiyyacittau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| anāpatti, bhikkhu, athaiyyacittassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vātamaṇḍalikāya ukkhittaṃ vaiṭhanaṃ passitvā ‘purai sāmikā passantī’ti thaiyyacittau aggahaisi| sāmikā taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

137. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu susānaṃ gantvā abhin‍nai sarīrai paṃsukūlaṃ aggahaisi| tasmiñ‍ca sarīrai paitau adhivatthau hauti| atha khau sau paitau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, bhantai, mayhaṃ sāṭakaṃ aggahaisī’’ti| sau bhikkhu anādiyantau agamāsi| atha khau taṃ sarīraṃ uṭṭhahitvā tassa bhikkhunau piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| atha khau sau bhikkhu vihāraṃ pavisitvā dvāraṃ thakaisi| atha khau taṃ sarīraṃ tatthaiva paripati| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, abhin‍nai sarīrai paṃsukūlaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| sau gaṇhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

138. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa cīvarai bhājīyamānai thaiyyacittau kusaṃ saṅkāmaitvā cīvaraṃ aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

139. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau jantāgharai añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau antaravāsakaṃ attanau mañ‍ñamānau nivāsaisi| atha khau sau bhikkhu āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa mai tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, antaravāsakaṃ nivāsaisī’’ti? ‘‘sakasañ‍ñī ahaṃ, āvusau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, sakasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

140. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantā sīhavighāsaṃ  passitvā pacāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, sīhavighāsai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantā byagghavighāsaṃ passitvā… dīpivighāsaṃ  passitvā… taracchavighāsaṃ passitvā… kaukavighāsaṃ passitvā pacāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatapariggahai’’ti|

141. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa audanai bhājīyamānai – ‘aparassa bhāgaṃ daihī’ti amūlakaṃ aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa khādanīyai bhājiyamānai… saṅghassa pūvai bhājiyamānai… saṅghassa ucchumhi bhājiyamānai… saṅghassa timbarūsakai bhājiyamānai – ‘aparassa bhāgaṃ daihī’ti amūlakaṃ aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰…| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyassā’’ti|

142. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dubbhikkhai audanīyagharaṃ pavisitvā pattapūraṃ audanaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dubbhikkhai sūnagharaṃ sūnāgharaṃ (sī॰ syā) pavisitvā pattapūraṃ maṃsaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dubbhikkhai pūvagharaṃ pavisitvā pattapūraṃ pūvaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari…pai॰… pattapūrā sakkhaliyau thaiyyacittau avahari…pai॰… pattapūrai  maudakai thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

143. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu divā parikkhāraṃ passitvā nimittaṃ akāsi – ‘‘rattiṃ avaharissāmī’’ti| sau taṃ mañ‍ñamānau taṃ avahari…pai॰… taṃ mañ‍ñamānau añ‍ñaṃ avahari…pai॰… añ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñamānau taṃ avahari…pai॰… añ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñamānau añ‍ñaṃ avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu divā parikkhāraṃ passitvā nimittaṃ akāsi – ‘‘rattiṃ avaharissāmī’’ti| sau taṃ mañ‍ñamānau attanau parikkhāraṃ avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

144. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pīṭhai thavikaṃ passitvā – ‘‘itau gaṇhantau pārājikau bhavissāmī’’ti saha pīṭhakaina saṅkāmaitvā  aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa bhisiṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

145. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu cīvaravaṃsai cīvaraṃ  thaiyyacittau  avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu vihārai cīvaraṃ avaharitvā – ‘‘itau nikkhamantau pārājikau bhavissāmī’’ti vihārā na nikkhami…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘nikkhami nikkhamaiyya (sī॰ syā॰) vā sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau na vā nikkhami nikkhamaiyya (sī॰ syā॰), āpatti pārājikassā’’ti|

146. taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhū sahāyakā haunti| aikau bhikkhu gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| dutiyau bhikkhu saṅghassa khādanīyai bhājīyamānai sahāyakassa bhāgaṃ gahaitvā tassa  vissasantau paribhuñ‍ji| sau jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃ cittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘vissāsaggāhau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, vissāsaggāhai’’ti|

147. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū cīvarakammaṃ karaunti| saṅghassa khādanīyai bhājīyamānai sabbaisaṃ paṭivisā āharitvā upanikkhittā haunti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau paṭivisaṃ attanau mañ‍ñamānau paribhuñ‍ji| sau jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘sakasañ‍ñī ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, sakasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū cīvarakammaṃ  karaunti| saṅghassa khādanīyai bhājiyamānai añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau pattaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau  paṭivisau āharitvā upanikkhittau hauti| pattasāmikau bhikkhu attanau mañ‍ñamānau paribhuñ‍ji| sau jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, sakasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

148. taina khau pana samayaina ambacaurakā ambaṃ pātaitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| sāmikā tai caurakai anubandhiṃsu| caurakā sāmikai passitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ pātaitvā palāyiṃsu| bhikkhū paṃsukūlasañ‍ñinau paṭiggahāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| sāmikā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘kiṃcittā tumhai, bhikkhavai’’ti? ‘‘paṃsukūlasañ‍ñinau mayaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina jambucaurakā… labujacaurakā… panasacaurakā… tālapak‍kacaurakā… ucchucaurakā… timbarūsakacaurakā timbarūsakai  uc‍cinitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| sāmikā tai caurakai anubandhiṃsu| caurakā sāmikai passitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ pātaitvā palāyiṃsu| bhikkhū paṃsukūlasañ‍ñinau paṭiggahāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| sāmikā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina ambacaurakā ambaṃ pātaitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ  ādāya agamaṃsu| sāmikā tai caurakai anubandhiṃsu| caurakā sāmikai passitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ pātaitvā palāyiṃsu| bhikkhū – ‘purai sāmikā passantī’ti, thaiyyacittā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| sāmikā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina jambucaurakā… labujacaurakā… panasacaurakā… tālapak‍kacaurakā… ucchucaurakā… timbarūsakacaurakā timbarūsakai uc‍cinitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| sāmikā tai caurakai anubandhiṃsu| caurakā sāmikai passitvā bhaṇḍikaṃ pātaitvā  palāyiṃsu| bhikkhū – ‘purai sāmikā passantī’ti, thaiyyacittā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| sāmikā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa ambaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari… saṅghassa jambuṃ… saṅghassa labujaṃ… saṅghassa panasaṃ… saṅghassa tālapak‍kaṃ… saṅghassa ucchuṃ… saṅghassa timbarūsakaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

149. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pupphārāmaṃ gantvā aucitaṃ pupphaṃ pañ‍camāsagghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau  pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pupphārāmaṃ gantvā pupphaṃ aucinitvā pañ‍camāsagghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

150. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gāmakaṃ gacchantau añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āvusau, tuyhaṃ upaṭṭhākakulaṃ vuttau vaj‍jaimī’’ti| sau gantvā aikaṃ sāṭakaṃ āharāpaitvā attanā paribhuñ‍ji| sau jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, vuttau vaj‍jaimīti vattabbau| yau vadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gāmakaṃ gacchati| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āvusau, mayhaṃ upaṭṭhākakulaṃ vuttau vaj‍jaihī’’ti| sau gantvā yugasāṭakaṃ āharāpaitvā aikaṃ attanā paribhuñ‍ji, aikaṃ tassa bhikkhunau adāsi| sau jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, vuttau vaj‍jaihīti vattabbau| yau vadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gāmakaṃ gacchantau  añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āvusau, tuyhaṃ upaṭṭhākakulaṃ vuttau vaj‍jaimī’’ti| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘vuttau vaj‍jaihī’’ti| sau gantvā āḷhakaṃ sappiṃ tulaṃ guḷaṃ dauṇaṃ taṇḍulaṃ āharāpaitvā attanā paribhuñ‍ji| sau jānitvā taṃ caudaisi  – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, vuttau vaj‍jaimīti vattabbau, na ca vuttau vaj‍jaihīti vattabbau| yau vadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

151. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau mahagghaṃ maṇiṃ ādāya añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| atha khau sau purisau suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ passitvā tassa bhikkhunau ajānantassa thavikāya maṇiṃ pakkhipitvā suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ atik‍kamitvā aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, jānāmī’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, ajānantassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau mahagghaṃ maṇiṃ ādāya añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| atha khau sau purisau suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ passitvā gilānālayaṃ karitvā attanau bhaṇḍikaṃ tassa bhikkhunau adāsi| atha khau sau purisau suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ atik‍kamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āhara mai, bhantai, bhaṇḍikaṃ; nāhaṃ akal‍lakau’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, āvusau, aivarūpaṃ akāsī’’ti? atha khau sau purisau tassa bhikkhunau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, jānāmī’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, ajānantassā’’ti|

152. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu satthaina saddhiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| añ‍ñatarau purisau  taṃ bhikkhuṃ āmisaina upalāpaitvā suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ passitvā mahagghaṃ maṇiṃ tassa bhikkhunau adāsi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, maṇiṃ suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ atik‍kāmaihī’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu taṃ maṇiṃ suṅkaṭṭhānaṃ atik‍kāmaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

153. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pāsai bandhaṃ sūkaraṃ kāruñ‍ñaina muñ‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘kāruñ‍ñādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, kāruñ‍ñādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pāsai bandhaṃ sūkaraṃ – ‘‘purai sāmikā passantī’’ti, thaiyyacittau muñ‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pāsai bandhaṃ migaṃ kāruñ‍ñaina muñ‍ci… pāsai bandhaṃ migaṃ – ‘‘purai sāmikā passantī’’ti, thaiyyacittau muñ‍ci … kuminai bandhai macchai kāruñ‍ñaina muñ‍ci… kuminai bandhai macchai – ‘‘purai sāmikā passantī’’ti thaiyyacittau muñ‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yānai bhaṇḍaṃ passitvā – ‘‘itau gaṇhantau pārājikau bhavissāmī’’ti, atik‍kamitvā pavaṭṭaitvā pavattaitvā (ka॰) aggahaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kulalaina ukkhittaṃ maṃsapaisiṃ – ‘‘sāmikānaṃ dassāmī’’ti aggahaisi| sāmikā taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, athaiyyacittassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kulalaina ukkhittaṃ maṃsapaisiṃ – ‘‘purai sāmikā passantī’’ti, thaiyyacittau aggahaisi| sāmikā taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

154. taina khau pana samayaina manussā uḷumpaṃ bandhitvā aciravatiyā nadiyā ausārainti| bandhanai chin‍nai kaṭṭhāni vippakiṇṇāni agamaṃsu| bhikkhū paṃsukūlasañ‍ñinau uttāraisuṃ| sāmikā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha  tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā uḷumpaṃ bandhitvā aciravatiyā nadiyā ausārainti| bandhanai chin‍nai kaṭṭhāni vippakiṇṇāni agamaṃsu| bhikkhū – ‘‘purai sāmikā passantī’’ti, thaiyyacittā uttāraisuṃ| sāmikā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau gaupālakau rukkhai sāṭakaṃ ālaggaitvā uc‍cāraṃ agamāsi| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṃsukūlasañ‍ñī aggahaisi| atha khau sau gaupālakau taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau nadiṃ tarantassa rajakānaṃ hatthatau muttaṃ sāṭakaṃ pādai laggaṃ hauti| sau bhikkhu – ‘‘sāmikānaṃ dassāmī’’ti aggahaisi| sāmikā taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, athaiyyacittassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau nadiṃ tarantassa rajakānaṃ hatthatau muttaṃ sāṭakaṃ pādai laggaṃ hauti| sau bhikkhu – ‘‘purai sāmikā passantī’’ti, thaiyyacittau aggahaisi| sāmikā taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

155. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sappikumbhiṃ passitvā thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paribhuñ‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū saṃvidahitvā agamaṃsu – ‘‘bhaṇḍaṃ avaharissāmā’’ti| aikau bhaṇḍaṃ avahari| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na  mayaṃ pārājikā| yau avahaṭau sau pārājikau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ… ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū saṃvidahitvā bhaṇḍaṃ avaharitvā bhājaisuṃ| taihi bhājīyamānai aikamaikassa paṭivisau na pañ‍camāsakau pūri| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ pārājikā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘āpattiṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, āpan‍nā pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāvatthiyaṃ dubbhikkhai āpaṇikassa taṇḍulamuṭṭhiṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāvatthiyaṃ dubbhikkhai āpaṇikassa muggamuṭṭhiṃ… māsamuṭṭhiṃ… tilamuṭṭhiṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ andhavanai caurakā gāviṃ hantvā maṃsaṃ khāditvā saisakaṃ paṭisāmaitvā agamaṃsu| bhikkhū paṃsukūlasañ‍ñinau paṭiggahāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| caurakā tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ andhavanai caurakā sūkaraṃ hantvā maṃsaṃ khāditvā saisakaṃ paṭisāmaitvā agamaṃsu| bhikkhū paṃsukūlasañ‍ñinau paṭiggahāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| caurakā tai  bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tiṇakkhaittaṃ gantvā lūtaṃ tiṇaṃ pañ‍camāsagghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi  …pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tiṇakkhaittaṃ gantvā tiṇaṃ lāyitvā pañ‍camāsagghanakaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

156. taina  khau pana samayaina āgantukā bhikkhū saṅghassa ambaṃ bhājāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| āvāsikā bhikkhū tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘kiṃcittā tumhai, bhikkhavai’’ti? ‘‘paribhaugatthāya paribhaugatthā (sī॰) mayaṃ bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paribhaugatthāyā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina āgantukā bhikkhū saṅghassa jambuṃ… saṅghassa labujaṃ… saṅghassa panasaṃ… saṅghassa tālapak‍kaṃ… saṅghassa ucchuṃ… saṅghassa timbarūsakaṃ bhājāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| āvāsikā bhikkhū tai bhikkhū caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇāttha, tumhai’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, paribhaugatthāyā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina ambapālakā bhikkhūnaṃ ambaphalaṃ dainti| bhikkhū – ‘‘gaupaituṃ imai issarā, nayimai dātu’’nti, kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, gaupakassa dānai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina jambupālakā… labujapālakā… panasapālakā… tālapak‍kapālakā… ucchupālakā… timbarūsakapālakā bhikkhūnaṃ timbarūsakaṃ dainti| bhikkhū  – ‘‘gaupaituṃ imai issarā, nayimai dātu’’nti, kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, gaupakassa dānai’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa dāruṃ tāvakālikaṃ haritvā attanau vihārassa kuṭṭaṃ upatthambhaisi| bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisuṃ – ‘‘assamaṇausi tva’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘tāvakālikau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, tāvakālikai’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa udakaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari… saṅghassa mattikaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari… saṅghassa puñ‍jakitaṃ tiṇaṃ thaiyyacittau avahari… tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa puñ‍jakitaṃ tiṇaṃ thaiyyacittau jhāpaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa mañ‍caṃ thaiyyacittau avahari… tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa pīṭhaṃ… saṅghassa bhisiṃ… saṅghassa bibbauhanaṃ bimbauhanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) … saṅghassa  kavāṭaṃ… saṅghassa ālaukasandhiṃ… saṅghassa gaupānasiṃ thaiyyacittau avahari… tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

157. cūḷava॰ 324 taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū añ‍ñatarassa upāsakassa vihāraparibhaugaṃ  saināsanaṃ añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍janti| atha khau sau upāsakau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā añ‍ñatra paribhaugaṃ añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍jissantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatra paribhaugau añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍jitabbau| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

cūḷava॰ 324 taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū upausathaggampi san‍nisaj‍jampi harituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā chamāyaṃ nisīdanti| gattānipi cīvarānipi paṃsukitāni haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tāvakālikaṃ haritu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina campāyaṃ thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā antaivāsinī bhikkhunī thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā upaṭṭhākakulaṃ gantvā – ‘‘ayyā icchati  taikaṭulayāguṃ pātu’’nti, pacāpaitvā haritvā attanā paribhuñ‍ji| sā jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇīsi tva’’nti| tassā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi| atha khau sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, pārājikassa; āpatti sampajānamusāvādai  pācittiyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā antaivāsinī bhikkhunī thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā upaṭṭhākakulaṃ gantvā – ‘‘ayyā icchati madhugauḷakaṃ khāditu’’nti, pacāpaitvā haritvā attanā paribhuñ‍ji| sā jānitvā taṃ caudaisi – ‘‘assamaṇīsi tva’’nti| tassā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, pārājikassa; āpatti sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyassā’’ti|

158. taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ āyasmatau aj‍jukassa upaṭṭhākassa gahapatinau dvai dārakā haunti – puttau ca bhāginaiyyau ca| atha khau sau gahapati āyasmantaṃ aj‍jukaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, aukāsaṃ yau imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ dārakānaṃ saddhau hauti pasan‍nau tassa ācikkhaiyyāsī’’ti ācikkhaiyyāsīti sau kālamakāsi (syā॰)| taina khau pana samayaina tassa gahapatinau bhāginaiyyau saddhau hauti pasan‍nau| atha khau āyasmā aj‍jukau taṃ aukāsaṃ tassa dārakassa ācikkhi| sau taina sāpataiyyaina kuṭumbañ‍ca saṇṭhapaisi dānañ‍ca paṭṭhapaisi| atha khau tassa gahapatinau puttau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai ānanda, pitunau dāyaj‍jau – puttau vā bhāginaiyyau vā’’ti? ‘‘puttau khau, āvusau, pitunau dāyaj‍jau’’ti| ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, ayyau aj‍jukau amhākaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ  amhākaṃ maithunakassa ācikkhī’’ti| ‘‘assamaṇau, āvusau, āyasmā aj‍jukau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā aj‍jukau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daihi mai, āvusau ānanda, vinicchaya’’nti| taina khau  pana samayaina āyasmā upāli āyasmatau aj‍jukassa pakkhau hauti| atha khau āyasmā upāli āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yau  nu khau, āvusau ānanda, sāmikaina ‘imaṃ aukāsaṃ itthan‍nāmassa ācikkhaiyyāsī’ti vuttau tassa ācikkhati, kiṃ sau āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘na, bhantai, kiñ‍ci āpaj‍jati, antamasau duk‍kaṭamattampī’’ti| ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmā aj‍jukau  sāmikaina – ‘imaṃ aukāsaṃ itthan‍nāmassa ācikkhā’ti vuttau tassa ācikkhati; anāpatti, āvusau, āyasmatau aj‍jukassā’’ti|
