tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jinti vattukāmau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jinti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

vatthuvisārakassa aikamūlakassa baddhacak‍kaṃ|

mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

217. tīhākāraihi  mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti vattukāmau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jinti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti vattukāmau dausā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰… vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

vatthuvisārakassa aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

yathā aikamūlakaṃ vitthāritaṃ aivamaiva dumūlakādipi vitthāraitabbaṃ|

idaṃ sabbamūlakaṃ

218. tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ suñ‍ñatañ‍ca vimaukkhaṃ animittañ‍ca vimaukkhaṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca vimaukkhaṃ suñ‍ñatañ‍ca samādhiṃ animittañ‍ca samādhiṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca samādhiṃ suñ‍ñatañ‍ca samāpattiṃ animittañ‍ca samāpattiṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca samāpattiṃ tissau ca vij‍jā cattārau ca satipaṭṭhānai cattārau ca sammappadhānai cattārau ca iddhipādai pañ‍ca ca indriyāni pañ‍ca ca balāni satta ca baujjhaṅgai ariyañ‍ca aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ sautāpattiphalañ‍ca sakadāgāmiphalañ‍ca anāgāmiphalañ‍ca arahattañ‍ca samāpaj‍jiṃ…pai॰… rāgau ca mai cattau… dausau ca mai cattau… mauhau ca mai cattau vantau muttau pahīnau paṭinissaṭṭhau ukkhaiṭitau samukkhaiṭitau| rāgā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ… dausā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti vattukāmau mauhā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa  paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| [ ] aitthantarai pāṭhā syāmapautthakai natthi

219. tīhākāraihi dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ suñ‍ñatañ‍ca vimaukkhaṃ animittañ‍ca vimaukkhaṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca vimaukkhaṃ suñ‍ñatañ‍ca samādhiṃ animittañ‍ca samādhiṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca samādhiṃ suñ‍ñatañ‍ca samāpattiṃ animittañ‍ca samāpattiṃ appaṇihitañ‍ca samāpattiṃ tissau ca vij‍jā cattārau ca satipaṭṭhānai cattārau ca sammappadhānai cattārau ca iddhipādai pañ‍ca  ca indriyāni pañ‍ca ca balāni satta ca baujjhaṅgai ariyañ‍ca aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ  sautāpattiphalañ‍ca sakadāgāmiphalañ‍ca anāgāmiphalañ‍ca arahattañ‍ca samāpaj‍jiṃ…pai॰… rāgau ca mai cattau… dausau ca mai cattau… mauhau ca mai cattau vantau muttau pahīnau paṭinissaṭṭhau ukkhaiṭitau samukkhaiṭitau| rāgā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ… dausā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ… mauhā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti vattukāmau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jinti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

tīhākāraihi tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ…pai॰… mauhā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jinti vattukāmau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jinti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi mauhā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ…pai॰… rāgā ca mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti vattukāmau dausā mai cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti pārājikassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| pubbaivassa hauti musā bhaṇissanti, bhaṇantassa hauti musā bhaṇāmīti, bhaṇitassa hauti musā mayā bhaṇitanti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

vatthuvisārakassa sabbamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

vatthuvisārakassa cak‍kapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

vatthukāmavārakathā niṭṭhitā|

220. tīhākāraihi  yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji… samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

catūhākāraihi … pañ‍cahākāraihi… chahākāraihi… sattahākāraihi yau tai vihārai vasi sau  bhikkhu paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji… samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pubbaivassa hauti musā bhaṇissanti, bhaṇantassa hauti musā bhaṇāmīti, bhaṇitassa hauti musā mayā bhaṇitanti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

tīhākāraihi yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ… suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhaṃ… animittaṃ vimaukkhaṃ… appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhaṃ… suñ‍ñataṃ samādhiṃ… animittaṃ samādhiṃ… appaṇihitaṃ samādhiṃ… suñ‍ñataṃ samāpattiṃ… animittaṃ samāpattiṃ… appaṇihitaṃ samāpattiṃ… tissau vij‍jā… cattārau satipaṭṭhānai… cattārau sammappadhānai… cattārau iddhipādai… pañ‍ca indriyāni… pañ‍ca balāni… satta baujjhaṅgai… ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ… sautāpattiphalaṃ… sakadāgāmiphalaṃ… anāgāmiphalaṃ… arahattaṃ samāpaj‍ji… samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu arahattassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā arahattaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tīhākāraihi yau tai vihārai vasi, tassa bhikkhunau rāgau cattau… dausau cattau… mauhau cattau  vantau muttau pahīnau paṭinissaṭṭhau ukkhaiṭitau samukkhaiṭitauti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi yau tai vihārai vasi, tassa bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ… dausā cittaṃ vinīvaraṇaṃ… mauhā cittaṃ vinīvaraṇanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… pubbaivassa hauti musā bhaṇissanti, bhaṇantassa hauti musā bhaṇāmīti, bhaṇitassa hauti musā mayā bhaṇitanti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi yau tai vihārai vasi sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji … samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa  āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pubbaivassa hauti musā bhaṇissanti, bhaṇantassa hauti musā bhaṇāmīti bhaṇitassa hauti, musā mayā bhaṇitanti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

yathā idaṃ vitthāritaṃ aivamaiva saisānipi vitthāraitabbāni|

221. tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi yau tai cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍ji… yau tai piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍ji… yau tai saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍ji… yau tai gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍ji sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji… samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā  bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

tīhākāraihi…pai॰… sattahākāraihi yaina tai vihārau paribhuttau… yaina tai cīvaraṃ paribhuttaṃ… yaina tai piṇḍapātau paribhuttau… yaina tai saināsanaṃ paribhuttaṃ… yaina tai gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārau paribhuttau… sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji… samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

tīhākāraihi  …pai॰… sattahākāraihi yaṃ tvaṃ āgamma vihāraṃ adāsi… cīvaraṃ adāsi… piṇḍapātaṃ adāsi… saināsanaṃ adāsi… gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ adāsi sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji… samāpaj‍jati… samāpan‍nau… sau bhikkhu suñ‍ñāgārai catutthassa jhānassa lābhī… vasī… taina bhikkhunā suñ‍ñāgārai catutthaṃ jhānaṃ sacchikatanti sampajānamusā bhaṇantassa paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| pubbaivassa hauti  musā bhaṇissanti, bhaṇantassa hauti musā bhaṇāmīti, bhaṇitassa hauti musā mayā bhaṇitanti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ|

paiyyālapan‍narasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pac‍cayappaṭisaṃyuttavārakathā niṭṭhitā|

uttarimanussadhammacak‍kapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

222. anāpatti adhimānaina, anul‍lapanādhippāyassa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

adhimānai  adhimānaina (pī॰) arañ‍ñamhi, piṇḍaupajjhāriyāpathau|

saṃyaujanā rahaudhammā, vihārau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau||

na duk‍karaṃ vīriyamathaupi mac‍cunau|

bhāyāvusau vippaṭisāri sammā|

viriyaina yaugaina ārādhanāya|

atha vaidanāya adhivāsanā duvai||

brāhmaṇai pañ‍ca vatthūni, añ‍ñaṃ byākaraṇā tayau|

agārāvaraṇā kāmā, rati cāpi apak‍kami||

aṭṭhi paisi ubhau gāvaghātakā|

piṇḍau sākuṇikau nicchavi aurabbhi|

asi ca sūkarikau satti māgavi|

usu ca kāraṇikau sūci sārathi||

yau ca sibbīyati sūcakau hi sau|

aṇḍabhāri ahu gāmakūṭakau|

kūpai nimuggau hi sau pāradārikau|

gūthakhādī ahu duṭṭhabrāhmaṇau||

nicchavitthī  aticārinī ahu|

maṅgulitthī ahu ikkhaṇitthikā|

aukilinī  hi sapattaṅgāraukiri|

sīsacchin‍nau ahu cauraghātakau||

bhikkhu bhikkhunī sikkhamānā|

sāmaṇairau atha sāmaṇairikā|

kassapassa vinayasmiṃ pabbajaṃ|

pāpakammamakariṃsu tāvadai||

tapaudā rājagahai yuddhaṃ, nāgānaugāhanaina ca|

saubhitau arahaṃ bhikkhu, pañ‍cakappasataṃ saraiti||

vinītavatthu

223. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, adhimānainā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṇidhāya arañ‍ñai  viharati – ‘‘aivaṃ maṃ janau sambhāvaissatī’’ti| taṃ janau sambhāvaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, paṇidhāya arañ‍ñai vatthabbaṃ| yau vasaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṇidhāya piṇḍāya carati – ‘‘aivaṃ maṃ janau sambhāvaissatī’’ti| taṃ janau sambhāvaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, paṇidhāya piṇḍāya caritabbaṃ| yau caraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yai, āvusau, amhākaṃ upajjhāyassa saddhivihārikā sabbaiva arahantau’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘ul‍lapanādhippāyau  ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yai, āvusau, amhākaṃ upajjhāyassa antaivāsikā sabbaiva mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘ul‍lapanādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṇidhāya caṅkamati… paṇidhāya tiṭṭhati… paṇidhāya nisīdati… paṇidhāya saiyyaṃ kappaiti – ‘‘aivaṃ maṃ janau sambhāvaissatī’’ti| taṃ janau sambhāvaisi| tassa  kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, paṇidhāya saiyyā kappaitabbā| yau kappaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘mayhampi, āvusau, saṃyaujanā pahīnā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

224. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu rahaugatau uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati| paracittavidū bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ apasādaisi – ‘‘mā, āvusau, aivarūpaṃ abhaṇi| natthaisau tuyha’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau  bhikkhu rahaugatau uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati| daivatā taṃ bhikkhuṃ apasādaisi – ‘‘mā, bhantai, aivarūpaṃ abhaṇi| natthaisau tuyha’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yau, āvusau, tuyhaṃ vihārai vasati sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti| sau ca tassa vihārai vasati| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘ul‍lapanādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, upaṭṭhaisi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti| sau ca taṃ upaṭṭhaiti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘ul‍lapanādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

225. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthāyasmatau uttarimanussadhammau’’ti? ‘‘nāvusau, duk‍karaṃ añ‍ñaṃ byākātu’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘yai khau tai bhagavatau sāvakā tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ| ahañ‍camhi na bhagavatau sāvakau| kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘anul‍lapanādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthāyasmatau uttarimanussadhammau’’ti? ‘‘ārādhanīyau khau, āvusau, dhammau āraddhavīriyainā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā khau, āvusau, bhāyī’’ti| nāhaṃ, āvusau, mac‍cunau bhāyāmī’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā khau, āvusau, bhāyī’’ti| ‘‘yau nūnāvusau, vippaṭisārī assa sau bhāyaiyyā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina  añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthāyasmatau uttarimanussadhammau’’ti? ‘‘ārādhanīyau khau, āvusau, dhammau sammāpayuttainā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu  gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthāyasmatau uttarimanussadhammau’’ti? ‘‘ārādhanīyau khau, āvusau, dhammau āraddhavīriyainā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthāyasmatau uttarimanussadhammau’’ti? ‘‘ārādhanīyau khau, āvusau, dhammau yuttayaugainā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍cāvusau, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, sak‍kā yaina vā taina vā adhivāsaitu’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍cāvusau khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīya’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, sak‍kā puthuj‍janaina adhivāsaitu’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘ul‍lapanādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, pārājikassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

226. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau bhikkhū nimantaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘āyantu, bhauntau arahantau’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘mayañ‍camha na arahantau anarahantau (sī॰)| ayañ‍ca brāhmaṇau amhai  arahantavādaina samudācarati| kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, pasādabhañ‍ñai’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau bhikkhū nimantaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘nisīdantu, bhauntau arahantau’’ti… ‘‘bhuñ‍jantu, bhauntau arahantau’’ti… ‘‘tappaintu, bhauntau arahantau’’ti… ‘‘gacchantu, bhauntau arahantau’’ti| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘mayañ‍camha na arahantau| ayañ‍ca brāhmaṇau amhai arahantavādaina samudācarati| kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhavai, pasādabhañ‍ñai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘mayhampi, āvusau, āsavā pahīnā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau  pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘mayhampi, āvusau, aitai dhammā saṃvij‍jantī’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘ahampāvusau, taisu dhammaisu sandissāmī’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ñātakā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, agāraṃ ajjhāvasā’’ti| ‘‘abhabbau khau, āvusau, mādisau agāraṃ ajjhāvasitu’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

227. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ñātakā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, kāmai paribhuñ‍jā’’ti| ‘‘āvaṭā mai, āvusau, kāmā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ñātakā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhiramasi, bhantai’’ti? ‘‘abhiratau ahaṃ, āvusau, paramāya abhiratiyā’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi| ‘‘yai khau tai bhagavatau sāvakā tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ! ahañ‍camhi na bhagavatau sāvakau| kac‍ci  nu khau ahaṃ pārājikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘anul‍lapanādhippāyau ahaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, anul‍lapanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū katikaṃ katvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu – ‘‘yau imamhā āvāsā paṭhamaṃ pak‍kamissati taṃ mayaṃ arahāti jānissāmā’’ti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu  – ‘‘maṃ arahāti jānantū’’ti, tamhā āvāsā paṭhamaṃ pak‍kāmi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau pārājika’’nti|

228. imāni vatthuni saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.202 āgatāni taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca lakkhaṇau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai viharanti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaraṃ ādāya yaināyasmā lakkhaṇau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāmāvusau lakkhaṇa, rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā lakkhaṇau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau gijjhakūṭā pabbatā  aurauhantau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai sitaṃ pātvākāsi| atha khau āyasmā lakkhaṇau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, haitu kau pac‍cayau sitassa pātukammāyā’’ti? ‘‘akālau khau, āvusau lakkhaṇa, aitassa pañhassa pañhassa byākaraṇāya (syā॰)| bhagavatau maṃ santikai aitaṃ pañhaṃ pucchāti| atha khau āyasmā ca lakkhaṇau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau  rājagahai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā lakkhaṇau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai sitaṃ pātvākāsi| kau nu khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, haitu kau pac‍cayau sitassa pātukammāyā’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā phāsuḷantarikāhi vituḍainti vitudainti vitacchainti virājainti (syā॰)| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, aivarūpaupi nāma sattau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi  nāma  yakkhau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma attabhāvappaṭilābhau bhavissatī’’’ti! bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘uttarimanussadhammaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau ul‍lapatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi  – ‘‘cakkhubhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti| ñāṇabhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti| yatra hi nāma sāvakau aivarūpaṃ ñassati vā dakkhati vā sakkhiṃ vā karissati| pubbaiva mai sau, bhikkhavai, sattau diṭṭhau ahausi| api cāhaṃ na byākāsiṃ| ahañ‍caitaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ parai ca mai na saddahaiyyuṃ| yai mai na saddahaiyyuṃ taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai gaughātakau ahausi| sau tassa kammassa vipākaina bahūni vassāni bahūni vassasatāni bahūni vassasahassāni bahūni vassasatasahassāni nirayai pac‍citvā tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisaina aivarūpaṃ attabhāvappaṭilābhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sac‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau āha| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānassā’’ti|

229. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ maṃsapaisiṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti vitudainti vitacchainti virājainti (syā॰)| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ  karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai gaughātakau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ maṃsapiṇḍaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sākuṇikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ nicchaviṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰…  aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai aurabbhikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ asilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tai asī uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sūkarikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sattilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tā sattiyau uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai māgavikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ usulaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tai usū uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai kāraṇikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sūcilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tā sūciyau uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva  kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sārathikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sūcilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tā sūciyau sīsai pavisitvā mukhatau nikkhamanti; mukhai pavisitvā uratau nikkhamanti; urai pavisitvā udaratau nikkhamanti; udarai pavisitvā ūrūhi nikkhamanti; ūrūsu pavisitvā jaṅghāhi nikkhamanti; jaṅghāsu pavisitvā pādaihi nikkhamanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sūcakau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ kumbhaṇḍaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| sau gacchantaupi taiva aṇḍai khandhai āraupaitvā gacchati, nisīdantaupi taisvaiva aṇḍaisu nisīdati| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi  kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai gāmakūṭau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ purisaṃ gūthakūpai sasīsakaṃ nimuggaṃ…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai pāradārikau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ  purisaṃ gūthakūpai sasīsakaṃ nimuggaṃ ubhauhi hatthaihi gūthaṃ khādantaṃ…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai duṭṭhabrāhmaṇau  ahausi| sau kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa pāvacanai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhattaina nimantaitvā dauṇiyau dauṇiyau (itipi) gūthassa pūrāpaitvā kālaṃ āraucāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘atau itau (syā॰) ahau (itipi), bhauntau, yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jantu caiva harantu cā’’’ti…pai॰…|

230. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ nicchaviṃ itthiṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisā, bhikkhavai, itthī imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai aticārinī ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ itthiṃ duggandhaṃ maṅguliṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisā, bhikkhavai, itthī imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai ikkhaṇikā ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ itthiṃ uppak‍kaṃ aukiliniṃ aukiriniṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisā, bhikkhavai, itthī kāliṅgassa rañ‍ñau aggamahaisī ahausi| sā issāpakatā sapattiṃ aṅgārakaṭāhaina aukiri…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ asīsakaṃ kabandhaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa urai akkhīni caiva haunti mukhañ‍ca| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti vibhaj‍jainti| sau  sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai hārikau nāma cauraghātakau ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ bhikkhuṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa saṅghāṭipi ādittā sampaj‍jalitā sajautibhūtā, pattaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau, kāyabandhanampi ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ, kāyaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa pāvacanai pāpabhikkhu ahausi…pai॰…|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ bhikkhuniṃ… addasaṃ sikkhamānaṃ… addasaṃ sāmaṇairaṃ… addasaṃ sāmaṇairiṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tassā saṅghāṭipi ādittā sampaj‍jalitā sajautibhūtā, pattaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau, kāyabandhanampi ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ, kāyaupi ādittau  sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, aivarūpaupi nāma sattau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma yakkhau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma attabhāvappaṭilābhau bhavissatī’’’ti! bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘uttarimanussadhammaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau ul‍lapatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘cakkhubhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti| ñāṇabhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti| yatra hi nāma sāvakau aivarūpaṃ ñassati vā dakkhati vā sakkhiṃ vā karissati! pubbaiva mai sā, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairī diṭṭhā ahausi| api cāhaṃ na byākāsiṃ| ahañ‍caitaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ parai ca mai na saddahaiyyuṃ| yai mai na saddahaiyyuṃ taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| aisā, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairī kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa pāvacanai pāpasāmaṇairī ahausi| sā tassa kammassa vipākaina bahūni vassāni bahūni vassasatāni bahūni vassasahassāni bahūni vassasatasahassāni nirayai pac‍citvā tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisaina aivarūpaṃ attabhāvappaṭilābhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sac‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau āha| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānassā’’ti|

231. atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yatāyaṃ, āvusau, tapaudā sandati sau dahau acchaudakau sītaudakau sātaudakau saitakau suppatitthau ramaṇīyau pahūtamacchakacchapau cak‍kamattāni ca padumāni pupphantī’’ti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘yatāyaṃ, āvusau, tapaudā sandati sau dahau acchaudakau sītaudakau sātaudakau saitakau suppatitthau ramaṇīyau pahūtamacchakacchapau cak‍kamattāni  ca padumāni pupphantī’ti| atha ca panāyaṃ tapaudā kuthitā sandati| uttarimanussadhammaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau ul‍lapatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘yatāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, tapaudā sandati sau dahau acchaudakau sītaudakau sātaudakau saitakau suppatitthau ramaṇīyau pahūtamacchakacchapau cak‍kamattāni ca padumāni pupphanti| api cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, tapaudā dvin‍naṃ mahānirayānaṃ antarikāya āgacchati| taināyaṃ tapaudā kuthitā sandati| sac‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau āha| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau licchavīhi saddhiṃ saṅgāmaintau pabhaggau ahausi| atha rājā pacchā sainaṃ saṅkaḍḍhitvā licchavayau parājaisi| saṅgāmai ca nandi carati – ‘‘rañ‍ñā licchavī pabhaggā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘rājā, āvusau, licchavīhi pabhaggau’’ti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘rājā, āvusau, licchavīhi pabhaggau’ti! saṅgāmai ca nandiṃ carati – ‘rañ‍ñā licchavī pabhaggā’ti! uttarimanussadhammaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau ul‍lapatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘paṭhamaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā licchavīhi pabhaggau| atha  rājā pacchā sainaṃ saṅkaḍḍhitvā licchavayau parājaisi| sac‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau āha| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānassā’’ti|

232. atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sappinikāya nadiyā tīrai ānaiñ‍jaṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nau nāgānaṃ augayha uttarantānaṃ kauñ‍caṃ karauntānaṃ saddaṃ assausi’’nti| bhikkhū  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau ānaiñ‍jaṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nau saddaṃ saussati! uttarimanussadhammaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau ul‍lapatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘atthaisau, bhikkhavai, samādhi sau ca khau aparisuddhau| sac‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau āha| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānassā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā saubhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍ca kappasatāni  anussarāmī’’ti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā saubhitau aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍ca kappasatāni anussarāmī’ti! uttarimanussadhammaṃ āyasmā saubhitau ul‍lapatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘atthaisā, bhikkhavai, saubhitassa| sā ca khau aikāyaiva jāti| sac‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, saubhitau āha| anāpatti, bhikkhavai, saubhitassāti|

catutthapārājikaṃ samattaṃ|

233. uddiṭṭhā khau āyasmantau cattārau pārājikā dhammā, yaisaṃ bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā āpaj‍jitvā na labhati bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ, yathā purai tathā pacchā, pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’’? parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

pārājikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

maithunādin‍nādānañ‍ca, manussaviggahuttari|

pārājikāni cattāri, chaij‍javatthū asaṃsayāti||

pārājikakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ

1. suk‍kavissaṭṭhisikkhāpadaṃ

imai  khau panāyasmantau tairasa saṅghādisaisā

dhammā uddaisaṃ āgacchanti|

234. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā saiyyasakau anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carati| sau taina kisau hauti lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| addasa khau āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ kisaṃ lūkhaṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātaṃ dhamanisanthatagattaṃ| disvāna āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau saiyyasaka, kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau? kac‍ci nau tvaṃ, āvusau saiyyasaka, anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carasī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, āvusau saiyyasaka, yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍ja yāvadatthaṃ supa yāvadatthaṃ nhāya| yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā yāvadatthaṃ supitvā yāvadatthaṃ nhāyitvā yadā tai anabhirati uppaj‍jati rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti tadā hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, kappati aivarūpaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘āma, āvusau| ahampi aivaṃ karaumī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā saiyyasakau yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍ji yāvadatthaṃ supi yāvadatthaṃ nhāyi| yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā yāvadatthaṃ supitvā yāvadatthaṃ nhāyitvā yadā anabhirati uppaj‍jati rāgau cittaṃ  anuddhaṃsaiti tadā hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisi| atha khau āyasmā saiyyasakau aparaina samayaina vaṇṇavā ahausi pīṇindriyau pasan‍namukhavaṇṇau vippasan‍nachavivaṇṇau| atha khau āyasmatau saiyyasakassa sahāyakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pubbai khau tvaṃ, āvusau saiyyasaka, kisau ahausi lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| sau dāni tvaṃ aitarahi vaṇṇavā pīṇindriyau pasan‍namukhavaṇṇau  vippasan‍nachavivaṇṇau| kiṃ nu khau tvaṃ, āvusau saiyyasaka, bhaisaj‍jaṃ karausī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhaisaj‍jaṃ karaumi| apicāhaṃ yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi yāvadatthaṃ supāmi yāvadatthaṃ nhāyāmi| yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā yāvadatthaṃ supitvā yāvadatthaṃ nhāyitvā yadā mai anabhirati uppaj‍jati rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti tadā hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaimī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau saiyyasaka, yainaiva hatthaina saddhādaiyyaṃ bhuñ‍jasi tainaiva hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā saiyyasakau hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaissatī’’ti!

atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, saiyyasaka, hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaissasi! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau nau sarāgāya, visañ‍ñaugāya dhammau daisitau nau sañ‍ñaugāya, anupādānāya dhammau daisitau nau saupādānāya! tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā virāgāya dhammai daisitai sarāgāya caitaissasi, visañ‍ñaugāya dhammai daisitai sañ‍ñaugāya caitaissasi, anupādānāya dhammai daisitai saupādānāya caitaissasi! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina rāgavirāgāya dhammau daisitau, madanimmadanāya pipāsavinayāya ālayasamugghātāya vaṭṭupacchaidāya taṇhakkhayāya virāgāya niraudhāya nibbānāya dhammau daisitau? nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina kāmānaṃ pahānaṃ akkhātaṃ, kāmasañ‍ñānaṃ pariñ‍ñā akkhātā, kāmapipāsānaṃ paṭivinayau akkhātau, kāmavitak‍kānaṃ samugghātau akkhātau, kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau? naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya| atha khvaitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya, pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ  anaikapariyāyaina  vigarahitvā dubbharatāya…pai॰… ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

‘‘sañ‍caitanikā suk‍kavissaṭṭhi visaṭṭhi (sī॰ syā॰) saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

235. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū paṇītabhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā muṭṭhassatī asampajānā niddaṃ auk‍kamanti| taisaṃ muṭṭhassatīnaṃ asampajānānaṃ niddaṃ auk‍kamantānaṃ supinantaina asuci muc‍cati| taisaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘sañ‍caitanikā suk‍kavissaṭṭhi saṅghādisaisau’ti| amhākañ‍ca supinantaina asuci muc‍cati| atthi caittha caitanā labbhati| kac‍ci nu khau mayaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘atthaisā, bhikkhavai, caitanā; sā ca khau abbauhārikāti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

236. ‘‘sañ‍caitanikā suk‍kavissaṭṭhi añ‍ñatra supinantā saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

237. sañ‍caitanikāti jānantau sañ‍jānantau caic‍ca abhivitaritvā vītik‍kamau|

suk‍kanti dasa suk‍kāni – nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lauhitakaṃ audātaṃ tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ dakavaṇṇaṃ tailavaṇṇaṃ khīravaṇṇaṃ dadhivaṇṇaṃ sappivaṇṇaṃ|

vissaṭṭhīti ṭhānatau cāvanā vuc‍cati vissaṭṭhīti|

añ‍ñatra supinantāti ṭhapaitvā supinantaṃ|

saṅghādisaisauti saṅghauva tassā āpattiyā parivāsaṃ daiti, mūlāya paṭikassati, mānattaṃ daiti, abbhaiti; na sambahulā, na aikapuggalau| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti| tassaiva āpattinikāyassa nāmakammaṃ adhivacanaṃ| tainapi vuc‍cati – ‘‘saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

ajjhattarūpai  maucaiti, bahiddhārūpai maucaiti, ajjhattabahiddhārūpai maucaiti, ākāsai kaṭiṃ kampaintau maucaiti; rāgūpatthambhai maucaiti, vac‍cūpatthambhai maucaiti, passāvūpatthambhai maucaiti, vātūpatthambhai maucaiti, uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakadaṭṭhūpatthambhai maucaiti; āraugyatthāya maucaiti, sukhatthāya maucaiti, bhaisaj‍jatthāya maucaiti, dānatthāya maucaiti, puñ‍ñatthāya  maucaiti, yañ‍ñatthāya maucaiti, saggatthāya maucaiti, bījatthāya maucaiti, vīmaṃsatthāya maucaiti, davatthāya maucaiti; nīlaṃ maucaiti, pītakaṃ maucaiti, lauhitakaṃ maucaiti, audātaṃ maucaiti, tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ maucaiti, dakavaṇṇaṃ maucaiti, tailavaṇṇaṃ maucaiti, khīravaṇṇaṃ maucaiti, dadhivaṇṇaṃ maucaiti, sappivaṇṇaṃ maucaiti|

238. ajjhattarūpaiti ajjhattaṃ upādin‍nai upādiṇṇai (sī॰ ka॰) rūpai|

bahiddhārūpaiti bahiddhā upādin‍nai vā anupādin‍nai vā|

ajjhattabahiddhārūpaiti tadubhayai|

ākāsai kaṭiṃ kampaintauti ākāsai vāyamantassa aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti|

rāgūpatthambhaiti rāgaina pīḷitassa aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti|

vac‍cūpatthambhaiti vac‍caina pīḷitassa aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti|

passāvūpatthambhaiti passāvaina pīḷitassa aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti|

vātūpatthambhaiti vātaina pīḷitassa aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti|

uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakadaṭṭhūpatthambhaiti uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakadaṭṭhaina aṅgajātaṃ kammaniyaṃ hauti|

239. āraugyatthāyāti  araugau bhavissāmi|

sukhatthāyāti sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ uppādaissāmi|

bhaisaj‍jatthāyāti bhaisaj‍jaṃ bhavissati|

dānatthāyāti  dānaṃ dassāmi|

puñ‍ñatthāyāti puñ‍ñaṃ bhavissati|

yañ‍ñatthāyāti yañ‍ñaṃ yajissāmi|

saggatthāyāti saggaṃ gamissāmi|

bījatthāyāti bījaṃ bhavissati|

vīmaṃsatthāyāti  nīlaṃ bhavissati, pītakaṃ bhavissati, lauhitakaṃ bhavissati, audātaṃ bhavissati, tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ bhavissati, dakavaṇṇaṃ bhavissati, tailavaṇṇaṃ bhavissati, khīravaṇṇaṃ bhavissati, dadhivaṇṇaṃ bhavissati, sappivaṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti|

davatthāyāti khiḍḍādhippāyau|

240. ajjhattarūpai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bahiddhārūpai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ajjhattabahiddhārūpai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ākāsai kaṭiṃ kampaintau caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

rāgūpatthambhai  caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

vac‍cūpatthambhai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

passāvūpatthambhai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

vātūpatthambhai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

uc‍cāliṅgapāṇakadaṭṭhūpatthambhai caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

āraugyatthāya caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sukhatthāya…pai॰… bhaisaj‍jatthāya… dānatthāya… puñ‍ñatthāya… yañ‍ñatthāya… saggatthāya… bījatthāya… vīmaṃsatthāya… davatthāya caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nīlaṃ caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

suddhikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

āraugyatthañ‍ca  sukhatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

āraugyatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca…pai॰… āraugyatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca… āraugyatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… āraugyatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… āraugyatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… āraugyatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… āraugyatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca… āraugyatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakassa khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

241. sukhatthañ‍ca  bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sukhatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca…pai॰… sukhatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… sukhatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… sukhatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… sukhatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… sukhatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca… sukhatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sukhatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

242. bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca  dānatthañ‍ca…pai॰… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dānatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca…pai॰… dānatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… dānatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… dānatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… dānatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca… dānatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dānatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… dānatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… dānatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca…pai॰… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca  āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca… puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca  saggatthañ‍ca…pai॰… yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca … yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca… yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca… yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saggatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca…pai॰… saggatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca… saggatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saggatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… saggatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… saggatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca… saggatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca… saggatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… saggatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bījatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca…pai॰… bījatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bījatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… bījatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… bījatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca… bījatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca… bījatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… bījatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… bījatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

davatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca…pai॰…  davatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca… davatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakassa baddhacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

āraugyatthañ‍ca  sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰… āraugyatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakassa khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰… sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca…pai॰… sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakassa baddhacak‍kaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca āraugyatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

timūlakampi  catumūlakampi pañ‍camūlakampi chamūlakampi sattamūlakampi aṭṭhamūlakampi navamūlakampi aivamaiva vitthāraitabbaṃ|

idaṃ sabbamūlakaṃ|

243. āraugyatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sabbamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

244. nīlañ‍ca  pītakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nīlañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca…pai॰… nīlañ‍ca audātañ‍ca… nīlañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca… nīlañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… nīlañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… nīlañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca… nīlañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… nīlañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍catti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakassa khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

245. pītakañ‍ca  lauhitakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

pītakañ‍ca audātañ‍ca…pai॰… pītakañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca… pītakañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… pītakañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… pītakañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca… pītakañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… pītakañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

pītakañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti  saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakassa baddhacak‍kaṃ|

246. lauhitakañ‍ca audātañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

lauhitakañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… lauhitakañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… lauhitakañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… lauhitakañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca… lauhitakañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… lauhitakañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

lauhitakañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… lauhitakañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

audātañ‍ca  tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… audātañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… audātañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… audātañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca… audātañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… audātañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

audātañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… audātañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca… audātañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca… tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca audātañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dakavaṇṇañ‍ca  tailavaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dakavaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca audātañ‍ca… dakavaṇṇañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tailavaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tailavaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca audātañ‍ca… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca… tailavaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khīravaṇṇañ‍ca  dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khīravaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca audātañ‍ca… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… khīravaṇṇañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca…pai॰… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca audātañ‍ca… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sappivaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sappivaṇṇañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca…pai॰… sappivaṇṇañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca… sappivaṇṇañ‍ca audātañ‍ca… sappivaṇṇañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca… sappivaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca… sappivaṇṇañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca… sappivaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca … sappivaṇṇañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakassa baddhacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

nīlañ‍ca  pītakañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰… nīlañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakassa khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

pītakañ‍ca  lauhitakañ‍ca audātañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰… pītakañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca…pai॰… pītakañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakassa baddhacak‍kaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰… dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

timūlakampi catumūlakampi pañ‍camūlakampi chamūlakampi sattamūlakampi aṭṭhamūlakampi navamūlakampi aivamaiva vitthāraitabbaṃ|

idaṃ sabbamūlakaṃ|

247. nīlañ‍ca  pītakañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca audātañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca khīravaṇṇañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sabbamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

248. āraugyatthañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca āraugyatthaṃ nīlaṃ (?) caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

āraugyatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

āraugyatthañ‍ca  sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ubhatau  vaḍḍhakaṃ aivamaiva vaḍḍhaitabbaṃ|

āraugyatthañ‍ca  sukhatthañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jatthañ‍ca dānatthañ‍ca puñ‍ñatthañ‍ca yañ‍ñatthañ‍ca saggatthañ‍ca bījatthañ‍ca vīmaṃsatthañ‍ca davatthañ‍ca nīlañ‍ca pītakañ‍ca lauhitakañ‍ca audātañ‍ca tak‍kavaṇṇañ‍ca dakavaṇṇañ‍ca tailavaṇṇañ‍ca  khīravaṇṇañ‍ca dadhivaṇṇañ‍ca sappivaṇṇañ‍ca caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

missakacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

249. nīlaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati pītakaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nīlaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati lauhitakaṃ…pai॰… audātaṃ … tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

250. pītakaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati lauhitakaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

pītakaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati audātaṃ…pai॰… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīlaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

baddhacak‍kamūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

251. sappivaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati nīlaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sappivaṇṇaṃ  maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati pītakaṃ…pai॰… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ … tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kucchicak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

252. pītakaṃ  maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati nīlaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

lauhitakaṃ…pai॰… audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati nīlaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa paṭhamaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

253. lauhitakaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati pītakaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

audātaṃ…pai॰… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ

… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīlaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati pītakaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa dutiyaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

254. audātaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati lauhitakaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ…pai॰… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ…

sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīlaṃ  … pītakaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati lauhitakaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa tatiyaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

255. tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati audātaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dakavaṇṇaṃ…pai॰… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīlaṃ… pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati audātaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa catutthaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

256. dakavaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tailavaṇṇaṃ  …pai॰… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīla…

pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa pañ‍camaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

257. tailavaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati dakavaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khīravaṇṇaṃ…pai॰… dadhivaṇṇaṃ… sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīlaṃ … pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ…

audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati dakavaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa chaṭṭhaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

258. khīravaṇṇaṃ  maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati tailavaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dadhivaṇṇaṃ…pai॰… sappivaṇṇaṃ… nīlaṃ… pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati tailavaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa sattamaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

259. dadhivaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati khīravaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

sappivaṇṇaṃ…pai॰… nīlaṃ… pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati khīravaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa aṭṭhamaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

260. sappivaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati dadhivaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nīlaṃ…pai॰… pītakaṃ… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ… tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati dadhivaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa navamaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

261. nīlaṃ  maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati sappivaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

pītakaṃ  …pai॰… lauhitakaṃ… audātaṃ… tak‍kavaṇṇaṃ… dakavaṇṇaṃ … tailavaṇṇaṃ… khīravaṇṇaṃ… dadhivaṇṇaṃ maucaissāmīti caitaiti upak‍kamati sappivaṇṇaṃ muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

piṭṭhicak‍kassa dasamaṃ gamanaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

piṭṭhicak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

262. caitaiti  upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

caitaiti upak‍kamati na muc‍cati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

caitaiti na upak‍kamati muc‍cati, anāpatti|

caitaiti na upak‍kamati na muc‍cati, anāpatti|

na caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, anāpatti|

na caitaiti upak‍kamati na muc‍cati, anāpatti|

na caitaiti na upak‍kamati muc‍cati, anāpatti|

na caitaiti na upak‍kamati na muc‍cati, anāpatti|

anāpatti supinantaina, namaucanādhippāyassa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

supinauc‍cārapassāvau, vitak‍kuṇhaudakaina ca|

bhaisaj‍jaṃ kaṇḍuvaṃ maggau, vatthi jantāgharupak‍kamau jantaggupak‍kamau (sī॰), jantāgharaṃ ūru (syā॰)||

sāmaṇairau ca suttau ca, ūru muṭṭhinā pīḷayi|

ākāsai thambhaṃ nijjhāyi, chiddaṃ kaṭṭhaina ghaṭṭayi||

sautau  udañ‍jalaṃ dhāvaṃ, pupphāvaliyaṃ paukkharaṃ|

vālikā kaddamussaikau kaddamausaikau (?), sayanaṅguṭṭhakaina cāti||

vinītavatthu

263. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau supinantaina asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, supinantainā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau uc‍cāraṃ karauntassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, maucanādhippāyau’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau passāvaṃ karauntassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kāmavitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaintassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, vitak‍kaintassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau uṇhaudakaina nhāyantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa  kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘kiṃcittau  tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, maucanādhippāyau’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa uṇhaudakaina nhāyantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa uṇhaudakaina nhāyantassa asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aṅgajātai vaṇau hauti| bhaisaj‍jaina tassa bhaisaj‍jaina (?) ālimpaintassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aṅgajātai vaṇau hauti| maucanādhippāyassa tassa maucanāmippāyassa (syā॰) bhaisaj‍jaina ālimpaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aṇḍaṃ kaṇḍuvantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa aṇḍaṃ kaṇḍuvantassa kaṇḍūvantassa (sī॰) asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

264. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maggaṃ gacchantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa maggaṃ gacchantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau vatthiṃ gahaitvā passāvaṃ karauntassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa vatthiṃ gahaitvā passāvaṃ karauntassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau jantāgharai udaravaṭṭiṃ tāpaintassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana  samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa jantāgharai udaravaṭṭiṃ tāpaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau jantāgharai upajjhāyassa piṭṭhiparikammaṃ karauntassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa jantāgharai upajjhāyassa piṭṭhiparikammaṃ karauntassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

265. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau ūruṃ ghaṭṭāpaintassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa ūruṃ ghaṭṭāpaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu maucanādhippāyau añ‍ñataraṃ sāmaṇairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi mai tvaṃ, āvusau sāmaṇaira, aṅgajātaṃ gaṇhāhī’’ti| sau tassa aṅgajātaṃ aggahaisi| tassaiva asuci  muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu suttassa sāmaṇairassa aṅgajātaṃ aggahaisi| tassaiva asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi  …pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

266. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa ūrūhi aṅgajātaṃ pīḷaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa muṭṭhinā aṅgajātaṃ pīḷaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa ākāsai kaṭiṃ kampaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na  muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kāyaṃ thambhaintassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa kāyaṃ thambhaintassa asuci  muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau mātugāmassa aṅgajātaṃ upanijjhāyi| tassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, sārattaina mātugāmassa aṅgajātaṃ upanijjhāyitabbaṃ| yau upanijjhāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

267. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa tāḷacchiddaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa kaṭṭhaina aṅgajātaṃ ghaṭṭaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau paṭisautai nhāyantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa paṭisautai nhāyantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau udañ‍jalaṃ kīḷantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa udañ‍jalaṃ kīḷantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau udakai dhāvantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa udakai dhāvantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau pupphāvaliyaṃ pupphāvaḷiyaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) kīḷantassa  asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa pupphāvaliyaṃ kīḷantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

268. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau paukkharavanai dhāvantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa paukkharavanai dhāvantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa vālikaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa kaddamaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau udakaina aṅgajātaṃ  ausiñ‍cantassa asuci muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, namaucanādhippāyassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa udakaina aṅgajātaṃ ausiñ‍cantassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa sayanai aṅgajātaṃ ghaṭṭaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau maucanādhippāyassa aṅguṭṭhaina aṅgajātaṃ ghaṭṭaintassa asuci muc‍ci…pai॰… asuci na muc‍ci| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi  – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

suk‍kavissaṭṭhisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. kāyasaṃsaggasikkhāpadaṃ

269. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī arañ‍ñai viharati| tassāyasmatau vihārau abhirūpau hauti dassanīyau pāsādikau, majjhaigabbhau, samantāpariyāgārau, supañ‍ñattaṃ mañ‍capīṭhaṃ bhisibimbauhanaṃ, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ supaṭṭhitaṃ, parivaiṇaṃ susammaṭṭhaṃ| bahū manussā āyasmatau udāyissa vihārapaikkhakā āgacchanti| añ‍ñataraupi brāhmaṇau sapajāpatikau yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāma mayaṃ bhautau udāyissa  vihāraṃ paikkhitu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, paikkhassū’’ti, avāpuraṇaṃ apāpuraṇaṃ (syā॰) ādāya ghaṭikaṃ ugghāṭaitvā kavāṭaṃ paṇāmaitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| saupi khau brāhmaṇau āyasmatau udāyissa piṭṭhitau pāvisi| sāpi khau brāhmaṇī tassa brāhmaṇassa piṭṭhitau pāvisi| atha khau āyasmā udāyī aikac‍cai vātapānai vivarantau aikac‍cai vātapānai thakaintau gabbhaṃ anuparigantvā piṭṭhitau āgantvā tassā brāhmaṇiyā aṅgamaṅgāni parāmasi| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau āyasmatā udāyinā saddhiṃ paṭisammauditvā agamāsi| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau attamanau attamanavācaṃ nicchāraisi – ‘‘uḷārā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā yai imai  aivarūpai arañ‍ñai viharanti| bhavampi udāyī uḷārau yau aivarūpai arañ‍ñai viharatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai sā brāhmaṇī taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘kutau tassa uḷārattatā! yathaiva mai tvaṃ aṅgamaṅgāni parāmasi aivamaiva mai samaṇau udāyī aṅgamaṅgāni parāmasī’’ti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘alaj‍jinau imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dussīlā musāvādinau| imai hi nāma dhammacārinau samacārinau brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau  sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā paṭijānissanti! natthi imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ natthi imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, naṭṭhaṃ imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ naṭṭhaṃ imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, kutau imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ kutau imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, apagatā imai sāmañ‍ñā apagatā imai brahmañ‍ñā| kathañhi nāma samaṇau udāyī mama bhariyāya aṅgamaṅgāni parāmasissati! na hi sak‍kā kulitthīhi kuladhītāhi kulakumārīhi kulasuṇhāhi kuladāsīhi ārāmaṃ vā vihāraṃ vā gantuṃ| sacai sacai hi (syā॰) kulitthiyau kuladhītarau kuladhītāyau (sī॰ syā॰) kulakumāriyau kulasuṇhāyau kuladāsiyau ārāmaṃ vā vihāraṃ vā gacchaiyyuṃ, tāpi samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dūsaiyyu’’nti!

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa brāhmaṇassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ  pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ  kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jissasi! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau nau sarāgāya…pai॰… kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

270. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu autiṇṇau vipariṇataina cittaina mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya hatthaggāhaṃ vā vaiṇiggāhaṃ vā añ‍ñatarassa vā añ‍ñatarassa vā aṅgassa parāmasanaṃ, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

271. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

autiṇṇau  nāma sārattau apaikkhavā paṭibaddhacittau|

vipariṇatanti rattampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ| duṭṭhampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ| mūḷhampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ| apica, rattaṃ cittaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitaṃ vipariṇatanti|

mātugāmau nāma manussitthī, na yakkhī na paitī, na tiracchānagatā| antamasau tadahujātāpi dārikā, pagaiva mahattarī|

saddhinti aikatau|

kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  samāpaj‍jaiyyāti ajjhācārau vuc‍cati|

hatthau nāma kapparaṃ upādāyaṃ yāva agganakhā|

vaiṇī nāma suddhakaisā vā, suttamissā vā, mālāmissā vā, hirañ‍ñamissā vā, suvaṇṇamissā vā, muttāmissā vā, maṇimissā vā|

aṅgaṃ nāma hatthañ‍ca vaiṇiñ‍ca ṭhapaitvā avasaisaṃ aṅgaṃ nāma|

272. āmasanā, parāmasanā, aumasanā, ummasanā, aulaṅghanā, ul‍laṅghanā, ākaḍḍhanā, patikaḍḍhanā, abhiniggaṇhanā, abhinippīḷanā, gahaṇaṃ, chupanaṃ|

āmasanā nāma āmaṭṭhamattā|

parāmasanā nāma itaucitau ca saṃcaupanā|

aumasanā nāma haiṭṭhā auraupanā|

ummasanā nāma uddhaṃ uc‍cāraṇā|

aulaṅghanā nāma haiṭṭhā aunamanā|

ul‍laṅghanā nāma uddhaṃ uc‍cāraṇā|

ākaḍḍhanā nāma āviñchanā āviñ‍janā (sī॰ syā॰)|

patikaḍḍhanā nāma patippaṇāmanā|

abhiniggaṇhanā nāma aṅgaṃ gahaitvā nippīḷanā|

abhinippīḷanā nāma kainaci saha nippīḷanā|

gahaṇaṃ nāma gahitamattaṃ|

chupanaṃ nāma phuṭṭhamattaṃ|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

273. itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca bhikkhu ca| naṃ itthiyā kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati aumasati ummasati aulaṅghaiti ul‍laṅghaiti  ākaḍḍhati patikaḍḍhati abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷaiti gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

itthī ca hauti vaimatikau sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

itthī ca hauti paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

itthī ca hauti purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

itthī ca hauti tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

paṇḍakau ca hauti paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa  kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

paṇḍakau ca hauti vaimatikau sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

paṇḍakau ca hauti purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

paṇḍakau ca hauti tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

paṇḍakau  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

purisau ca hauti purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ purisassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

purisau ca hauti vaimatikau…pai॰… purisau ca hauti tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… purisau ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī… purisau ca hauti paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ purisassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tiracchānagatau ca hauti tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau  ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ tiracchānagatassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tiracchānagatau ca hauti vaimatikau…pai॰… tiracchānagatau ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatau ca hauti paṇḍakasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatau ca hauti| purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ tiracchānagatassa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

274. dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ vaimatikau sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ paṇḍakasañ‍ñī…pai॰… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau  ca| bhikkhu ca dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti  chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ|

dvai  paṇḍakā dvin‍naṃ paṇḍakānaṃ paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ paṇḍakānaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ|

dvai paṇḍakā dvin‍naṃ paṇḍakānaṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ paṇḍakānaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

dvai purisā dvin‍naṃ purisānaṃ purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ purisānaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

dvai purisā dvin‍naṃ purisānaṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… itthisañ‍ñī… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ purisānaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

dvai tiracchānagatā dvin‍naṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

dvai tiracchānagatā dvin‍naṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… itthisañ‍ñī… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī … purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

275. itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu  ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī  ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ vaimatikau sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ|

itthī  ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī ca purisau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī  ca purisau ca ubhin‍naṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī ca tiracchānagatau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa|

itthī ca tiracchānagatau ca ubhin‍naṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī… purisasañ‍ñī … tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

paṇḍakau  ca purisau ca ubhin‍naṃ paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

paṇḍakau ca purisau ca ubhin‍naṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

paṇḍakau ca tiracchānagatau ca ubhin‍naṃ paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa|

paṇḍakau  ca tiracchānagatau ca ubhin‍naṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti  chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

purisau ca tiracchānagatau ca ubhin‍naṃ purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

purisau ca tiracchānagatau ca ubhin‍naṃ vaimatikau…pai॰… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… itthisañ‍ñī… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

dumūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

276. itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ  āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ  …pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti  thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|
