dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ  duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

bhikkhupaiyyālau niṭṭhitau|

277. itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati aumasati ummasati aulaṅghaiti ul‍laṅghaiti ākaḍḍhati patikaḍḍhati abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷaiti  gaṇhāti chupati, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti aumasanti ummasanti aulaṅghainti ul‍laṅghainti ākaḍḍhanti patikaḍḍhanti abhiniggaṇhanti abhinippīḷainti gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī  ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰…|

dvai  itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

278. itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati parāmasati…pai॰… gaṇhāti chupati, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasanti parāmasanti…pai॰… gaṇhanti chupanti, saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti  dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasati| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina kāyaṃ āmasanti| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina  kāyaṃ āmasanti| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasanti| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasanti| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthī ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasati| saivanādhippāyau  kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| itthiyau ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasanti| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| ubhau ca naṃ bhikkhussa nissaggiyaina nissaggiyaṃ āmasanti| saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

279. saivanādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saivanādhippāyau  kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

saivanādhippāyau na ca kāyaina vāyamati, phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, anāpatti|

saivanādhippāyau na ca kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, anāpatti|

maukkhādhippāyau kāyaina vāyamati, phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, anāpatti|

maukkhādhippāyau  kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, anāpatti|

maukkhādhippāyau na ca kāyaina vāyamati, phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, anāpatti|

maukkhādhippāyau na ca kāyaina vāyamati, na ca phassaṃ paṭivijānāti, anāpatti|

280. anāpatti asañ‍cic‍ca, asatiyā, ajānantassa, asādiyantassa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

mātā  dhītā bhaginī ca, jāyā yakkhī ca paṇḍakau|

suttā matā tiracchānā, dārudhitalikāya ca||

sampīḷai saṅkamau maggau, rukkhau nāvā ca raj‍ju ca|

daṇḍau pattaṃ paṇāmaisi, vandai vāyami nacchupaiti||

vinītavatthu

281. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu mātuyā mātupaimaina āmasi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ āpattiṃ  āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dhītuyā dhītupaimaina āmasi…pai॰… bhaginiyā bhaginipaimaina āmasi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu purāṇadutiyikāya kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yakkhiniyā kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṇḍakassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu suttitthiyā kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu matitthiyā kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tiracchānagatitthiyā kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dārudhītalikāya kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

282. taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā itthiyau añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ sampīḷaitvā bāhāparamparāya ānaisuṃ| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘sādiyi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhagavā, sādiyi’’nti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhū, asādiyantassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiyā abhirūḷhaṃ saṅkamaṃ sārattau sañ‍cālaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiṃ paṭipathai passitvā  sārattau aṃsakūṭaina pahāraṃ adāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiyā abhirūḷhaṃ rukkhaṃ sārattau sañ‍cālaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiyā abhirūḷhaṃ nāvaṃ sārattau sañ‍cālaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiyā gahitaṃ raj‍juṃ sārattau āviñchi āviñ‍ji (sī॰ syā॰)| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiyā gahitaṃ daṇḍaṃ sārattau āviñchi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau itthiṃ pattaina paṇāmaisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu itthiyā vandantiyā sārattau pādaṃ uc‍cāraisi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu  itthiṃ gahaissāmīti vāyamitvā na chupi|

tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

kāyasaṃsaggasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. duṭṭhul‍lavācāsikkhāpadaṃ

283. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī arañ‍ñai viharati| tassāyasmatau vihārau abhirūpau hauti dassanīyau pāsādikau| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā itthiyau ārāmaṃ āgamaṃsu vihārapaikkhikāyau| atha khau tā itthiyau  yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘icchāma mayaṃ, bhantai, ayyassa vihāraṃ paikkhitu’’nti| atha khau āyasmā udāyī tā itthiyau vihāraṃ paikkhāpaitvā tāsaṃ itthīnaṃ vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati yācatipi āyācatipi pucchatipi paṭipucchatipi ācikkhatipi anusāsatipi ak‍kausatipi| yā tā itthiyau  chin‍nikā dhuttikā ahirikāyau tā āyasmatā udāyinā saddhiṃ uhasantipi ul‍lapantipi uj‍jagghantipi uppaṇḍaintipi| yā pana tā itthiyau hirimanā tā nikkhamitvā bhikkhū ujjhāpainti – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ| sāmikainapi mayaṃ aivaṃ vuttā na icchaiyyāma, kiṃ panāyyaina udāyinā’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰…  tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā  āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsissasi! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau nau sarāgāya…pai॰… kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

284. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu autiṇṇau vipariṇataina cittaina mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsaiyya yathā taṃ yuvā yuvatiṃ maithunupasaṃhitāhi, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

285. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

autiṇṇau nāma sārattau apaikkhavā paṭibaddhacittau|

vipariṇatanti  rattampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ, duṭṭhampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ mūḷhampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ| apica, rattaṃ cittaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitaṃ vipariṇatanti|

mātugāmau nāma manussitthī, na yakkhī, na paitī, na tiracchānagatā| viñ‍ñū paṭibalā subhāsitadubbhāsitaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ  ājānituṃ|

duṭṭhul‍lā nāma vācā vac‍camaggapassāvamaggamaithunadhammappaṭisaṃyuttā vācā|

aubhāsaiyyāti ajjhācārau vuc‍cati|

yathā taṃ yuvā yuvatinti daharau dahariṃ, taruṇau taruṇiṃ, kāmabhaugī kāmabhauginiṃ|

maithunupasaṃhitāhīti  maithunadhammappaṭisaṃyuttāhi|

saṅghādisaisauti  …pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

dvai maggai ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati, avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, yācatipi, āyācatipi, pucchatipi, paṭipucchatipi, ācikkhatipi, anusāsatipi, ak‍kausatipi|

vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati nāma dvai maggai thaumaiti vaṇṇaiti pasaṃsati|

avaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati nāma dvai maggai khuṃsaiti vambhaiti garahati|

yācati nāma daihi mai, arahasi mai dātunti|

āyācati nāma kadā tai mātā pasīdissati, kadā tai pitā pasīdissati, kadā tai daivatāyau pasīdissanti, kadā kadā tai (syā॰) sukhaṇau sulayau sumuhuttau bhavissati, kadā tai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ labhissāmīti|

pucchati nāma kathaṃ tvaṃ sāmikassa daisi, kathaṃ jārassa daisīti?

paṭipucchati nāma aivaṃ kira tvaṃ sāmikassa daisi, aivaṃ jārassa daisīti|

ācikkhati nāma puṭṭhau bhaṇati – ‘‘aivaṃ daihi| aivaṃ daintā sāmikassa piyā bhavissasi manāpā cā’’ti|

anusāsati nāma apuṭṭhau bhaṇati – ‘‘aivaṃ daihi| aivaṃ daintā sāmikassa piyā bhavissati manāpā cā’’ti|

ak‍kausati  nāma animittāsi, nimittamattāsi, alauhitāsi, dhuvalauhitāsi, dhuvacauḷāsi, paggharantīsi, sikharaṇīsi, itthipaṇḍakāsi, vaipurisikāsi, sambhin‍nāsi, ubhataubyañ‍janāsīti|

286. itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati yācatipi āyācatipi pucchatipi paṭipucchatipi ācikkhatipi anusāsatipi ak‍kausatipi, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇaṃpi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ṭhapaitvā adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ṭhapaitvā adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti dvin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ṭhapaitvā adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ  itthiyā ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai  itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī  ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati…pai॰… ak‍kausatipi, āpatti  dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰…|

287. anāpatti atthapuraikkhārassa, dhammapuraikkhārassa, anusāsanipuraikkhārassa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

lauhitaṃ kak‍kasākiṇṇaṃ, kharaṃ dīghañ‍ca vāpitaṃ|

kac‍ci saṃsīdati maggau, saddhā dānaina kammunāti||

vinītavatthu

288. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī navarattaṃ kambalaṃ pārutā hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘lauhitaṃ khau tai, bhaginī’’ti| sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āmāyya, navarattau kambalau’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kharakambalaṃ pārutā hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kak‍kasalaumaṃ khau tai, bhaginī’’ti| sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āmāyya, kharakambalakau’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī navāvutaṃ kambalaṃ pārutā hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ākiṇṇalaumaṃ khau tai, bhaginī’’ti| sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āmāyya, navāvutau kambalau’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kharakambalaṃ pārutā hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kharalaumaṃ khau tai, bhaginī’’ti| sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āmāyya, kharakambalakau’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī pāvāraṃ dīghapāvāraṃ (syā॰) pārutā hauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dīghalaumaṃ khau tai, bhaginī’’ti| sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āmāyya, pāvārau’’ dīghapāvārau (syā॰) ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

289. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī khaittaṃ vapāpaitvā āgacchati| añ‍ñatarau  bhikkhu sārattau taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vāpitaṃ khau tai, bhaginī’’ti? sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āmāyya, nau ca khau paṭivutta’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paribbājikaṃ paṭipathai passitvā sārattau taṃ paribbājikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhagini, maggau saṃsīdatī’’ti? sā na paṭivijāni| ‘‘āma bhikkhu, paṭipaj‍jissasī’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau añ‍ñataraṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saddhāsi tvaṃ, bhagini| apica, yaṃ sāmikassa daisi taṃ nāmhākaṃ daisī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai’’ti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau añ‍ñataraṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saddhāsi tvaṃ, bhagini| apica, yaṃ aggadānaṃ taṃ nāmhākaṃ daisī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kammaṃ karauti| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sārattau  taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭha, bhagini, ahaṃ karissāmīti…pai॰… nisīda, bhagini, ahaṃ karissāmīti…pai॰… nipaj‍ja, bhagini, ahaṃ karissāmī’’ti| sā na paṭivijāni| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

duṭṭhul‍lavācāsikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. attakāmapāricariyasikkhāpadaṃ

290. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī sāvatthiyaṃ kulūpakau hauti, bahukāni kulāni upasaṅkamati| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī matapatikā abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā| atha khau āyasmā udāyī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassā itthiyā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sā itthī yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ itthiṃ āyasmā udāyī dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau sā itthī āyasmatā udāyinā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vadaiyyātha, bhantai, yaina atthau| paṭibalā mayaṃ ayyassa dātuṃ yadidaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāra’’nti|

‘‘na  khau tai, bhagini, amhākaṃ dul‍labhā yadidaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā| apica, yau amhākaṃ dul‍labhau taṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai’’ti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| ‘‘atthau, bhantai’’ti? ‘‘atthau, bhaginī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai’’ti, auvarakaṃ pavisitvā sāṭakaṃ nikkhipitvā mañ‍cakai uttānā nipaj‍ji| atha khau āyasmā udāyī yaina sā itthī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā – ‘‘kau imaṃ vasalaṃ duggandhaṃ āmasissatī’’ti, niṭṭhuhitvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sā itthī ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘alaj‍jinau imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dussīlā musāvādinau| imai hi nāma dhammacārinau samacārinau brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā paṭijānissanti! natthi imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ natthi imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, naṭṭhaṃ imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ naṭṭhaṃ imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, kutau imaisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ kutau imaisaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, apagatā imai sāmañ‍ñā apagatā imai brahmañ‍ñā| kathañhi nāma samaṇau udāyī maṃ sāmaṃ maithunadhammaṃ yācitvā, ‘kau imaṃ vasalaṃ duggandhaṃ āmasissatī’’ti niṭṭhuhitvā pak‍kamissati! kiṃ mai pāpakaṃ kiṃ mai duggandhaṃ, kassāhaṃ kaina hāyāmī’’ti? añ‍ñāpi itthiyau ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘alaj‍jinau imai samaṇā  sakyaputtiyā dussīlā musāvādinau…pai॰… kathañhi nāma samaṇau udāyī imissā sāmaṃ maithunadhammaṃ  yācitvā, ‘kau imaṃ vasalaṃ duggandhaṃ āmasissatī’ti niṭṭhuhitvā pak‍kamissati! kiṃ imissā pāpakaṃ kiṃ imissā duggandhaṃ, kassāyaṃ kaina hāyatī’’ti? assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tāsaṃ itthīnaṃ ujjhāyantīnaṃ khiyyantīnaṃ vipācaintīnaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsissatī’’ti!

atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā  āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsissasi! nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina virāgāya dhammau daisitau nau sarāgāya…pai॰… kāmapariḷāhānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau? naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

291. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu autiṇṇau vipariṇataina cittaina mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyya – ‘aitadaggaṃ, bhagini, pāricariyānaṃ yā mādisaṃ sīlavantaṃ kalyāṇadhammaṃ brahmacāriṃ aitaina dhammaina paricaraiyyāti maithunupasaṃhitaina’, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

292. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

autiṇṇau nāma sārattau apaikkhavā paṭibaddhacittau|

vipariṇatanti rattampi cittaṃ vipariṇattaṃ, duṭṭhampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ, mūḷhampi cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ| apica, rattaṃ cittaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitaṃ vipariṇatanti|

mātugāmau  nāma manussitthī, na yakkhī, na paitī, na tiracchānagatā| viñ‍ñū paṭibalā subhāsitadubbhāsitaṃ duṭṭhal‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ ājānituṃ|

mātugāmassa santikaiti mātugāmassa sāmantā, mātugāmassa avidūrai|

attakāmanti attanau kāmaṃ attanau haituṃ attanau adhippāyaṃ attanau pāricariyaṃ|

aitadagganti aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ aitaṃ maukkhaṃ aitaṃ uttamaṃ aitaṃ pavaraṃ|

yāti khattiyī khattiyā (syā॰) vā brāhmaṇī vā vaissī vā suddī vā|

mādisanti khattiyaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vaissaṃ vā suddaṃ vā|

sīlavantanti pāṇātipātā paṭivirataṃ, adin‍nādānā paṭivirataṃ, musāvādā paṭivirataṃ|

brahmacārinti maithunadhammā paṭivirataṃ|

kalyāṇadhammau nāma taina ca sīlaina taina ca brahmacariyaina kalyāṇadhammau hauti|

aitaina  dhammaināti maithunadhammaina|

paricaraiyyāti abhiramaiyya|

maithunupasaṃhitaināti maithunadhammappaṭisaṃyuttaina|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

293. itthī  ca hauti itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

itthī  ca hauti vaimatikau…pai॰… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ itthiyā santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

paṇḍakau ca hauti paṇḍakasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

paṇḍakau ca hauti vaimatikau…pai॰… purisasañ‍ñī… tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī… itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ paṇḍakassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

purisau ca hauti…pai॰… tiracchānagatau ca hauti tiracchānagatasañ‍ñī…pai॰… vaimatikau itthisañ‍ñī… paṇḍakasañ‍ñī… purisasañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ tiracchānagatassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

dvai itthiyau dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ dvin‍naṃ itthīnaṃ santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ…pai॰…|

itthī ca paṇḍakau ca ubhin‍naṃ itthisañ‍ñī sārattau ca| bhikkhu ca naṃ ubhin‍naṃ santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰…|

294. anāpatti ‘‘cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina upaṭṭhahā’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa ādikammikassāti|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

kathaṃ  vañjhā labhai puttaṃ, piyā ca subhagā siyaṃ|

kiṃ daj‍jaṃ kainupaṭṭhaiyyaṃ, kathaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ suggatinti||

vinītavatthu

295. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā vañjhā itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, vijāyaiyya’’nti? ‘‘taina hi, bhagini, aggadānaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā vijāyinī itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, puttaṃ labhaiyya’’nti? ‘‘taina hi, bhagini, aggadānaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, sāmikassa piyā assa’’nti? ‘‘taina hi, bhagini, aggadānaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, subhagā assa’’nti? ‘‘taina hi, bhagini, aggadānaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kyāhaṃ, bhantai, ayyassa daj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘aggadānaṃ, bhaginī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaināhaṃ, bhantai, ayyaṃ upaṭṭhaimī’’ti? ‘‘aggadānaina, bhaginī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī kulūpakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ,

bhantai, sugatiṃ gacchaiyya’’nti? ‘‘taina hi, bhagini, aggadānaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aggadāna’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhamma’’nti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āpan‍nau saṅghādisaisa’’nti|

attakāmapāricariyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. sañ‍carittasikkhāpadaṃ

296. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī sāvatthiyaṃ kulūpakau hauti| bahukāni kulāni upasaṅkamati| yattha passati kumārakaṃ vā apajāpatikaṃ, kumārikaṃ vā apatikaṃ, kumārakassa mātāpitūnaṃ santikai kumārikāya vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati – ‘‘amukassa kulassa kumārikā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā paṇḍitā byattā maidhāvinī dakkhā analasā| chan‍nā sā kumārikā imassa kumārakassā’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘aitai khau, bhantai, amhai na jānanti – ‘kai vā imai kassa vā’ti| sacai, bhantai, ayyau dāpaiyya ānaiyyāma mayaṃ taṃ kumārikaṃ imassa kumārakassā’’ti| kumārikāya mātāpitūnaṃ santikai kumārakassa vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati – ‘‘amukassa kulassa kumārakau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī dakkhau analasau| chan‍nāyaṃ kumārikā tassa kumārakassā’’ti chan‍nau sau kumārakau imissā kumārikāyāti (syā॰)| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘aitai khau, bhantai, amhai na jānanti – ‘kai vā imai kassa vā’ti, kismiṃ viya kumārikāya vattuṃ| sacai, bhantai, ayyau yācāpaiyya daj‍jaiyyāma mayaṃ imaṃ kumārikaṃ tassa kumārakassā’’ti| aitainaiva upāyaina āvāhānipi kārāpaiti, vivāhānipi kārāpaiti, vāraiyyānipi kārāpaiti|

297. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarissā purāṇagaṇakiyā dhītā abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā| tiraugāmakā ājīvakasāvakā āgantvā taṃ gaṇakiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daihāyyai, imaṃ kumārikaṃ amhākaṃ kumārakassā’’ti| sā aivamāha – ‘‘ahaṃ khvayyau khvayyā (syā॰), khvāyyau (ka॰) tumhai  na jānāmi – ‘kai vā imai kassa vā’ti| ayañ‍ca mai aikadhītikā, tiraugāmau ca gantabbau, nāhaṃ dassāmī’’ti| manussā tai ājīvakasāvakai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tumhai, ayyau, āgatatthā’’ti? ‘‘idha mayaṃ, ayyau, amukaṃ nāma gaṇakiṃ dhītaraṃ yācimhā amhākaṃ kumārakassa| sā aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ, khvayyau tumhai na jānāmi – kai vā imai kassa vā’ti| ayañ‍ca mai aikadhītikā, tiraugāmau ca gantabbau, nāhaṃ dassāmī’’ti| ‘‘kissa tumhai, ayyau, taṃ gaṇakiṃ dhītaraṃ yācittha? nanu ayyau udāyī vattabbau| ayyau udāyī dāpaissatī’’ti|

atha khau tai ājīvakasāvakā  yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, amukaṃ nāma gaṇakiṃ dhītaraṃ yācimhā amhākaṃ kumārakassa| sā aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ khvayyau tumhai na jānāmi – kai vā imai kassa vāti| ayañ‍ca mai aikadhītikā, tiraugāmau ca gantabbau, nāhaṃ dassāmī’ti| sādhu, bhantai, ayyau taṃ gaṇakiṃ dhītaraṃ dāpaitu amhākaṃ kumārakassā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā udāyī yaina sā gaṇakī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ gaṇakiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissimaisaṃ dhītaraṃ na  daisī’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ khvayya, imai na jānāmi – ‘kai vā imai kassa vā’ti| ayañ‍ca mai aikadhītikā, tiraugāmau ca gantabbau, nāhaṃ dassāmī’’ti| ‘‘daihimaisaṃ| ahaṃ imai jānāmī’’ti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, ayyau jānāti, dassāmī’’ti| atha khau sā gaṇakī taisaṃ ājīvakasāvakānaṃ dhītaraṃ adāsi| atha khau tai ājīvakasāvakā taṃ kumārikaṃ naitvā māsaṃyaiva suṇisabhaugaina bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tatau aparaina dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍janti|

atha khau sā kumārikā mātuyā santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘ahamhi duggatā dukkhitā, na sukhaṃ labhāmi| māsaṃyaiva maṃ suṇisabhaugaina bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tatau aparaina dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍janti| āgacchatu mai mātā, maṃ naissatū’’ti| atha khau sā gaṇakī yaina tai ājīvakasāvakā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai ājīvakasāvakai aitadavauca – ‘‘māyyau, imaṃ kumārikaṃ dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍jittha| suṇisabhaugaina imaṃ kumārikaṃ bhuñ‍jathā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘natthamhākaṃ tayā saddhiṃ āhārūpahārau, samaṇaina saddhiṃ amhākaṃ āhārūpahārau| gaccha tvaṃ| na mayaṃ taṃ jānāmā’’ti| atha khau sā gaṇakī taihi ājīvakasāvakaihi apasāditā punadaiva sāvatthiṃ pac‍cāgañchi| dutiyampi khau sā kumārikā mātuyā santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘ahamhi duggatā dukkhitā, na sukhaṃ labhāmi| māsaṃyaiva maṃ suṇisabhaugaina bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tatau aparaina dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍janti| āgacchatu mai mātā, maṃ naissatū’’ti| atha khau sā gaṇakī yaināyasmā  udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘sā kira, bhantai, kumārikā duggatā dukkhitā, na sukhaṃ labhati| māsaṃyaiva naṃ suṇisabhaugaina bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tatau aparaina dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍janti| vadaiyyātha, bhantai – ‘māyyau, imaṃ kumārikaṃ dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍jittha| suṇisabhaugaina imaṃ kumārikaṃ bhuñ‍jithā’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā udāyī yaina tai ājīvakasāvakā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai ājīvakasāvakai aitadavauca – ‘‘māyyau, imaṃ kumārikaṃ dāsibhaugaina bhuj‍jittha| suṇisabhaugaina imaṃ kumārikaṃ bhuñ‍jathā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘natthamhākaṃ tayā saddhiṃ āhārūpahārau, gaṇakiyā saddhiṃ amhākaṃ āhārūpahārau| samaṇaina bhavitabbaṃ abyāvaṭaina| samaṇau  assa susamaṇau, gaccha tvaṃ, na mayaṃ taṃ jānāmā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā udāyī taihi ājīvakasāvakaihi apasāditau punadaiva sāvatthiṃ pac‍cāgañchi| tatiyampi khau sā kumārikā mātuyā santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘ahamhi duggatā dukkhitā, na sukhaṃ labhāmi| māsaṃyaiva maṃ suṇisabhaugaina bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tatau aparaina dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍janti| āgacchatu mai mātā, maṃ naissatū’’ti| dutiyampi khau sā gaṇakī yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sā kira, bhantai, kumārikā duggatā dukkhitā, na sukhaṃ labhati| māsaṃyaiva naṃ suṇisabhaugaina bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tatau aparaina dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍janti| vadaiyyātha, bhantai – ‘māyyau, imaṃ kumārikaṃ dāsibhaugaina bhuñ‍jittha, suṇisabhaugaina imaṃ  kumārikaṃ bhuñ‍jathā’’’ti| ‘‘paṭhamaṃpāhaṃ taihi ājīvakasāvakaihi apasāditau| gaccha tvaṃ| nāhaṃ gamissāmī’’ti|

298. atha khau sā gaṇakī ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘aivaṃ duggatau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ dukkhitau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ  mā sukhaṃ labhatu ayyau udāyī, yathā mai kumārikā duggatā dukkhitā na sukhaṃ labhati pāpikāya sassuyā pāpakaina sasuraina pāpakaina sāmikainā’’ti| sāpi khau kumārikā ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘aivaṃ duggatau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ dukkhitau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ mā sukhaṃ labhatu ayyau udāyī, yathāhaṃ duggatā dukkhitā na sukhaṃ labhāmi pāpikāya sassuyā pāpakaina sasuraina pāpakaina sāmikainā’’ti| añ‍ñāpi itthiyau asantuṭṭhā sassūhi vā sasuraihi vā sāmikaihi vā, tā aivaṃ auyācanti – ‘‘aivaṃ duggatau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ dukkhitau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ mā sukhaṃ labhatu ayyau udāyī, yathā mayaṃ duggatā dukkhitā na  sukhaṃ labhāma pāpikāhi sassūhi pāpakaihi sasuraihi pāpakaihi sāmikaihī’’ti| yā pana tā itthiyau santuṭṭhā sassūhi vā sasuraihi vā sāmikaihi vā tā aivaṃ āyācanti – ‘‘aivaṃ sukhitau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ saj‍jitau hautu ayyau udāyī, aivaṃ sukhamaidhau sukhamaidhitau (sī॰ ka॰) hautu ayyau udāyī, yathā mayaṃ sukhitā saj‍jitā sukhamaidhā bhaddikāhi sassūhi bhaddakaihi sasuraihi bhaddakaihi sāmikaihī’’ti|

assausuṃ  khau bhikkhū aikac‍cānaṃ itthīnaṃ auyācantīnaṃ aikac‍cānaṃ itthīnaṃ āyācantīnaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

299. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, itthiyā vā purisamatiṃ purisassa vā itthimatiṃ, jāyattanai vā jārattanai vā, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ  bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

300. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā dhuttā uyyānai paricāraintā añ‍ñatarissā vaisiyā santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisuṃ – ‘‘āgacchatu uyyānai, paricāraissāmā’’ti| sā aivamāha – ‘‘ahaṃ khvayyau tumhai na jānāmi – ‘kai vā imai kassa vā’ti| ahañ‍camhi bahubhaṇḍā bahuparikkhārā, bahinagarañ‍ca gantabbaṃ| nāhaṃ gamissāmī’’ti| atha khau sau dūtau taisaṃ dhuttānaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau purisau  tai dhuttai aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tumhai ayyau aitaṃ vaisiṃ yācittha? nanu ayyau udāyī vattabbau! ayyau udāyī uyyaujaissatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau upāsakau taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘māyyau aivaṃ avaca| na kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ aivarūpaṃ kātuṃ| nāyyau udāyī aivaṃ karissatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, ‘‘karissati na karissatī’’ti abbhutamakaṃsu| atha khau tai dhuttā yaināyasmā  udāyī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, uyyānai paricāraintā asukāya nāma vaisiyā santikai dūtaṃ pahiṇimhā – ‘āgacchatu uyyānai, paricāraissāmā’ti| sā aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ khvayyau tumhai na jānāmi – kai vā imai kassa vāti, ahañ‍camhi bahubhaṇḍā bahuparikkhārā, bahinagarañ‍ca gantabbaṃ| nāhaṃ gamissāmī’ti| sādhu, bhantai, ayyau taṃ vasiṃ uyyaujaitū’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā udāyī yaina sā vaisī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ vaisiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissimaisaṃ na gacchasī’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ khvayya imai na jānāmi – ‘kai vā imai kassa vā’ti| ahañ‍camhi bahubhaṇḍā bahuparikkhārā, bahinagarañ‍ca gantabbaṃ| nāhaṃ gamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘gacchimaisaṃ| ahaṃ imai jānāmī’’ti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, ayyau jānāti ahaṃ gamissāmī’’ti| atha khau tai dhuttā taṃ vaisiṃ ādāya uyyānaṃ agamaṃsu| atha khau sau upāsakau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyau udāyī taṅkhaṇikaṃ sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jissatī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa upāsakassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa  vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā udāyī taṅkhaṇikaṃ sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ  udāyiṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, udāyi, taṅkhaṇikaṃ sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, taṅkhaṇikaṃ sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jissasi? naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

301. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, itthiyā vā purisamatiṃ purisassa vā itthimatiṃ, jāyattanai vā jārattanai vā, antamasau taṅkhaṇikāyapi, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

302. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāti itthiyā vā pahitau purisassa santikai gacchati, purisaina vā pahitau itthiyā santikai gacchati|

itthiyā  vā purisamatinti purisassa matiṃ itthiyā āraucaiti|

purisassa vā itthimatinti itthiyā matiṃ purisassa āraucaiti|

jāyattanai vāti jāyā bhavissasi|

jārattanai vāti jārī bhavissasi|

antamasau taṅkhaṇikāyapīti muhuttikā bhavissasi|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

303. dasa itthiyau – māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sārakkhā saparidaṇḍā|

dasa bhariyāyau – dhanak‍kītā chandavāsinī bhaugavāsinī paṭavāsinī audapattakinī aubhaṭacumbaṭā dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā muhuttikā|

304. māturakkhitā nāma mātā rakkhati gaupaiti issariyaṃ kāraiti vasaṃ vattaiti|

piturakkhitā  nāma pitā rakkhati gaupaiti issariyaṃ kāraiti vasaṃ vattaiti|

mātāpiturakkhitā nāma mātāpitarau rakkhanti gaupainti issariyaṃ kārainti vasaṃ vattainti|

bhāturakkhitā nāma bhātā rakkhati gaupaiti issariyaṃ kāraiti vasaṃ vattaiti|

bhaginirakkhitā nāma bhaginī rakkhati gaupaiti issariyaṃ kāraiti vasaṃ vattaiti|

ñātirakkhitā  nāma ñātakā rakkhanti gaupainti issariyaṃ kārainti vasaṃ vattainti|

gauttarakkhitā nāma sagauttā rakkhanti gaupainti issariyaṃ kārainti vasaṃ vattainti|

dhammarakkhitā nāma sahadhammikā rakkhanti gaupainti issariyaṃ kārainti vasaṃ vattainti|

sārakkhā nāma gabbhaipi pariggahitā hauti – mayhaṃ aisāti| antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi|

saparidaṇḍā nāma kaihici daṇḍau ṭhapitau hauti – yau itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ gacchati aittakau daṇḍauti|

dhanak‍kītā  nāma dhanaina kiṇitvā vāsaiti|

chandavāsinī nāma piyau piyaṃ vāsaiti|

bhaugavāsinī nāma bhaugaṃ datvā vāsaiti|

paṭavāsinī nāma paṭaṃ datvā vāsaiti|

audapattakinī nāma udakapattaṃ āmasitvā vāsaiti|

aubhaṭacumbaṭā nāma cumbaṭaṃ auraupaitvā vāsaiti|

dāsī nāma dāsī caiva hauti bhariyā ca|

kammakārī nāma kammakārī caiva hauti bhariyā ca|

dhajāhaṭā nāma karamarānītā vuc‍cati|

muhuttikā  nāma taṅkhaṇikā vuc‍cati|

305. purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitaṃ brūhi – ‘hauhi kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ piturakkhitaṃ brūhi…pai॰… mātāpiturakkhitaṃ brūhi… bhāturakkhitaṃ brūhi… bhaginirakkhitaṃ brūhi… ñātirakkhitaṃ brūhi… gauttarakkhitaṃ brūhi… dhammarakkhitaṃ brūhi… sārakkhaṃ brūhi… saparidaṇḍaṃ brūhi – ‘hauhi kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nikkhaipapadāni|

306. purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitañ‍ca piturakkhitañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau  dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitañ‍ca mātāpituraakhatañ‍ca…pai॰…

māturakkhitañ‍ca bhāturakkhitañ‍ca… māturakkhitañ‍ca bhaginirakkhitañ‍ca… māturakkhitañ‍ca ñātirakkhitañ‍ca… māturakkhitañ‍ca gauttarakkhitañ‍ca… māturakkhitañ‍ca dhammarakkhitañ‍ca… māturakkhitañ‍ca sārakkhañ‍ca… māturakkhitañ‍ca saparidaṇḍañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

307. purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ piturakkhitañ‍ca mātāpiturakkhitañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ piturakkhitañ‍ca bhāturakkhitañ‍ca…pai॰… piturakkhitañ‍ca bhaginirakkhitañ‍ca… piturakkhitañ‍ca ñātirakkhitañ‍ca… piturakkhitañ‍ca gauttarakkhitañ‍ca… piturakkhitañ‍ca dhammarakkhitañ‍ca… piturakkhitañ‍ca sārakkhañ‍ca… piturakkhitañ‍ca saparidaṇḍañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ piturakkhitañ‍ca māturakkhitañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau  dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

baddhacak‍kaṃ mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

308. purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ saparidaṇḍañ‍ca māturakkhitañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ saparidaṇḍañ‍ca pituraakhatañ‍ca…pai॰…

saparidaṇḍañ‍ca mātāpiturakkhitañ‍ca… saparidaṇḍañ‍ca bhāturakkhitañ‍ca… saparidaṇḍañ‍ca bhaginirakkhitañ‍ca… saparidaṇḍañ‍ca ñātirakkhitañ‍ca… saparidaṇḍañ‍ca gauttarakkhitañ‍ca… saparidaṇḍañ‍ca dhammarakkhitañ‍ca… saparidaṇḍañ‍ca sārakkhañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aivaṃ dumūlakampi  timūlakampi  yāva navamūlakaṃ kātabbaṃ|

idaṃ dasamūlakaṃ

309. purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitañ‍ca piturakkhitañ‍ca  mātāpiturakkhitañ‍ca bhāturakkhitañ‍ca bhaginirakkhitañ‍ca ñātirakkhitañ‍ca gauttarakkhitañ‍ca dhammarakkhitañ‍ca sārakkhañ‍ca saparidaṇḍañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dhanak‍kītācak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

nikkhaipapadāni|

aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dumūlakampi timūlakampi yāva navamūlakaṃ aivamaiva kātabbaṃ|

idaṃ dasamūlakaṃ|

321. purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ saparidaṇḍaṃ brūhi – ‘hauhi kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca paṭavāsinī ca audapattakinī ca aubhaṭacumbaṭā ca dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saparidaṇḍācak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitaṃ brūhi – ‘hauhi kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitañ‍ca piturakkhitañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitañ‍ca piturakkhitañ‍ca mātāpiturakkhitañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aivaṃ ubhatauvaḍḍhakaṃ kātabbaṃ|

purisau  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ māturakkhitañ‍ca piturakkhitañ‍ca mātāpiturakkhitañ‍ca bhāturakkhitañ‍ca bhaginirakkhitañ‍ca ñātirakkhitañ‍ca gauttarakkhitañ‍ca dhammarakkhitañ‍ca sārakkhañ‍ca saparidaṇḍañ‍ca brūhi – ‘hautha kira itthan‍nāmassa bhariyāyau dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca paṭavāsinī ca audapattakinī ca aubhaṭacumbaṭā ca dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

ubhatauvaḍḍhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

purisassa  mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati…pai॰… purisassa pitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati…pai॰… purisassa mātāpitarau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti…pai॰… purisassa bhātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati…pai॰… purisassa bhaginī bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati…pai॰… purisassa ñātakā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti…pai॰… purisassa gauttā bhikkhuṃ  pahiṇanti…pai॰… purisassa sahadhammikā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti…pai॰…|

purisassa paiyyālau vitthāraitabbau|

ubhatauvaḍḍhakaṃ yathā purimanayau tathaiva vitthāraitabbaṃ|

322. māturakkhitāya mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

māturakkhitāya mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī…pai॰… bhaugavāsinī, paṭavāsinī, audapattakinī, aubhaṭacumbaṭā, dāsī ca bhariyā ca, kammakārī ca bhariyā ca, dhajāhaṭā, muhuttikā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nikkhaipapadāni|

323. māturakkhitāya  mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… dhanak‍kītā ca bhaugavāsinī ca, dhanak‍kītā ca  muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

324. māturakkhitāya mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca…pai॰… chandavāsinī ca muhuttikā ca, chandavāsinī ca dhanak‍kītā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

baddhacak‍kaṃ mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

325. māturakkhitāya mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā muhuttikā ca dhanak‍kītā ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca dhajāhaṭā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dumūlakampi timūlakampi yāva navamūlakaṃ aivamaiva kātabbaṃ|

idaṃ dasamūlakaṃ

326. māturakkhitāya  mātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca paṭavāsinī ca audapattakinī ca aubhaṭacumbaṭā ca dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

mātucak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

piturakkhitāya  pitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati…pai॰… mātāpiturakkhitāya mātāpitarau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti… bhāturakkhitāya bhātā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… bhaginirakkhitāya bhaginī bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… ñātirakkhitāya ñātakā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti… gauttarakkhitāya gauttā sagauttā (?) bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti… dhammarakkhitāya sahadhammikā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇanti… sārakkhāya yaina pariggahitā hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… saparidaṇḍāya yaina daṇḍau ṭhapitau hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saparidaṇḍāya yaina daṇḍau ṭhapitau hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī…pai॰… bhaugavāsinī… paṭavāsinī… audapattakinī… aubhaṭacumbaṭā… dāsī ca bhariyā ca… kammakārī ca bhariyā ca… dhajāhaṭā… muhuttikā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nikkhaipapadāni|

327. saparidaṇḍāya  yaina daṇḍau ṭhapitau hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… dhanak‍kītā ca bhaugavāsinī ca… dhanak‍kītā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

328. saparidaṇḍāya  yaina daṇḍau ṭhapitau hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca…pai॰… chandavāsinī ca muhuttikā ca… chandavāsinī ca dhanak‍kītā cā’’’ ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

baddhacak‍kaṃ mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

329. saparidaṇḍāya  yaina daṇḍau, ṭhapitau hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā muhuttikā ca dhanak‍kītā ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca dhajāhaṭā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dumūlakampi timūlakampi yāva navamūlakaṃ aivamaiva kātabbaṃ|

idaṃ dasamūlakaṃ

330. saparidaṇḍāya  yaina daṇḍau ṭhapitau hauti sau bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘hautu itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca paṭavāsinī ca audapattakinī ca aubhaṭacumbaṭā ca dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

daṇḍaṭhapitacak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

māturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

māturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī…pai॰… bhaugavāsinī… paṭavāsinī… audapattakinī… aubhaṭacumbaṭā… dāsī ca bhariyā ca… kammakārī ca bhariyā ca… dhajāhaṭā… muhuttikā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nikkhaipapadāni|

331. māturakkhitā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa  bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

māturakkhitā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca bhaugavāsinī ca…pai॰… dhanak‍kītā ca paṭavāsinī ca…pai॰… dhanak‍kītā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

332. māturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca…pai॰… chandavāsinī ca muhuttikā ca… chandavāsinī ca dhanak‍kītā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

baddhacak‍kaṃ mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

māturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā muhuttikā ca dhanak‍kītā ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca dhajāhaṭā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dumūlakādīnipi aivamaiva kātabbāni|

idaṃ dasamūlakaṃ

333. māturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca paṭavāsinī ca audapattakinī ca aubhaṭacumbaṭā ca dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aparaṃ māturakkhitācak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

piturakkhitā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati…pai॰… mātāpiturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… bhāturakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… bhaginirakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… ñātirakkhitā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… gauttarakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… dhammarakkhitā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… sārakkhā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati… saparidaṇḍā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saparidaṇḍā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī…pai॰… bhaugavāsinī… paṭavāsinī… audapattakinī… aubhaṭacumbaṭā… dāsī ca bhariyā ca… kammakārī ca bhariyā ca… dhajāhaṭā… muhuttikā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

nikkhaipapadāni|

334. saparidaṇḍā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… dhanak‍kītā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

khaṇḍacak‍kaṃ|

335. saparidaṇḍā bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca…pai॰… chandavāsinī ca muhuttikā ca… chandavāsinī ca dhanak‍kītā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

baddhacak‍kaṃ mūlaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|

336. saparidaṇḍā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā muhuttikā ca dhanak‍kītā  ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca chandavāsinī ca…pai॰… muhuttikā ca dhajāhaṭā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikamūlakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dumūlakādīnipi aivamaiva kātabbāni|

idaṃ dasamūlakaṃ

337. saparidaṇḍā  bhikkhuṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ brūhi – ‘haumi itthan‍nāmassa bhariyā dhanak‍kītā ca chandavāsinī ca bhaugavāsinī ca paṭavāsinī ca audapattakinī ca aubhaṭacumbaṭā ca dāsī ca bhariyā ca kammakārī ca bhariyā ca dhajāhaṭā ca muhuttikā cā’’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aparaṃ saparidaṇḍācak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

sabbaṃ cak‍kapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

338. paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| paṭiggaṇhāti  vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| paṭiggaṇhāti na vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| paṭiggaṇhāti na vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| na  paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na paṭiggaṇhāti na vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na paṭiggaṇhāti na vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, anāpatti|

purisau sambahulai bhikkhū āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gacchatha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsathā’’ti| sabbai paṭiggaṇhanti sabbai vīmaṃsanti sabbai pac‍cāharanti, āpatti sabbaisaṃ saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau sambahulai bhikkhū āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gacchatha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsathā’’ti| sabbai paṭiggaṇhanti sabbai vīmaṃsanti aikaṃ pac‍cāharāpainti, āpatti sabbaisaṃ saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau  sambahulai bhikkhū āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gacchatha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsathā’’ti| sabbai paṭiggaṇhanti, aikaṃ vīmaṃsāpaitvā sabbai pac‍cāharanti, āpatti sabbaisaṃ saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau sambahulai bhikkhū āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gacchatha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsathā’’ti| sabbai paṭiggaṇhanti, aikaṃ vīmaṃsāpaitvā aikaṃ pac‍cāharāpainti, āpatti sabbaisaṃ saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau  bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsā’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsā’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati antaivāsiṃ pac‍cāharāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsā’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti antaivāsiṃ vīmaṃsāpaitvā attanā pac‍cāharati āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

purisau bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaiti  – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthi vimaṃsā’’ti| paṭiggaṇhāti antaivāsiṃ vīmaṃsāpaiti antaivāsī vīmaṃsitvā bahiddhā pac‍cāharati, āpatti ubhin‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayassa|

339. gacchantau sampādaiti, āgacchantau visaṃvādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

gacchantau visaṃvādaiti, āgacchantau sampādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

gacchantau sampādaiti, āgacchantau sampādaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

gacchantau visaṃvādaiti, āgacchantau visaṃvādaiti, anāpatti|

340. anāpatti saṅghassa vā caitiyassa vā gilānassa vā karaṇīyaina gacchati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vinītavatthuuddānagāthā

suttā  matā ca nikkhantā, anitthī itthipaṇḍakā|

kalahaṃ katvāna sammaudi, sañ‍carittañ‍ca paṇḍakaiti||

vinītavatthu

341. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ  āṇāpaisi – āṇāpaiti (syā॰ ka॰) ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsā’’ti| sau gantvā manussai pucchi – ‘‘kahaṃ itthan‍nāmā’’ti? ‘‘suttā, bhantai’’ti| tassa  kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  ahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kac‍ci nu khau ahaṃ saṅghādisaisaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ itthiṃ vīmaṃsā’’ti| sau gantvā manussai pucchi – ‘‘kahaṃ itthan‍nāmā’’ti? ‘‘matā, bhantai’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘nikkhantā, bhantai’’ti… ‘‘anitthī, bhantai’’ti… ‘‘itthipaṇḍakā, bhantai’’ti| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī sāmikaina saha bhaṇḍitvā mātugharaṃ agamāsi| kulūpakau bhikkhu sammaudanīyaṃ akāsi| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘alaṃvacanīyā, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nālaṃvacanīyā, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, nālaṃvacanīyāyā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṇḍakai sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahausi…pai॰… ‘‘anāpatti, bhikkhu, saṅghādisaisassa; āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

sañ‍carittasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kuṭikārasikkhāpadaṃ

342. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āḷavakā bhikkhū sañ‍ñācikāyau kuṭiyau kārāpainti assāmikāyau attuddaisikāyau appamāṇikāyau| tāyau na niṭṭhānaṃ gacchanti| tai yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharanti – ‘‘purisaṃ daitha, purisatthakaraṃ daitha, gauṇaṃ daitha, sakaṭaṃ daitha, vāsiṃ daitha, parasuṃ daitha, kuṭhāriṃ daitha, kudālaṃ daitha, nikhādanaṃ daitha, val‍liṃ daitha, vaiḷuṃ daitha, muñ‍jaṃ daitha, pabbajaṃ daitha, tiṇaṃ daitha, mattikaṃ daithā’’ti| manussā upaddutā yācanāya upaddutā viñ‍ñattiyā bhikkhū disvā ubbij‍jantipi uttasantipi palāyantipi añ‍ñainapi gacchanti añ‍ñainapi mukhaṃ karaunti dvārampi thakainti, gāvimpi disvā palāyanti bhikkhūti  mañ‍ñamānā|

atha  khau āyasmā mahākassapau rājagahai vassaṃvuṭṭhau yaina āḷavī taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina āḷavī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā mahākassapau āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āḷaviṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| manussā  āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ passitvā ubbij‍jantipi uttasantipi palāyantipi añ‍ñainapi gacchanti añ‍ñainapi mukhaṃ karaunti dvārampi thakainti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āḷaviyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘pubbāyaṃ, āvusau, āḷavī subhikkhā ahausi sulabhapiṇḍā sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ; aitarahi panāyaṃ āḷavī dubbhikkhā dul‍labhapiṇḍā, na sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| kau nu khau, āvusau, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaināyaṃ āḷavī dubbhikkhā dul‍labhapiṇḍā, na sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaitu’’nti? atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākassapassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

343. atha khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina āḷavī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina āḷavī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākassapau  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āḷavakai bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñācikāyau kuṭiyau kārāpaitha assāmikāyau attuddaisikāyau appamāṇikāyau| tāyau na niṭṭhānaṃ gacchanti| tai tumhai yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharatha – ‘purisaṃ daitha purisatthakaraṃ daitha…pai॰… tiṇaṃ daitha mattikaṃ daithā’ti| manussā upaddutā yācanāya upaddutā viñ‍ñattiyā bhikkhū disvā ubbij‍jantipi uttasantipi palāyantipi añ‍ñainapi gacchanti añ‍ñainapi mukhaṃ karaunti dvārampi thakainti, gāvimpi disvā palāyanti bhikkhūti mañ‍ñamānā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, saṃyācikāyau kuṭiyau kārāpaissatha assāmikāyau attuddaisikāyau appamāṇikāyau! tāyau na  niṭṭhānaṃ gacchanti| tai tumhai yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharissatha – ‘purisaṃ daitha purisatthakaraṃ daitha…pai॰… tiṇaṃ daitha mattikaṃ daithā’ti! naitaṃ maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…’’ vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

344. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvai bhātarau isayau gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ upanissāya vihariṃsu| atha khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau  nāgarājā gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttaritvā yaina kaniṭṭhau isi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvā uparimuddhani mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ karitvā aṭṭhāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, kaniṭṭhau isi tassa nāgassa bhayā kisau ahausi lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| addasa khau, bhikkhavai, jaiṭṭhau isi kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ kisaṃ lūkhaṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātaṃ dhamanisanthatagattaṃ| disvāna kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, bhau, kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhau, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttaritvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvā uparimuddhani mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ karitvā aṭṭhāsi| tassāhaṃ, bhau, nāgassa bhayā bhayāmhi (sī॰) kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau’’ti| ‘‘icchasi pana tvaṃ, bhau, tassa nāgassa anāgamana’’nti? ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhau, tassa nāgassa anāgamana’’nti| ‘‘api pana tvaṃ, bhau, tassa nāgassa kiñ‍ci passasī’’ti? ‘‘passāmahaṃ, bhau, maṇimassa maṇissa (sī॰ ka॰) kaṇṭhai pilandhana’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, bhau, taṃ nāgaṃ maṇiṃ yāca – ‘maṇiṃ mai, bhau, daihi; maṇinā mai atthau’’’ti|

atha khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttaritvā yaina kaniṭṭhau isi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ  aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhaṃ nāgarājānaṃ kaniṭṭhau isi aitadavauca – ‘‘maṇiṃ mai, bhau, daihi; maṇinā mai atthau’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā – ‘bhikkhu maṇiṃ yācati, bhikkhussa maṇinā atthau’ti khippañ‍ñaiva agamāsi| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttaritvā yaina kaniṭṭhau isi tainupasaṅkami| addasa  khau, bhikkhavai, kaniṭṭhau isi maṇikaṇṭhaṃ nāgarājānaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna maṇikaṇṭhaṃ nāgarājānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘maṇiṃ mai, bhau, daihi; maṇinā mai atthau’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā – ‘‘bhikkhu maṇiṃ yācati, bhikkhussa maṇinā atthau’’ti tatauva paṭinivatti| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttarati| addasa khau, bhikkhavai, kaniṭṭhau isi maṇikaṇṭhaṃ nāgarājānaṃ gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttarantaṃ| disvāna maṇikaṇṭhaṃ nāgarājānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘maṇiṃ mai, bhau, daihi; maṇinā mai atthau’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

jā॰ 1.3.7 maṇikaṇṭhajātakaipi ‘‘maman‍napānaṃ  vipulaṃ uḷāraṃ,

uppaj‍jatīmassa maṇissa haitu|

taṃ tai na dassaṃ atiyācakausi|

na cāpi tai assamamāgamissaṃ||

jā॰ 1.3.8 maṇikaṇṭhajātakaipi ‘‘susū yathā sakkharadhautapāṇī|

tāsaisi  maṃ sailamāyācamānau|

taṃ tai na dassaṃ atiyācakausi|

na cāpi tai assamamāgamissa’’nti||

atha khau, bhikkhavai, maṇikaṇṭhau nāgarājā – ‘‘bhikkhu maṇiṃ yācati, bhikkhussa maṇinā atthau’’ti pak‍kāmi| tathā pak‍kantauva tadāpak‍kantauva (ka॰) ahausi, na puna pac‍cāgañchi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, kaniṭṭhau isi tassa nāgassa dassanīyassa adassanaina bhiyyausaumattāya kisau ahausi lūkhau dubbaṇṇau, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| addasa khau, bhikkhavai, jaiṭṭhau isi kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ bhiyyausaumattāya kisaṃ lūkhaṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātaṃ dhamanisanthatagattaṃ| disvāna kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, bhau, bhiyyausaumattāya kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau  uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau’’ti? ‘‘tassāhaṃ, bhau, nāgassa dassanīyassa adassanaina bhiyyausaumattāya kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, jaiṭṭhau isi kaniṭṭhaṃ isiṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

jā॰ 1.3.9 maṇikaṇṭhajātakaipi ‘‘na  taṃ yācai yassa piyaṃ jigīsai,

vidaissau daissau (sī॰), daissau ca (syā॰) hauti atiyācanāya|

nāgau  maṇiṃ yācitau brāhmaṇaina|

adassanañ‍ñaiva tadajjhagamā’’ti||

taisañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatānaṃ pāṇānaṃ amanāpā bhavissati yācanā amanāpā viñ‍ñatti| kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰) pana manussabhūtānaṃ!

345. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu himavantapassai viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| tassa khau, bhikkhavai, vanasaṇḍassa avidūrai mahantaṃ nin‍naṃ pal‍lalaṃ| atha khau,

vāsāya upagacchati| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sau bhikkhu tassa sakuṇasaṅghassa saddaina ubbāḷhau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca  – ‘kac‍ci, bhikkhu, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ kac‍cisi appakilamathaina addhānaṃ āgatau? kutau ca tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āgacchasī’ti? ‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ, bhagavā| appakilamathaina cāhaṃ, bhantai, addhānaṃ āgatau| atthi, bhantai, himavantapassai mahāvanasaṇḍau| tassa khau pana, bhantai, vanasaṇḍassa avidūrai mahantaṃ nin‍naṃ pal‍lalaṃ| atha khau, bhantai, mahāsakuṇasaṅghau tasmiṃ pal‍lalai divasaṃ gaucaraṃ caritvā sāyaṃ taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ vāsāya upagacchati| tatau ahaṃ, bhagavā, āgacchāmi – tassa sakuṇasaṅghassa saddaina ubbāḷhau’ti| ‘icchasi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, tassa sakuṇasaṅghassa anāgamana’nti? ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhagavā, tassa sakuṇasaṅghassa anāgamana’nti| ‘taina hi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, tattha gantvā taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ tikkhattuṃ saddamanussāvaihi – suṇantu mai, bhauntau sakuṇā, yāvatikā imasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai vāsaṃ upagatā, pattaina mai atthau| aikaikaṃ mai, bhauntau, pattaṃ dadantū’ti| rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ… rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ tikkhattuṃ saddamanussāvaihi – ‘suṇantu  mai, bhauntau sakuṇā, yāvatikā imasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai vāsaṃ upagatā, pattaina mai atthau| aikaikaṃ mai, bhauntau, pattaṃ dadantū’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sau bhikkhu tattha gantvā taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ tikkhattuṃ saddamanussāvaisi – ‘suṇantu mai, bhauntau sakuṇā, yāvatikā imasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai vāsaṃ upagatā, pattaina mai atthau| aikaikaṃ mai, bhauntau, pattaṃ dadantū’ti| rattiyā majjhima yāmaṃ… rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ tikkhattuṃ saddamanussāvaisi – ‘suṇantu mai, bhauntau sakuṇā, yāvatikā imasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai vāsaṃ upagatā, pattaina mai atthau| aikaikaṃ mai, bhauntau, pattaṃ dadantū’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sau sakuṇasaṅghau – ‘bhikkhu pattaṃ yācati bhikkhussa pattaina atthau’ti tamhā vanasaṇḍā pak‍kāmi| tathā pak‍kantauva ahausi na puna pac‍cāgañchi| taisañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatānaṃ pāṇānaṃ amanāpā bhavissati yācanā amanāpā viñ‍ñatti| kimaṅgaṃ pana manussabhūtānaṃ’’!

346. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, raṭṭhapālassa kulaputtassa pitā raṭṭhapālaṃ kulaputtaṃ  gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘apāhaṃ tai na jānāmi, raṭṭhapāla bahū janā|

jā॰ 1.7.54 tai maṃ saṅgamma yācanti, kasmā maṃ tvaṃ na yācasī’ti||

jā॰ 1.7.55 ‘yācakau appiyau hauti, yācaṃ adadamappiyau|

tasmāhaṃ taṃ na yācāmi, mā mai vidaissanā ahū’ti||

‘‘sau hi nāma, bhikkhavai, raṭṭhapālau kulaputtau sakaṃ pitaraṃ aivaṃ vakkhati| kimaṅgaṃ pana janau janaṃ!

347. ‘‘gihīnaṃ, bhikkhavai, dussaṃharāni bhaugāni sambhatānipi durakkhiyāni| tattha nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, aivaṃ dussaṃharaisu bhaugaisu sambhataisupi durakkhiyaisu yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharissatha – ‘purisaṃ daitha, purisatthakaraṃ daitha, gauṇaṃ daitha, sakaṭaṃ daitha, vāsiṃ daitha, parasuṃ daitha, kuṭhāriṃ daitha, kudālaṃ daitha, nikhādanaṃ daitha, val‍liṃ daitha, vaiḷuṃ daitha, muñ‍jaṃ daitha, pabbajaṃ daitha, tiṇaṃ daitha, mattikaṃ daithā’’ti! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

348. ‘‘sañ‍ñācikāya pana bhikkhunā kuṭiṃ kārayamānaina assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ pamāṇikā kāraitabbā| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau dvādasa  vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā; tiriyaṃ sattantarā| bhikkhū abhinaitabbā vatthudaisanāya| taihi bhikkhūhi vatthu daisaitabbaṃ  – anārambhaṃ anārabbhaṃ (ka॰) saparik‍kamanaṃ| sārambhai sārabbhai (ka॰) cai bhikkhu vatthusmiṃ aparik‍kamanai sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kāraiyya, bhikkhū vā anabhinaiyya vatthudaisanāya, pamāṇaṃ vā atik‍kāmaiyya, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

349. sañ‍ñācikā nāma sayaṃ yācitvā purisampi purisatthakarampi gauṇampi sakaṭampi vāsimpi parasumpi kuṭhārimpi kudālampi nikhādanampi val‍limpi vaiḷumpi muñ‍jampi pabbajampi tiṇampi mattikampi|

kuṭi nāma ul‍littā vā hauti avalittā vā ul‍littāvalittā vā|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā|

assāmikanti na añ‍ñau kauci sāmikau hauti, itthī vā purisau vā gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā|

attuddaisanti attanau atthāya|

pamāṇikā kāraitabbā| tatridaṃ pamāṇaṃ – dīghasau dvādasa vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyāti bāhirimaina mānaina|

tiriyaṃ sattantarāti abbhantarimaina mānaina|

bhikkhū abhinaitabbā vatthudaisanāyāti taina kuṭikārakaina bhikkhunā kuṭivatthuṃ saudhaitvā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā  añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kattukāmau assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ kuṭivatthuaulaukanaṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi  yācitabbā| sacai  sabbau saṅghau ussahati kuṭivatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ, sabbaina saṅghaina aulaukaitabbaṃ| nau cai sabbau saṅghau ussahati kuṭivatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ, yai tattha haunti bhikkhū byattā paṭibalā sārambhaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ jānituṃ tai yācitvā samman‍nitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

350. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kattukāmau assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ kuṭivatthuaulaukanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhū samman‍naiyya itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kattukāmau assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ kuṭivatthuaulaukanaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhū samman‍nati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa ca itthan‍nāmassa ca bhikkhūnaṃ sammuti sammati (syā॰) itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca bhikkhū itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

351. taihi sammataihi bhikkhūhi tattha gantvā kuṭivatthu aulaukaitabbaṃ, sārambhaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ jānitabbaṃ| sacai sārambhaṃ hauti  aparik‍kamanaṃ, ‘mā idha karī’ti vattabbau| sacai anārambhaṃ hauti saparik‍kamanaṃ, saṅghassa āraucaitabbaṃ – ‘anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamana’nti| taina kuṭikārakaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kattukāmau assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ kuṭivatthudaisanaṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

352. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kattukāmau assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ kuṭivatthudaisanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthuṃ daisaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kattukāmau assāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ kuṭivatthudaisanaṃ yācati| saṅghau  itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthuṃ daisaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthussa daisanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘daisitaṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kuṭivatthu| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

353. sārambhaṃ nāma kipil‍likānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, upacikānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, undurānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, ahīnaṃ vā āsayau hauti, vicchikānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, satapadīnaṃ vā āsayau hauti, hatthīnaṃ  vā āsayau hauti, assānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, sīhānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, byagghānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, dīpīnaṃ vā āsayau hauti, acchānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, taracchānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci tiracchānagatānaṃ pāṇānaṃ āsayau hauti, pubbaṇṇanissitaṃ vā hauti, aparaṇṇanissitaṃ vā hauti, abbhāghātanissitaṃ vā hauti, āghātananissitaṃ vā hauti, susānanissitaṃ vā hauti, uyyānanissitaṃ vā hauti, rājavatthunissitaṃ vā hauti, hatthisālānissitaṃ vā hauti, assasālānissitaṃ vā hauti, bandhanāgāranissitaṃ vā hauti, pānāgāranissitaṃ vā hauti, sūnanissitaṃ vā hauti, racchānissitaṃ vā hauti, cac‍caranissitaṃ vā hauti, sabhānissitaṃ vā hauti, saṃsaraṇanissitaṃ vā sañ‍caraṇanissitaṃ vā (ka॰) hauti| aitaṃ sārambhaṃ nāma|

aparik‍kamanaṃ nāma na sak‍kā hauti yathāyuttaina sakaṭaina anuparigantuṃ samantā nissaiṇiyā anuparigantuṃ| aitaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ nāma|

anārambhaṃ  nāma na kipil‍likānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, na upacikānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, na undurānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, na ahīnaṃ vā āsayau hauti, na vicchikānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, na satapadīnaṃ vā āsayau hauti…pai॰… na saṃsaraṇanissitaṃ vā hauti| aitaṃ anārambhaṃ nāma|

saparik‍kamanaṃ  nāma sak‍kā hauti yathāyuttaina sakaṭaina anuparigantuṃ, samantā nissaiṇiyā anuparigantuṃ| aitaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ nāma|

sañ‍ñācikā nāma sayaṃ yācitvā purisampi purisatthakarampi…pai॰… mattikampi|

kuṭi nāma ul‍littā vā hauti vā avalittā vā ul‍littāvalittā vā|

kāraiyyāti karauti vā kārāpaiti vā|

bhikkhū vā anabhinaiyya, vatthudaisanāya pamāṇaṃ vā atik‍kāmaiyyāti ñattidutiyaina kammaina kuṭivatthuṃ na daisāpaitvā, āyāmatau vā vitthāratau vā antamasau kaisaggamattampi atik‍kāmaitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

354. bhikkhu  kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

355. bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu  kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu  kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu  kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇikaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti pamāṇikaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ|

bhikkhu  kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu kuṭiṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

356. bhikkhu samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|
