bhikkhu samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ  aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu  samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ|

bhikkhu samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

357. bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti  saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa  …pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

358. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati adaisitavatthukā sārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu  anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi  kira mai kayirati adaisitavatthukā sārambhā saparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā cā’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati adaisitavatthukā anārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati adaisitavatthukā anārambhā saparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘daisitavatthukā hautū’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati daisitavatthukā  sārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘anārambhā ca hautu saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi  kira mai kayirati daisitavatthukā sārambhā saparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘anārambhā hautū’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati daisitavatthukā anārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘saparik‍kamanā hautū’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā  ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

359. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca  – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati pamāṇātik‍kantā sārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘pamāṇikā hautū’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati pamāṇikā sārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘anārambhā ca hautu saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘anārambhā hautū’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘saparik‍kamanā hautū’’ti…pai॰… anāpatti|

360. bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati adaisitavatthukā pamāṇātik‍kantā sārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca  saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā cā’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā  ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘kuṭi kira mai kayirati daisitavatthukā pamāṇikā sārambhā aparik‍kamanā’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘anārambhā ca hautu saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘anārambhā hautū’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘saparik‍kamanā hautū’’ti…pai॰… anāpatti|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā  cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kāṭanaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘pamāṇikā ca hautu anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti  kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ catun‍naṃ  duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukā ca hautu pamāṇikā ca anārambhā ca saparik‍kamanā cā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ  aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

361. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ  aparik‍kamanaṃ…pai॰… āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ…pai॰… āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ  sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

362. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai  vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ saṅghādisaisānaṃ|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ  pamāṇikaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sā kuṭi añ‍ñassa vā dātabbā bhinditvā vā puna kātabbā| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kāṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

363. attanā  vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti pariyausāvāpaiti (ka॰), āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti pariyausāvāpaiti (ka॰), āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

364. anāpatti laiṇai guhāya tiṇakuṭikāya añ‍ñassatthāya vāsāgāraṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbattha, anāpatti ummattakassa ādikammikassāti|

kuṭikārasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vihārakārasikkhāpadaṃ

365. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau chan‍nassa upaṭṭhākau gahapati āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vihāravatthuṃ, bhantai, jānāhi ayyassa vihāraṃ kārāpaissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau vihāravatthuṃ saudhaintau añ‍ñataraṃ caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaisi gāmapūjitaṃ nigamapūjitaṃ nagarapūjitaṃ janapadapūjitaṃ raṭṭhapūjitaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā caitiyarukkhaṃ  chaidāpaissanti gāmapūjitaṃ  nigamapūjitaṃ nagarapūjitaṃ janapadapūjitaṃ raṭṭhapūjitaṃ! aikindriyaṃ samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jīvaṃ vihaiṭhaintī’’ vihaiṭhaissanti (katthaci) ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaissati gāmapūjitaṃ…pai॰… raṭṭhapūjita’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, chan‍na, caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaisi gāmapūjitaṃ…pai॰… raṭṭhapūjita’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaissasi gāmapūjitaṃ nigamapūjitaṃ nagarapūjitaṃ janapadapūjitaṃ raṭṭhapūjitaṃ! jīvasañ‍ñinau hi, maughapurisa, manussā rukkhasmiṃ| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

366. ‘‘mahal‍lakaṃ pana bhikkhunā vihāraṃ kārayamānaina sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ bhikkhū abhinaitabbā vatthudaisanāya| taihi bhikkhūhi vatthu daisaitabbaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ| sārambhai cai bhikkhu vatthusmiṃ aparik‍kamanai mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kāraiyya bhikkhū vā anabhinaiyya vatthudaisanāya, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

367. mahal‍lakau nāma vihārau sassāmikau vuc‍cati|

vihārau nāma ul‍littau vā hauti avalittau vā ul‍littāvalittau vā|

kārayamānaināti  karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā|

sassāmikanti añ‍ñau kauci sāmikau hauti itthī vā purisau vā gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā|

attuddaisanti attanau atthāya|

bhikkhū abhinaitabbā vatthudaisanāyāti taina vihārakārakaina bhikkhunā vihāravatthuṃ saudhaitvā saṅghaṃ  upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kattukāmau sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ vihāravatthuaulaukanaṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| sacai sabbau saṅghau ussahati vihāravatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ sabbaina saṅghaina aulaukaitabbaṃ| nau cai sabbau saṅghau ussahati vihāravatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ, yai tattha haunti bhikkhū byattā paṭibalā sārambhaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ  aparik‍kamanaṃ jānituṃ tai yācitvā samman‍nitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

368. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kattukāmau sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ vihāravatthuaulaukanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhu samman‍naiyya itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kattukāmau sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ vihāravatthuaulaukanaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhū samman‍nati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa ca itthan‍nāmassa ca bhikkhūnaṃ sammuti itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca bhikkhū itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ aulaukaituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

369. taihi  sammataihi bhikkhūhi tattha gantvā vihāravatthu aulaukaitabbaṃ; sārambhaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ jānitabbaṃ| sacai sārambhaṃ hauti aparik‍kamanaṃ, ‘māyidha karī’ti vattabbau| sacai anārambhaṃ hauti saparik‍kamanaṃ, saṅghassa āraucaitabbaṃ – ‘anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamana’nti| taina vihārakārakaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kattukāmau sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ vihāravatthudaisanaṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

370. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kattukāmau sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ vihāravatthudaisanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ daisaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kattukāmau sassāmikaṃ attuddaisaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ vihāravatthudaisanaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ daisaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthussa daisanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘daisitaṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau vihāravatthuṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

371. sārambhaṃ nāma kipil‍likānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, upacikānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, undūrānaṃ vā…pai॰… ahīnaṃ vā vicchikānaṃ vā satapadīnaṃ vā hatthīnaṃ vā assānaṃ vā sīhānaṃ vā byagghānaṃ vā dīpīnaṃ  vā acchānaṃ vā taracchānaṃ vā āsayau hauti, yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci tiracchānagatānaṃ pāṇānaṃ āsayau hauti, pubbaṇṇanissitaṃ vā hauti, aparaṇṇanissitaṃ vā hauti, abbhāghātanissitaṃ vā hauti, āghātananissitaṃ vā hauti, susānanissitaṃ vā hauti, uyyānanissitaṃ vā hauti, rājavatthunissitaṃ vā hauti, hatthisālānissitaṃ vā hauti, assasālānissitaṃ vā hauti, bandhanāgāranissitaṃ vā hauti, pānāgāranissitaṃ  vā hauti, sūnanissitaṃ vā hauti, racchānissitaṃ vā hauti, cac‍caranissitaṃ vā hauti, sabhānissitaṃ vā hauti, saṃsaraṇanissitaṃ vā hauti| aitaṃ sārambhaṃ nāma|

aparik‍kamanaṃ nāma na sak‍kā hauti yathāyuttaina sakaṭaina anuparigantuṃ, samantā nissaiṇiyā anuparigantuṃ| aitaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ nāma|

anārambhaṃ  nāma na kipil‍likānaṃ vā āsayau hauti…pai॰… na saṃsaraṇanissitaṃ vā hauti| aitaṃ anārambhaṃ nāma|

saparik‍kamanaṃ nāma sak‍kā hauti yathāyuttaina sakaṭaina anuparigantuṃ, samantā nissaiṇiyā anuparigantuṃ| aitaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ nāma|

mahal‍lakau nāma vihārau sassāmikau vuc‍cati|

vihārau nāma ul‍littau vā hauti avalittau vā ul‍littāvalittau vā|

kāraiyyāti karauti vā kārāpaiti vā|

bhikkhū vā anabhinaiyya vatthudaisanāyāti ñattidutiyaina kammaina vihāravatthuṃ na daisāpaitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai  duk‍kaṭaṃ| aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati – ‘saṅghādisaisau’ti|

372. bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhikkhu vihāraṃ karauti daisitavatthukaṃ anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

373. bhikkhu  samādisati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu samādisati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ  saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

374. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| na ca samādisati – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

375. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘vihārau kira mai kayirati adaisitavatthukau sārambhau aparik‍kamanau’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti  …pai॰… ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti  …pai॰… ‘‘daisitavatthukau hautū’’ti| nau cai sāmaṃ vā gacchaiyya dūtaṃ vā pahiṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau suṇāti – ‘‘vihārau kira mai kayirati daisitavatthukau sārambhau aparik‍kamanau’’ti| taina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘anārambhau ca hautu saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘anārambhau hautū’’ti ‘‘saparik‍kamanau hautū’’ti, anāpatti|

376. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa|

bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| samādisati ca – ‘‘daisitavatthukau ca hautu anārambhau ca saparik‍kamanau cā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti kārukānaṃ duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

377. bhikkhu samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sau vihārau añ‍ñassa vā dātabbau bhinditvā vā puna kātabbau| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu  samādisitvā pak‍kamati – ‘‘vihāraṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa vihāraṃ karaunti daisitavatthukaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| sau cai vippakatai āgacchati, taina bhikkhunā sau vihārau añ‍ñassa vā dātabbau bhinditvā vā puna kātabbau| nau cai añ‍ñassa vā dadaiyya bhinditvā vā puna kāraiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ…pai॰… sārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa…pai॰… anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ, anāpatti|

378. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

379. anāpatti laiṇai guhāya tiṇakuṭikāya añ‍ñassatthāya vāsāgāraṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbattha| anāpatti ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vihārakārasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. duṭṭhadausasikkhāpadaṃ

380. idaṃ vatthu cūḷava॰ 189 taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatā dabbaina mal‍laputtaina jātiyā sattavassaina arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti| yaṃ kiñ‍ci yañ‍ca kiñ‍ci (sī॰ ka॰) sāvakaina pattabbaṃ sabbaṃ taina anuppattaṃ hauti| natthi cassa kiñ‍ci uttari karaṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau| atha khau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘mayā khau jātiyā sattavassaina arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sāvakaina pattabbaṃ sabbaṃ mayā anuppattaṃ| natthi ca mai kiñ‍ci  uttari karaṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau| kin‍nu khau ahaṃ saṅghassa vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaiyya’’nti?

atha khau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaiyyaṃ bhattāni ca uddisaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā  dabbau mal‍laputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi mayā khau jātiyā sattavassaina arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ, yaṃ kiñ‍ci sāvakaina pattabbaṃ, sabbaṃ mayā anupattaṃ, natthi  ca mai kiñ‍ci uttari karaṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau, kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ saṅghassa vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaiyya’’nti| tassa mayhaṃ bhantai, aitadahausi yaṃnūnāhaṃ ‘‘saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaiyyaṃ bhattāni ca uddisaiyyanti| icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaituṃ bhattāni ca uddisitu’’nti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, dabba| taina hi tvaṃ, dabba, saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaihi bhattāni ca uddisā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakañ‍ca bhattuddaisakañ‍ca samman‍natu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ dabbau mal‍laputtau yācitabbau| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

381. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakañ‍ca bhattuddaisakañ‍ca  samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakañ‍ca bhattuddaisakañ‍ca samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa saināsanapañ‍ñāpakassa ca bhattuddaisakassa ca sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau ca bhattuddaisakau ca| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

382. sammatau  ca panāyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau sabhāgānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| yai tai bhikkhū suttantikā taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ  pañ‍ñapaiti – ‘‘tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ suttantaṃ saṅgāyissantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū vinayadharā taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – ‘‘tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vinayaṃ vinicchinissantī’’ti vinicchissantīti (ka॰)| yai tai bhikkhū dhammakathikā taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – ‘‘tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ sākacchissantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū jhāyinau taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – ‘‘tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ na byābādhissantī’’ti na byābāhissantīti (ka॰)| yai tai bhikkhū tiracchānakathikā kāyadaḷhibahulā kāyadaḍḍhibahulā (sī॰) viharanti taisampi aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – ‘‘imāyapimai āyasmantau ratiyā acchissantī’’ti| yaipi tai bhikkhū vikālai āgacchanti taisampi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā tainaiva ālaukaina saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| apisu bhikkhū sañ‍cic‍ca vikālai āgacchanti – ‘‘mayaṃ āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ passissāmā’’ti|

tai āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau dabba, saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti| tai āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘katthāyasmantā  icchanti, kattha pañ‍ñapaimī’’ti? tai sañ‍cic‍ca dūrai apadisanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau dabba, gijjhakūṭai pabbatai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, caurapapātai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, isigilipassai kāḷasilāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, vaibhārapassai sattapaṇṇiguhāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, sītavanai sappasauṇḍikapabbhārai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, gautamakakandarāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, tindukakandarāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, tapaudakandarāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, tapaudārāmai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, jīvakambavanai  saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, maddakucchismiṃ migadāyai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti|

taisaṃ āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā aṅguliyā jalamānāya puratau puratau gacchati| taipi tainaiva ālaukaina āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau gacchanti| taisaṃ āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – ‘‘ayaṃ mañ‍cau, idaṃ pīṭhaṃ, ayaṃ bhisi, idaṃ bimbauhanaṃ, idaṃ vac‍caṭṭhānaṃ, idaṃ passāvaṭṭhānaṃ, idaṃ pānīyaṃ, idaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ, ayaṃ  kattaradaṇḍau, idaṃ saṅghassa katikasaṇṭhānaṃ, imaṃ kālaṃ pavisitabbaṃ, imaṃ kālaṃ  nikkhamitabba’’nti| taisaṃ āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā punadaiva vaiḷuvanaṃ pac‍cāgacchati|

383. taina khau pana samayaina maittiyabhūmajakā bhummajakā (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhū navakā caiva haunti appapuñ‍ñā ca| yāni saṅghassa lāmakāni saināsanāni tāni taisaṃ pāpuṇanti lāmakāni ca bhattāni| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai manussā icchanti thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhisaṅkhārikaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ dātuṃ sappimpi tailampi uttaribhaṅgampi| maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ pana bhikkhūnaṃ pākatikaṃ dainti yathārandhaṃ kaṇājakaṃ bilaṅgadutiyaṃ| tai pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantā thairai bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘tumhākaṃ, āvusau, bhattaggai kiṃ ahausi? tumhākaṃ, āvusau, bhattaggai kiṃ ahausī’’ti? aikac‍cai thairā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau, sappi ahausi tailaṃ ahausi uttaribhaṅgaṃ ahausī’’ti| maittiyabhūmajakā pana bhikkhū aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau, na kiñ‍ci ahausi, pākatikaṃ yathārandhaṃ kaṇājakaṃ bilaṅgadutiya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati saṅghassa catuk‍kabhattaṃ daiti nic‍cabhattaṃ| sau bhattaggai saputtadārau upatiṭṭhitvā parivisati| añ‍ñai audanaina pucchanti, añ‍ñai sūpaina pucchanti, añ‍ñai tailaina pucchanti, añ‍ñai uttaribhaṅgaina pucchanti| taina khau pana samayaina kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau bhattaṃ svātanāya maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti| atha khau kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati ārāmaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| sau yaināyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kalyāṇabhattikaṃ gahapatiṃ  āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati āyasmatā dabbaina mal‍laputtaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa, bhantai, amhākaṃ gharai svātanāya bhattaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti? ‘‘maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ khau, gahapati, bhikkhūnaṃ tumhākaṃ  gharai svātanāya bhattaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti| atha khau kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati anattamanau ahausi – ‘‘kathañhi nāma pāpabhikkhū amhākaṃ gharai bhuñ‍jissantī’’ti! gharaṃ gantvā dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘yai, jai, svai bhattikā āgacchanti tai kauṭṭhakai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā kaṇājakaina bilaṅgadutiyaina parivisā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ ayyā’’ti khau sā dāsī kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau pac‍cassausi|

atha  khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū – ‘‘hiyyau khau, āvusau, amhākaṃ kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau gharai  bhattaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ, svai amhai kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati saputtadārau upatiṭṭhitvā parivisissati; añ‍ñai audanaina pucchissanti, añ‍ñai sūpaina pucchissanti, añ‍ñai tailaina pucchissanti, añ‍ñai uttaribhaṅgaina pucchissantī’’ti| tai tainaiva saumanassaina na cittarūpaṃ rattiyā supiṃsu| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaraṃ ādāya yaina kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasā khau sā dāsī maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna kauṭṭhakai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘nisīdatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau na tāva bhattaṃ siddhaṃ bhavissati! yathā yāva (sī॰) mayaṃ kauṭṭhakai nisīdaiyyāmā’’ti nisīdāpīyaiyyāmāti (sī॰), nisīdāpīyāmāti (syā॰)| atha khau sā dāsī kaṇājakaina bilaṅgadutiyaina upagacchi – ‘‘bhuñ‍jatha, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘mayaṃ khau, bhagini, nic‍cabhattikā’’ti| ‘‘jānāmi ayyā nic‍cabhattikāttha| apicāhaṃ hiyyauva gahapatinā āṇattā – ‘yai, jai, svai bhattikā āgacchanti tai kauṭṭhakai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā kaṇājakaina bilaṅgadutiyaina parivisā’ti| bhuñ‍jatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū – ‘‘hiyyau khau, āvusau, kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati ārāmaṃ agamāsi dabbassa mal‍laputtassa santikai| nissaṃsayaṃ khau mayaṃ dabbaina mal‍laputtaina gahapatinau antarai santikai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paribhin‍nā’’ti| tai tainaiva daumanassaina  na cittarūpaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ  piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantā ārāmaṃ gantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭisāmaitvā bahārāmakauṭṭhakai saṅghāṭipal‍latthikāya nisīdiṃsu tuṇhībhūtā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyantā appaṭibhānā|

atha  khau maittiyā bhikkhunī yaina maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi, ayyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau maittiyā bhikkhunī maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi, ayyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| ‘‘kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi? kissa maṃ ayyā nālapantī’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana tvaṃ, bhagini, amhai dabbaina mal‍laputtaina vihaiṭhīyamānai ajjhupaikkhasī’’ti? ‘‘kyāhaṃ, ayyā, karaumī’’ti? ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, bhagini, icchaiyyāsi aj‍jaiva bhagavā dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ nāsāpaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kyāhaṃ, ayyā, karaumi, kiṃ mayā sak‍kā kātu’’nti? ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhagini, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ|  yāyaṃ, bhantai, disā abhayā anītikā anupaddavā sāyaṃ disā sabhayā saītikā saupaddavā| yatau nivātaṃ tatau savātaṃ pavātaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)| udakaṃ mañ‍ñai ādittaṃ| ayyainamhi dabbaina mal‍laputtaina dūsitā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyā’’ti khau maittiyā bhikkhunī maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau maittiyā bhikkhunī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ| yāyaṃ, bhantai, disā abhayā anītikā anupaddavā sāyaṃ disā sabhayā saītikā saupaddavā| yatau nivātaṃ tatau savātaṃ| udakaṃ mañ‍ñai ādittaṃ! ayyainamhi dabbaina mal‍laputtaina dūsitā’’ti|

384. atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathāyaṃ bhikkhunī āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathāyaṃ bhikkhunī āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, dabba, dabbā aivaṃ nibbaiṭhainti| sacai tayā kataṃ katanti vadaihi, sacai tayā akataṃ akatanti vadaihī’’ti| ‘‘yatau ahaṃ, bhantai, jātau nābhijānāmi supinantainapi maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitā, pagaiva jāgarau’’ti! atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, maittiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ nāsaitha| imai ca bhikkhū anuyuñ‍jathā’’ti| idaṃ vatvā bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau tai bhikkhū maittiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ nāsaisuṃ| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘māvusau, maittiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ nāsaitha| na sā kiñ‍ci aparajjhati| amhaihi sā ussāhitā kupitaihi anattamanaihi cāvanādhippāyaihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ  pana tumhai, āvusau, āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaithā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau  bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

385. yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ duṭṭhau dausau appatītau amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiyya – ‘appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyya’nti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānau vā asamanuggāhīyamānau vā amūlakañ‍caiva taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti bhikkhu ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhāti, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

386. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

duṭṭhau dausauti kupitau anattamanau anabhiraddhau āhatacittau khilajātau|

appatītauti  taina ca kaupaina taina ca dausaina tāya ca anattamanatāya tāya ca anabhiraddhiyā appatītau hauti|

amūlakaṃ  nāma adiṭṭhaṃ asutaṃ aparisaṅkitaṃ|

pārājikaina dhammaināti catun‍naṃ añ‍ñataraina|

anuddhaṃsaiyyāti caudaiti vā caudāpaiti vā|

appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyyanti bhikkhubhāvā  cāvaiyyaṃ, samaṇadhammā cāvaiyyaṃ, sīlakkhandhā cāvaiyyaṃ, tapauguṇā cāvaiyyaṃ|

tatau  aparaina samayaināti yasmiṃ khaṇai anuddhaṃsitau hauti taṃ khaṇaṃ taṃ layaṃ taṃ muhuttaṃ vītivattai|

samanuggāhīyamānauti yaina vatthunā anuddhaṃsitau hauti tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ samanuggāhīyamānau|

asamanuggāhīyamānauti na kainaci vuc‍camānau|

adhikaraṇaṃ nāma cattāri adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

bhikkhu ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhātīti tucchakaṃ mayā bhaṇitaṃ, musā mayā bhaṇitaṃ, abhūtaṃ mayā bhaṇitaṃ, ajānantaina mayā bhaṇitaṃ|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

387. adiṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

asutassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘sutau mayā, pārājikaṃ  dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

aparisaṅkitassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

adiṭṭhassa  hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhau mayā sutau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

adiṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhau mayā parisaṅkitau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

adiṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhau mayā sutau ca parisaṅkitau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

asutassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘sutau mayā parisaṅkitau ca…pai॰… sutau mayā diṭṭhau ca…pai॰…  sutau mayā parisaṅkitau ca diṭṭhau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

aparisaṅkitassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitau mayā diṭṭhau ca…pai॰… parisaṅkitau mayā sutau ca…pai॰… parisaṅkitau mayā diṭṭhau ca sutau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

diṭṭhassa  hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘sutau mayā pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

diṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi’’…pai॰… ‘‘sutau mayā parisaṅkitau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

sutassa  hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi…pai॰…  diṭṭhau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi…pai॰… parisaṅkitau mayā diṭṭhau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

parisaṅkitassa  hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi…pai॰… sutau mayā, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi…pai॰… diṭṭhau mayā sutau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

diṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| diṭṭhai vaimatikau diṭṭhaṃ nau kappaiti diṭṭhaṃ nassarati diṭṭhaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… sutai vaimatikau sutaṃ nau kappaiti sutaṃ nassarati sutaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… parisaṅkitai vaimatikau parisaṅkitaṃ nau  kappaiti parisaṅkitaṃ nassarati parisaṅkitaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti| tañ‍cai caudaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitau mayā diṭṭhau ca…pai॰… parisaṅkitau mayā sutau ca…pai॰… parisaṅkitau mayā diṭṭhau ca sutau ca, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

388. adiṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

asutassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti…pai॰… aparisaṅkitassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

adiṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhausi sutausi…pai॰…  diṭṭhausi parisaṅkitausi…pai॰… diṭṭhausi sutausi parisaṅkitausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ  ajjhāpan‍nausi’’…pai॰… asutassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti…pai॰… aparisaṅkitassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitausi, diṭṭhausi…pai॰… parisaṅkitausi, sutausi…pai॰… parisaṅkitausi, diṭṭhausi, sutausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

diṭṭhassa  hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘sutausi’’…pai॰… tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitausi’’…pai॰… tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘sutausi, parisaṅkitausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

sutassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti…pai॰… parisaṅkitassa hauti – ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’’ti| tañ‍cai ‘‘diṭṭhausi’’…pai॰… tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘sutausi’’…pai॰… tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘diṭṭhausi, sutausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

diṭṭhassa hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| diṭṭhai vaimatikau diṭṭhaṃ nau kappaiti diṭṭhaṃ nassarati diṭṭhaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… sutai  vaimatikau sutaṃ nau kappaiti sutaṃ nassarati sutaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… parisaṅkitai vaimatikau parisaṅkitaṃ nau kappaiti parisaṅkitaṃ nassarati parisaṅkitaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti| tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitausi, diṭṭhausi’’…pai॰… parisaṅkitaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti, tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitausi sutausi’’…pai॰… parisaṅkitaṃ pamuṭṭhau hauti, tañ‍cai caudāpaiti – ‘‘parisaṅkitausi, diṭṭhausi, sutausi, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

389. asuddhai  suddhadiṭṭhi, suddhai asuddhadiṭṭhi, asuddhai asuddhadiṭṭhi, suddhai suddhadiṭṭhi|

asuddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa|

asuddhau  hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

asuddhau  hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādaina duk‍kaṭassa|

asuddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

suddhau  hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

suddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, anāpatti|

suddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādaina duk‍kaṭassa|

suddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

asuddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

asuddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, anāpatti|

asuddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādaina duk‍kaṭassa|

asuddhau  hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai asuddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā  ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

suddhau  hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa|

suddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

suddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau| tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādaina duk‍kaṭassa|

suddhau hauti puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ anajjhāpan‍nau tañ‍cai suddhadiṭṭhi samānau aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

390. anāpatti suddhai asuddhadiṭṭhissa, asuddhai asuddhadiṭṭhissa, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

duṭṭhadausasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyaduṭṭhadausasikkhāpadaṃ

391. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantā addasaṃsu chagalakaṃ chakalakaṃ (syā॰) ajikāya vippaṭipaj‍jantaṃ| disvāna  aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘handa mayaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ chagalakaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ nāma karauma| imaṃ ajikaṃ maittiyaṃ nāma bhikkhuniṃ karauma| aivaṃ mayaṃ vauharissāma| pubbai mayaṃ, āvusau, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ sutaina avaucumhā| idāni pana amhaihi sāmaṃ diṭṭhau maittiyāya bhikkhuniyā vippaṭipaj‍jantau’’ti| tai taṃ chagalakaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ nāma  akaṃsu| taṃ ajikaṃ maittiyaṃ nāma bhikkhuniṃ akaṃsu| tai bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisuṃ – ‘‘pubbai mayaṃ, āvusau, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ sutaina avaucumhā| idāni pana amhaihi sāmaṃ diṭṭhau maittiyāya bhikkhuniyā vippaṭipaj‍jantau’’ti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māvusau, aivaṃ avacuttha| nāyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ karissatī’’ti|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathayimai bhikkhū āhaṃsū’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathayimai bhikkhū āhaṃsū’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, dabba, dabbā aivaṃ nibbaiṭhainti| sacai tayā kataṃ katanti vadaihi, sacai tayā akataṃ akatanti vadaihī’’ti| ‘‘yatau ahaṃ, bhantai, jātau nābhijānāmi supinantainapi maithunadhammaṃ paṭisaivitā, pagaiva jāgarau’’ti! atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhuvai, imai bhikkhū anuyuñ‍jathā’’ti| idaṃ vatvā bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau tai bhikkhū maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū anuyuñ‍jiṃsu| tai bhikkhūhi anuyuñ‍jīyamānā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaithā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ  laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

392. ‘‘yau  pana bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ duṭṭhau dausau appatītau añ‍ñabhāgiyassa  adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiyya – ‘appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyā cāvaiyya’nti| tatau  aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānau vā asamanuggāhīyamānau vā añ‍ñabhāgiyañ‍caiva taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti kaucidaisau laisamattau upādin‍nau, bhikkhu ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhāti, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

393. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhunti añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

duṭṭhau dausauti kupitau anattamanau anabhiraddhau āhatacittau khilajātau|

appatītauti taina ca kaupaina, taina ca dausaina, tāya ca anattamanatāya, tāya ca anabhiraddhiyā appatītau hauti|

añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassāti āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā hauti adhikaraṇañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ vā| kathaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇassa āpattādhikaraṇassa kic‍cādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇassa kic‍cādhikaraṇassa vivādādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ| āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇassa vivādādhikaraṇassa anuvādādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ| kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇassa anuvādādhikaraṇassa  āpattādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ| aivaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ|

kathaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ| āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇassa siyā tabbhāgiyaṃ siyā añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ|

kathaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ? maithunadhammapārājikāpatti adin‍nādānapārājikāpattiyā  manussaviggahapārājikāpattiyā uttarimanussadhammapārājikāpattiyā añ‍ñabhāgiyā| adin‍nādānapārājikāpatti manussaviggahapārājikāpattiyā uttarimanussadhammapārājikāpattiyā maithunadhammapārājikāpattiyā añ‍ñabhāgiyā| manussaviggahapārājikāpatti uttarimanussadhammapārājikāpattiyā maithunadhammapārājikāpattiyā adin‍nādānapārājikāpattiyā  añ‍ñabhāgiyā| uttarimanussadhammapārājikāpatti maithunadhammapārājikāpattiyā adin‍nādānapārājikāpattiyā manussaviggahapārājikāpattiyā añ‍ñabhāgiyā| aivaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ|

kathaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ? maithunadhammapārājikāpatti maithunadhammapārājikāpattiyā tabbhāgiyā| adin‍nādānapārājikāpatti adin‍nādānapārājikāpattiyā tabbhāgiyā| manussaviggahapārājikāpatti manussaviggahapārājikāpattiyā tabbhāgiyā| uttarimanussadhammapārājikāpatti uttarimanussadhammapārājikāpattiyā  tabbhāgiyā| aivaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ| aivaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyaṃ|

394. kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāyāti laisau nāma dasa laisā – jātilaisau, nāmalaisau, gauttalaisau, liṅgalaisau, āpattilaisau, pattalaisau, cīvaralaisau, upajjhāyalaisau, ācariyalaisau, saināsanalaisau|

395. jātilaisau nāma khattiyau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ khattiyaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘khattiyau mayā diṭṭhau aittha ‘‘pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau’’ti pāṭhau ūnau mañ‍ñai, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ hi ‘‘khattiyau mayā diṭṭhau pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau’’ti dissati| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

brāhmaṇau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… vaissau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… suddau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ suddaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘suddau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

396. nāmalaisau nāma buddharakkhitau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… dhammarakkhitau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… saṅgharakkhitau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ saṅgharakkhitaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘saṅgharakkhitau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

397. gauttalaisau nāma gautamau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… mauggal‍lānau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… kac‍cāyanau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… vāsiṭṭhau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ vāsiṭṭhaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘vāsiṭṭhau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti  vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

398. liṅgalaisau nāma dīghau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… rassau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… kaṇhau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… audātau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ audātaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘audātau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

399. āpattilaisau nāma lahukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

400. pattalaisau nāma lauhapattadharau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… sāṭakapattadharau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… sumbhakapattadharau  diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ sumbhakapattadharaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘sumbhakapattadharau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

401. cīvaralaisau nāma paṃsukūlikau diṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… gahapaticīvaradharau diṭṭhau hauti  pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ gahapaticīvaradharaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘gahapaticīvaradharau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

402. upajjhāyalaisau  nāma itthan‍nāmassa saddhivihārikau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ itthan‍nāmassa saddhivihārikaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmassa saddhivihārikau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

403. ācariyalaisau nāma itthan‍nāmassa antaivāsikau diṭṭhau hauti pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ itthan‍nāmassa antaivāsikaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmassa antaivāsikau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi’’…pai॰… āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

404. saināsanalaisau nāma itthan‍nāmasaināsanavāsikau diṭṭhau hauti  pārājikaṃ  dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau| añ‍ñaṃ itthan‍nāmasaināsanavāsikaṃ passitvā caudaiti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmasaināsanavāsikau mayā diṭṭhau| pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nausi, assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

405. pārājikaina dhammaināti catun‍naṃ añ‍ñataraina|

anuddhaṃsaiyyāti caudaiti vā caudāpaiti vā|

appaiva nāma naṃ imamhā brahmacariyācāvaiyyanti bhikkhubhāvā cāvaiyyaṃ, samaṇadhammā cāvaiyyaṃ, sīlakkhandhā cāvaiyyaṃ, tapauguṇā cāvaiyyaṃ|

tatau aparaina samayaināti yasmiṃ khaṇai anuddhaṃsitau hauti, taṃ khaṇaṃ taṃ layaṃ taṃ muhuttaṃ vītivattai|

samanuggāhīyamānauti  yaina vatthunā anuddhaṃsitau hauti tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ samanuggāhīyamānau|

asamanuggāhīyamānauti na kainaci vuc‍camānau|

adhikaraṇaṃ nāma cattāri adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

kauci  daisau laisamattau upādin‍nauti taisaṃ dasan‍naṃ laisānaṃ añ‍ñatarau laisau upādin‍nau hauti|

bhikkhu ca dausaṃ patiṭṭhātīti tucchakaṃ mayā bhaṇitaṃ, musā mayā bhaṇitaṃ, abhūtaṃ mayā bhaṇitaṃ, ajānantaina mayā bhaṇitaṃ|

saṅghādisaisauti  …pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

406. bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti, saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti, saṅghādisaisai thul‍lac‍cayadiṭṭhi hauti…pai॰… pācittiyadiṭṭhi hauti… pāṭidaisanīyadiṭṭhi hauti… duk‍kaṭadiṭṭhi hauti… dubbhāsitadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu thul‍lac‍cayaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti thul‍lac‍cayai thul‍lac‍cayadiṭṭhi hauti…pai॰… thul‍lac‍cayai pācittiyadiṭṭhi hauti… pāṭidaisanīyadiṭṭhi  hauti… duk‍kaṭadiṭṭhi hauti… dubbhāsitadiṭṭhi hauti… saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi’’  …pai॰… aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti, dubbhāsitai dubbhāsitadiṭṭhi hauti…pai॰… dubbhāsitai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhi hauti… thul‍lac‍cayadiṭṭhi hauti… pācittiyadiṭṭhi hauti… pāṭidaisanīyadiṭṭhi hauti… duk‍kaṭadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

aikaikaṃ mūlaṃ kātuna cak‍kaṃ bandhitabbaṃ|

407. bhikkhu  saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudāpaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti, saṅghādisaisai thul‍lac‍cayadiṭṭhi  hauti…pai॰… pācittiyadiṭṭhi hauti… pāṭidaisanīyadiṭṭhi hauti… duk‍kaṭadiṭṭhi hauti… dubbhāsitadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudāpaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu thul‍lac‍cayaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti thul‍lac‍cayai thul‍lac‍cayadiṭṭhi hauti…pai॰… thul‍lac‍cayai pācittiyadiṭṭhi hauti… pāṭidaisanīyadiṭṭhi hauti… duk‍kaṭadiṭṭhi hauti… dubbhāsitadiṭṭhi hauti… saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudāpaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi’’…pai॰… aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhu pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau hauti dubbhāsitai dubbhāsitadiṭṭhi hauti…pai॰… dubbhāsitai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhi hauti… thul‍lac‍cayadiṭṭhi  hauti… pācittiyadiṭṭhi hauti… pāṭidaisanīyadiṭṭhi hauti… duk‍kaṭadiṭṭhi hauti| tañ‍cai pārājikaina caudāpaiti – ‘‘assamaṇausi, asakyaputtiyausi, natthi tayā saddhiṃ upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā saṅghakammaṃ vā’’ti, aivampi āpattañ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ hauti laisau ca upādin‍nau, āpatti vācāya, vācāya saṅghādisaisassa|

408. anāpatti tathāsañ‍ñī caudaiti vā caudāpaiti vā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyaduṭṭhadausasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. saṅghabhaidasikkhāpadaṃ

409. idaṃ vatthu cūḷava॰ 343 taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau daivadattau yaina kaukālikau kaṭamaudakatissakau kaṭamaurakatissakau (sī॰ syā॰) khaṇḍadaiviyā puttau samuddadattau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kaukālikaṃ kaṭamaudakatissakaṃ khaṇḍadaiviyā puttaṃ samuddadattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitha mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇassa gautamassa saṅghabhaidaṃ karissāma cak‍kabhaida’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai kaukālikau daivadattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘samaṇau khau, āvusau, gautamau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau| kathaṃ mayaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa saṅghabhaidaṃ karissāma cak‍kabhaida’’nti? ‘‘aitha mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ca vatthūni yācissāma – ‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vīriyārabbhassa (ka॰) vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya santuṭṭhiyā sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ ausaraiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ piṇḍapātikā assu; yau nimantanaṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ paṃsukūlikā assu; yau gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ rukkhamūlikā assu; yau chan‍naṃ upagacchaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ  na khādaiyyuṃ; yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’ti| imāni samaṇau gautamau nānujānissati| tai mayaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi  vatthūhi janaṃ sañ‍ñāpaissāmāti| sak‍kā khau, āvusau, imaihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi samaṇassa gautamassa saṅghabhaidau kātuṃ cak‍kabhaidau| lūkhapasan‍nā hi, āvusau, manussā’’ti|

atha khau daivadattau saparisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau daivadattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa…pai॰… vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya…pai॰… vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū  yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ ausaraiyya vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya…pai॰… yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ na khādaiyyuṃ, yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, daivadatta, yau icchati ārañ‍ñikau hautu, yau icchati gāmantai viharatu; yau icchati piṇḍapātikau  hautu, yau  icchati nimantanaṃ sādiyatu; yau icchati paṃsukūlikau hautu, yau icchati gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyatu| aṭṭhamāsai khau mayā, daivadatta, rukkhamūlasaināsanaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, tikauṭiparisuddhaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ – adiṭṭhaṃ asutaṃ aparisaṅkita’’nti| atha khau daivadattau – ‘‘na bhagavā imāni pañ‍ca vatthūni anujānātī’’ti haṭṭhau udaggau saparisau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

410. atha khau daivadattau saparisau rājagahaṃ pavisitvā pañ‍cahi vatthūhi janaṃ sañ‍ñāpaisi – ‘‘mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ca vatthūni yācimhā – ‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya santuṭṭhiyā sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ ausaraiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ piṇḍapātikā assu; yau nimantanaṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ paṃsukūlikā assu; yau gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ rukkhamūlikā assu; yau chan‍naṃ upagacchaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ na khādaiyyuṃ; yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’ti| imāni samaṇau gautamau nānujānāti| tai mayaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi samādāya vattāmā’’ti| tattha yai tai manussā assaddhā appasan‍nā dubbuddhinau tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhutā sal‍laikhavuttinau, samaṇau pana gautamau bāhul‍likau bāhul‍lāya caitaitī’’ti| yai  pana tai manussā saddhā pasan‍nā paṇḍitā byattā buddhimantau tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivadattau bhagavatau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamissati cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti  – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivadattau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamissati cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū daivadattaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, daivadatta, saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamasi cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamissasi cak‍kabhaidāya! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

411. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kamaiyya, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha tiṭṭhaiyya, sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘māyasmā samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā  adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha aṭṭhāsi| samaitāyasmā saṅghaina| samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’ti| aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva paggaṇhaiyya , sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbau tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānau taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyya, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

412. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

samaggau nāma saṅghau samānasaṃvāsakau samānasīmāyaṃ ṭhitau|

bhaidāya parak‍kamaiyyāti – ‘‘kathaṃ imai nānā assu, vinā assu, vaggā assū’’ti pakkhaṃ pariyaisati, gaṇaṃ bandhati|

bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇanti aṭṭhārasabhaidakaravatthūni|

samādāyāti  ādāya|

paggayhāti dīpaiyya|

tiṭṭhaiyyāti na paṭinissaj‍jaiyya|

sau bhikkhūti yau sau saṅghabhaidakau bhikkhu|

bhikkhūhīti añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi|

yai  passanti, yai suṇanti, taihi vattabbau – ‘‘māyasmā samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha aṭṭhāsi| samaitāyasmā saṅghaina| samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbau| tatiyampi vattabbau| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbau – ‘‘māyasmā samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, bhaidanasaṃvattanikaṃ vā adhikaraṇaṃ samādāya paggayha aṭṭhāsi| samaitāyasmā saṅghaina| samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbau| tatiyampi vattabbau| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sau bhikkhu samanubhāsitabbau – ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

413. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kamati| sau taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kamati| sau taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ  bhikkhuṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa  paṭinissaggāya| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kamati| sau taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassāyasmatau  khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘samanubhaṭṭhau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

414. ñattiyā  duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā, kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantassa ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā paṭippassambhanti|

saṅghādisaisauti…pai॰… tainapi vuc‍cati saṅghādisaisauti|

415. dhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dhammakammai vaimatikau na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

adhammakammai dhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

adhammakammai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

adhammakammai adhammakammasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

416. anāpatti  asamanubhāsantassa, paṭinissaj‍jantassa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

saṅghabhaidasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

11. bhaidānuvattakasikkhāpadaṃ

417. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina daivadattau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamati cak‍kabhaidāya| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘adhammavādī daivadattau, avinayavādī daivadattau| kathañhi nāma daivadattau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamissati cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti! aivaṃ vuttai kaukālikau kaṭamaudakatissakau khaṇḍadaiviyā puttau samuddadattau tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘māyasmantau aivaṃ avacuttha| dhammavādī  daivadattau, vinayavādī daivadattau| amhākañ‍ca daivadattau chandañ‍ca ruciñ‍ca ādāya vauharati jānāti nau bhāsati amhākaṃpaitaṃ khamatī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū daivadattassa  saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā bhavissanti vaggavādakā’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anuvattakai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū daivadattassa saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā haunti vaggavādakā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā daivadattassa saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā bhavissanti vaggavādakā! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

418. ‘‘tassaiva khau pana bhikkhussa bhikkhū haunti anuvattakā vaggavādakā aikau vā dvai vā tayau vā| tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘māyasmantau aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ kiñ‍ci avacuttha| dhammavādī caisau bhikkhu, vinayavādī caisau bhikkhu| amhākañ‍caisau bhikkhu chandañ‍ca ruciñ‍ca ādāya vauharati jānāti nau bhāsati amhākampaitaṃ khamatī’ti, tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘māyasmantau aivaṃ avacuttha, na caisau bhikkhu dhammavādī, na caisau bhikkhu vinayavādī, māyasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha, samaitāyasmantānaṃ saṅghaina, samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau  avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’ti| aivañ‍ca tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānā tathaiva paggaṇhaiyyuṃ, tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsitabbā tassa paṭinissaggāya| yāvatatiyañ‍cai samanubhāsiyamānā taṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ, nau cai paṭinissaj‍jaiyyuṃ, saṅghādisaisau’’ti|

419. tassaiva khau panāti tassa saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau|

bhikkhū hauntīti añ‍ñai bhikkhū haunti|

anuvattakāti yaṃdiṭṭhikau sau hauti yaṃkhantikau yaṃrucikau taipi taṃdiṭṭhikā haunti taṃkhantikā taṃrucikā|

vaggavādakāti tassa vaṇṇāya pakkhāya ṭhitā haunti|

aikau vā dvai vā tayau vāti aikau vā hauti dvai vā tayau vā| tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘‘māyasmantau aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ kiñ‍ci avacuttha, dhammavādī caisau  bhikkhu, vinayavādī caisau bhikkhu, amhākañ‍caisau bhikkhu chandañ‍ca ruciñ‍ca ādāya vauharati  jānāti nau bhāsati amhākaṃpaitaṃ khamatī’ti|

tai bhikkhūti yai tai anuvattakā bhikkhū|

bhikkhūhīti añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi|

yai passanti yai suṇanti taihi vattabbā – ‘‘māyasmantau aivaṃ avacuttha| na caisau bhikkhu dhammavādī, na caisau bhikkhu vinayavādī| māyasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha| samaitāyasmantānaṃ saṅghaina| samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍janti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sutvā na vadanti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tai bhikkhū saṅghamajjhampi ākaḍḍhitvā vattabbā – ‘‘māyasmantau aivaṃ avacuttha| na caisau  bhikkhu dhammavādī, na caisau bhikkhu vinayavādī| māyasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha| samaitāyasmantānaṃ saṅghaina| samaggau hi saṅghau sammaudamānau avivadamānau aikuddaisau phāsu viharatī’’ti| dutiyampi vattabbā| tatiyampi vattabbā| sacai paṭinissaj‍janti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tai bhikkhū samanubhāsitabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samanubhāsitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

420. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau ca itthanāmau ca bhikkhū itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā vaggavādakā| tai taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍janti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhū samanubhāsaiyya tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca bhikkhū itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā vaggavādakā| tai taṃ vatthuṃ na paṭinissaj‍janti| saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhū samanubhāsati tassa vatthussa paṭinissaggāya| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa ca itthan‍nāmassa ca bhikkhūnaṃ samanubhāsanā tassa vatthussa  paṭinissaggāya, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|
