517. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū sākaitā sāvatthiṃ 2 addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| antarāmaggai caurā nikkhamitvā tai bhikkhū acchindiṃsu| atha khau tai bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti, kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā  na viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ| yathānaggāva sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhū abhivādainti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘sundarā khau imai, āvusau, ājīvakā yai imai bhikkhūsu abhivādaintī’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, ājīvakā, bhikkhū maya’’nti| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘iṅghāvusau upāli, imai anuyuñ‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau āyasmatā upālinā anuyuñ‍jiyamānā tai bhikkhū aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau āyasmā upāli tai bhikkhū anuyuñ‍jitvā bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhū imai, āvusau| daitha naisaṃ cīvarānī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū naggā āgacchissanti! nanu nāma tiṇaina vā paṇṇaina vā paṭicchādaitvā āgantabba’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, acchin‍nacīvarassa vā naṭṭhacīvarassa vā añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaituṃ| yaṃ āvāsaṃ paṭhamaṃ upagacchati, sacai tattha hauti saṅghassa vihāracīvaraṃ vā uttarattharaṇaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā bhisicchavi vā, taṃ gahaitvā pārupituṃ labhitvā audahissāmī’’ti| nau cai hauti saṅghassa vihāracīvaraṃ vā uttarattharaṇaṃ vā bhumattharaṇaṃ vā  bhisicchavi vā tiṇaina vā paṇṇaina vā paṭicchādaitvā āgantabbaṃ; na tvaiva naggaina āgantabbaṃ| yau āgacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

518. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiyya, añ‍ñatra samayā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| tatthāyaṃ samayau – acchin‍nacīvarau vā hauti bhikkhu naṭṭhacīvarau vā| ayaṃ tattha samayau’’ti|

519. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

añ‍ñātakau nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhau|

gahapati nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

gahapatānī nāma yā kāci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

cīvaraṃ  nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

añ‍ñatra samayāti ṭhapaitvā samayaṃ|

acchin‍nacīvarau nāma bhikkhussa cīvaraṃ acchin‍naṃ hauti rājūhi vā cauraihi vā dhuttaihi vā, yaihi kaihici vā acchin‍naṃ hauti|

naṭṭhacīvarau nāma bhikkhussa cīvaraṃ agginā vā daḍḍhaṃ hauti, udakaina vā vuḷhaṃ hauti, undūraihi vā upacikāhi vā khāyitaṃ hauti, paribhaugajiṇṇaṃ vā hauti|

añ‍ñatra samayā viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ  saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā  puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ, añ‍ñatra samayā viñ‍ñāpitaṃ, nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

520. añ‍ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai vaimatikau, añ‍ñatra samayā, cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī, añ‍ñatra samayā, cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

521. anāpatti samayai, ñātakānaṃ, pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

añ‍ñātakaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatuttarisikkhāpadaṃ

522. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū acchin‍nacīvarakai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘bhagavatā, āvusau, anuñ‍ñātaṃ – ‘acchin‍nacīvarassa vā naṭṭhacīvarassa vā añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaituṃ’; viñ‍ñāpaitha, āvusau, cīvara’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, laddhaṃ amhaihi cīvara’’nti| ‘‘mayaṃ āyasmantānaṃ viñ‍ñāpaimā’’ti| ‘‘viñ‍ñāpaitha, āvusau’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘acchin‍nacīvarakā, āvusau, bhikkhū āgatā| daitha naisaṃ cīvarānī’’ti, bahuṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ|

taina  khau pana  samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau sabhāyaṃ nisin‍nau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchin‍nacīvarakā ayyau bhikkhū āgatā| taisaṃ mayā cīvaraṃ din‍na’’nti| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘mayāpi din‍na’’nti| aparaupi aivamāha – ‘‘mayāpi din‍na’’nti| tai ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaissanti, dussavāṇij‍jaṃ  vā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā karissanti, paggāhikasālaṃ paṭaggāhikasālaṃ (?) vā pasāraissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

523. ‘‘tañ‍cai añ‍ñātakau gahapati vā gahapatānī vā bahūhi cīvaraihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyya santaruttaraparamaṃ taina bhikkhunā tatau cīvaraṃ sāditabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

524. tañ‍caiti acchin‍nacīvarakaṃ bhikkhuṃ|

añ‍ñātakau nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhau|

gahapati nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

gahapatānī nāma yā kāci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

bahūhi cīvaraihīti bahukaihi cīvaraihi|

abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyāti  yāvatakaṃ icchasi tāvatakaṃ gaṇhāhīti|

santaruttaraparamaṃ  taina bhikkhunā tatau cīvaraṃ sāditabbanti sacai tīṇi naṭṭhāni haunti dvai sāditabbāni, dvai naṭṭhāni aikaṃ sāditabbaṃ, aikaṃ naṭṭhaṃ na kiñ‍ci sāditabbaṃ|

tatau cai uttari sādiyaiyyāti tatuttari viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ añ‍ñātakaṃ  gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tatuttari viñ‍ñāpitaṃ  nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

525. añ‍ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī tatuttari cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai vaimatikau tatuttari cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī tatuttari cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

526. anāpatti – ‘‘saisakaṃ āharissāmī’’ti harantau gacchati, ‘‘saisakaṃ tuyhaiva hautū’’ti dainti, na acchin‍nakāraṇā dainti, na naṭṭhakāraṇā  dainti, ñātakānaṃ, pavāritānaṃ, attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

tatuttarisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. upakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

527. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| assausi khau añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu tassa purisassa imaṃ vācaṃ bhāsamānassa| atha khau sau bhikkhu yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahāpuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, āvusau upananda, amukasmiṃ aukāsai añ‍ñatarau purisau pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’’ti| ‘‘atthāvusau, maṃ sau upaṭṭhākau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau yaina sau purisau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira maṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, cīvaraina acchādaitukāmausī’’ti? ‘‘api maiyya, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau maṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, cīvaraina acchādaitukāmausi, aivarūpaina cīvaraina acchādaihi| kyāhaṃ taina acchan‍naupi karissāmi yāhaṃ na paribhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti|

atha  khau sau purisau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘mahicchā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā asantuṭṭhā| nayimai sukarā cīvaraina acchādaituṃ| kathañhi  nāma ayyau upanandau mayā pubbai appavāritau maṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa purisassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ …pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, pubbai appavāritau gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātakau tai, upananda, añ‍ñātakau’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātakassa na jānāti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā santaṃ vā asantaṃ vā| tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

528. ‘‘bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa añ‍ñātakassa gahapatissa vā gahapatāniyā vā cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ cīvaracaitāpanaṃ (syā॰) upakkhaṭaṃ hauti – ‘iminā  cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’ti; tatra cai sau bhikkhu pubbai appavāritau upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya – ‘sādhu vata maṃ āyasmā iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā acchādaihī’ti, kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

529. bhikkhuṃ  panaiva uddissāti bhikkhussatthāya, bhikkhuṃ ārammaṇaṃ karitvā, bhikkhuṃ acchādaitukāmau|

añ‍ñātakau nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhau|

gahapati  nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

gahapatānī nāma yā kāci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ nāma hirañ‍ñaṃ vā suvaṇṇaṃ vā muttā vā maṇi vā pavāḷau vā phalikau vā paṭakau vā suttaṃ vā kappāsau vā|

iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naināti pac‍cupaṭṭhitaina|

caitāpaitvāti parivattaitvā|

acchādaissāmīti dassāmi|

tatra cai sau bhikkhūti yaṃ bhikkhuṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ upakkhaṭaṃ hauti sau bhikkhu|

pubbai appavāritauti pubbai avuttau hauti – ‘‘kīdisaina tai, bhantai, cīvaraina atthau, kīdisaṃ tai cīvaraṃ caitāpaimī’’ti?

upasaṅkamitvāti gharaṃ gantvā yattha katthaci upasaṅkamitvā  |

cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti āyataṃ vā hautu vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā saṇhaṃ vā|

iminā  cīvaracaitāpan‍naināti pac‍cupaṭṭhitaina|

aivarūpaṃ  vā aivarūpaṃ vāti| āyataṃ vā vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā saṇhaṃ vā|

caitāpaitvāti parivattaitvā|

acchādaihīti daj‍jaihi|

kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāyāti sādhatthikau sādhutthikau (syā॰) mahagghatthikau| tassa vacanaina āyataṃ vā vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā saṇhaṃ vā caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpan‍naṃ nissaggiyaṃ, imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

530. añ‍ñātakai  añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī pubbai appavāritau gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai vaimatikau pubbai appavāritau gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī pubbai appavāritau gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

531. anāpatti  ñātakānaṃ, pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, mahagghaṃ caitāpaitukāmassa appagghaṃ caitāpaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

upakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyaupakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ

532. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘ahampi ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| assausi khau añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu taisaṃ purisānaṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau sau bhikkhu yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahāpuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, āvusau upananda| amukasmiṃ aukāsai  añ‍ñatarau purisau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’ti| saupi aivamāha – ‘ahampi ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’’ti| ‘‘atthāvusau, maṃ tai upaṭṭhākā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau yaina tai purisā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai purisai aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira maṃ tumhai, āvusau, cīvaraihi acchādaitukāmātthā’’ti? ‘‘api nayya, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraihi acchādaissāmā’’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau maṃ tumhai, āvusau, cīvaraihi acchādaitukāmāttha, aivarūpaina cīvaraina acchādaitha, kyāhaṃ taihi acchan‍naupi karissāmi, yānāhaṃ na paribhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti| atha khau tai purisā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘mahicchā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā asantuṭṭhā| nayimai sukarā cīvaraihi acchādaituṃ| kathañhi nāma ayyau upanandau amhaihi pubbai appavāritau upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti!

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ purisānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, pubbai appavāritau gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātakā  tai, upananda, añ‍ñātakā’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātakā, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātakānaṃ na jānāti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā santaṃ vā asantaṃ vā| tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

533. ‘‘bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa ubhin‍naṃ añ‍ñātakānaṃ gahapatīnaṃ vā gahapatānīnaṃ vā pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍nāni upakkhaṭāni haunti – ‘imaihi mayaṃ pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihi pac‍caikacīvarāni caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaraihi acchādaissāmā’ti; tatra cai sau bhikkhu pubbai appavāritau upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya – ‘sādhu vata maṃ āyasmantau imaihi pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihi aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā acchādaitha, ubhauva santā aikainā’ti, kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

534. bhikkhuṃ  panaiva uddissāti bhikkhussatthāya, bhikkhuṃ ārammaṇaṃ karitvā, bhikkhuṃ acchādaitukāmā|

ubhin‍nanti  dvin‍naṃ|

añ‍ñātakā nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhā|

gahapatī nāma yai kaici agāra ajjhāvasanti|

gahapatāniyau nāma yā kāci agāraṃ ajjhāvasanti|

cīvaracaitāpan‍nāni nāma hirañ‍ñā vā suvaṇṇā vā muttā vā maṇī vā pavāḷā vā phalikā vā paṭakā vā suttā vā kappāsā vā|

imaihi pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihiti pac‍cupaṭṭhitaihi|

caitāpaitvāti  parivattaitvā|

acchādaissāmāti dassāma|

tatra cai sau bhikkhūti yaṃ bhikkhuṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍nāni upakkhaṭāni haunti sau bhikkhu|

pubbai appavāritauti pubbai avuttau hauti – ‘‘kīdisaina tai, bhantai, cīvaraina atthau, kīdisaṃ tai  cīvaraṃ caitāpaimā’’ti|

upasaṅkamitvāti gharaṃ gantvā yattha katthaci upasaṅkamitvā|

cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti āyataṃ vā hautu vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā saṇhaṃ vā|

imaihi pac‍caikacīvaracaitāpan‍naihīti pac‍cupaṭṭhitaihi|

aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vāti āyataṃ vā vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā saṇhaṃ vā|

caitāpaitvāti parivattaitvā|

acchādaithāti daj‍jaitha|

ubhauva santā aikaināti dvaipi janā aikaina|

kalyāṇakamyataṃ upādāyāti sādhatthikau mahagghatthikau|

tassa vacanaina āyataṃ vā vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā saṇhaṃ vā caitāpainti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ pubbai appavāritau  añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpan‍naṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa  nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

535. añ‍ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī pubbai appavāritau gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai vaimatikau pubbai appavāritau gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī pubbai appavāritau gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ  āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

536. anāpatti – ñātakānaṃ, pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, mahagghaṃ caitāpaitukāmānaṃ appagghaṃ caitāpaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dutiyaupakkhaṭasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. rājasikkhāpadaṃ

537. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa upaṭṭhākau mahāmattau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa dūtaina cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaihī’’ti| atha khau sau dūtau yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ| paṭiggaṇhātu āyasmā cīvaracaitāpan‍na’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai  āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau taṃ dūtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, cīvarañ‍ca khau mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma kālaina kappiya’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai sau dūtau āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi panāyasmatau  kauci vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti? taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau upāsakau ārāmaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau taṃ dūtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisau khau, āvusau, upāsakau bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti| atha khau sau dūtau taṃ upāsakaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmā vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ niddisi sañ‍ñattau sau mayā| upasaṅkamatu āyasmā kālaina, cīvaraina taṃ acchādaissatī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sau mahāmattau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘paribhuñ‍jatu ayyau taṃ cīvaraṃ, icchāma mayaṃ ayyaina taṃ cīvaraṃ paribhutta’’nti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau taṃ upāsakaṃ na kiñ‍ci avacāsi| dutiyampi khau sau mahāmattau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘paribhuñ‍jatu ayyau taṃ cīvaraṃ, icchāma mayaṃ ayyaina taṃ cīvaraṃ paribhutta’’nti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau taṃ upāsakaṃ na kiñ‍ci avacāsi| tatiyampi khau sau mahāmattau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘paribhuñ‍jatu ayyau taṃ cīvaraṃ, icchāma mayaṃ ayyaina taṃ cīvaraṃ paribhutta’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina naigamassa samayau hauti| naigamaina ca katikā katā hauti – ‘‘yau pacchā āgacchati pañ‍ñāsaṃ baddhau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau yaina sau upāsakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthau mai, āvusau, cīvarainā’’ti| ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaihi, aj‍ja naigamassa samayau| naigamaina ca katikā katā hauti – ‘yau pacchā āgacchati pañ‍ñāsaṃ baddhau’’’ti| ‘‘aj‍jaiva mai, āvusau, cīvaraṃ daihī’’ti auvaṭṭikāya parāmasi| atha khau sau upāsakau āyasmatā upanandaina sakyaputtaina nippīḷiyamānau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā pacchā agamāsi| manussā taṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ayyau, pacchā āgatau, pañ‍ñāsaṃ jīnausī’’ti|

atha khau sau upāsakau taisaṃ manussānaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘mahicchā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā asantuṭṭhā| nayimaisaṃ  sukaraṃ vaiyyāvac‍campi kātuṃ| kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau upāsakaina – ‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaihī’ti  vuc‍camānau nāgamaissatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau upāsakaina – ‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaihī’ti vuc‍camānau nāgamaissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, upāsakaina – ‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaihī’ti vuc‍camānau nāgamaisī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, upāsakaina – ‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaihī’ti vuc‍camānau nāgamaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

538. ‘‘bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa rājā vā rājabhauggau vā brāhmaṇau vā gahapatikau vā dūtaina cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pahiṇaiyya – ‘iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naina cīvaraṃ caitāpaitvā itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaraina acchādaihī’ti| sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘idaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ, paṭiggaṇhātu āyasmā cīvaracaitāpan‍na’nti, taina bhikkhunā sau dūtau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| cīvarañ‍ca khau mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, kālaina kappiya’nti| sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthi panāyasmatau kauci vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’ti, cīvaratthikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau niddisitabbau ārāmikau vā upāsakau vā – ‘aisau khau, āvusau, bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’ti| sau cai dūtau taṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmā vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ niddisi sañ‍ñattau sau mayā, upasaṅkamatu āyasmā kālaina, cīvaraina taṃ acchādaissatī’ti, cīvaratthikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau upasaṅkamitvā dvattikkhattuṃ caudaitabbau  sāraitabbau – ‘atthau mai, āvusau, cīvarainā’ti| dvattikkhattuṃ caudayamānau sārayamānau taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai abhinipphādaiyya, catukkhattuṃ  pañ‍cakkhattuṃ chakkhattuparamaṃ tuṇhībhūtaina uddissa ṭhātabbaṃ| catukkhattuṃ pañ‍cakkhattuṃ chakkhattuparamaṃ tuṇhībhūtau uddissa tiṭṭhamānau  taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiyya, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; tatau cai uttari vāyamamānau taṃ cīvara abhinipphādaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| nau cai abhinipphādaiyya, yatassa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ, tattha sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘yaṃ khau tumhai āyasmantau bhikkhuṃ  uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pahiṇittha, na taṃ tassa bhikkhunau kiñ‍ci atthaṃ anubhauti, yuñ‍jantāyasmantau sakaṃ, mā vau sakaṃ vinassā’ti, ayaṃ tattha sāmīcī’’ti|

539. bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissāti bhikkhussatthāya, bhikkhuṃ ārammaṇaṃ karitvā, bhikkhuṃ acchādaitukāmau|

rājā nāma yau kauci raj‍jaṃ kāraiti|

rājabhauggau nāma yau kauci rañ‍ñau bhattavaitanāhārau|

brāhmaṇau nāma jātiyā brāhmaṇau|

gahapatikau nāma ṭhapaitvā rājaṃ rājabhauggaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ avasaisau gahapatikau nāma|

cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ nāma hirañ‍ñaṃ vā suvaṇṇaṃ vā muttā vā maṇi vā|

iminā cīvaracaitāpan‍naināti pac‍cupaṭṭhitaina|

caitāpaitvāti parivattaitvā|

acchādaihīti daj‍jaihi|

sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘idaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ| paṭiggaṇhātu āyasmā cīvaracaitāpan‍na’’nti, taina bhikkhunā sau dūtau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| cīvarañ‍ca khau mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, kālaina kappiya’’nti| sau cai dūtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘atthi panāyasmatau kauci vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti? cīvaratthikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau niddisitabbau ārāmikau vā upāsakau vā – ‘‘aisau khau, āvusau, bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakarau’’ti| na vattabbau – ‘‘tassa daihīti vā, sau vā nikkhipissati, sau vā parivattaissati, sau vā caitāpaissatī’’ti|

sau  cai dūtau taṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ  sañ‍ñāpaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmā vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṃ niddisi sañ‍ñattau sau mayā| upasaṅkamatu āyasmā kālaina, cīvaraina taṃ acchādaissatī’’ti, cīvaratthikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā vaiyyāvac‍cakarau upasaṅkamitvā dvattikkhattuṃ caudaitabbau sāraitabbau – ‘‘atthau mai, āvusau, cīvarainā’’ti| na vattabbau – ‘‘daihi mai cīvaraṃ, āhara mai cīvaraṃ, parivattaihi mai cīvaraṃ, caitāpaihi mai cīvara’’nti| dutiyampi vattabbau| tatiyampi vattabbau| sacai abhinipphādaiti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai abhinipphādaiti, tattha gantvā tuṇhībhūtaina uddissa ṭhātabbaṃ| na āsanai nisīditabbaṃ| na āmisaṃ paṭiggahaitabbaṃ| na dhammau bhāsitabbau| ‘‘kiṃ kāraṇā āgatausī’’ti pucchiyamānau ‘‘jānāhi, āvusau’’ti vattabbau| sacai āsanai vā nisīdati, āmisaṃ vā paṭiggaṇhāti, dhammaṃ vā bhāsati, ṭhānaṃ bhañ‍jati| dutiyampi ṭhātabbaṃ| tatiyampi ṭhātabbaṃ| catukkhattuṃ caudaitvā catukkhattuṃ ṭhātabbaṃ| pañ‍cakkhatuṃ caudaitvā dvikkhattuṃ ṭhātabbaṃ| chakkhattuṃ caudaitvā na ṭhātabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari vāyamamānau taṃ cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ atiraikatikkhattuṃ caudanāya atiraikachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina abhinipphāditaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

nau cai abhinipphādaiyya, yatassa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ ābhataṃ tattha sāmaṃ vā gantabbaṃ dūtau vā pāhaitabbau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tumhai āyasmantau bhikkhuṃ uddissa cīvaracaitāpan‍naṃ pahiṇittha na taṃ tassa bhikkhunau kiñ‍ci atthaṃ anubhauti| yuñ‍jantāyasmantau sakaṃ, mā vau sakaṃ vinassā’’ti|

ayaṃ tattha sāmīcīti ayaṃ tattha anudhammatā|

540. atiraikatikkhattuṃ caudanāya atiraikachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina atiraikasañ‍ñī abhinipphādaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikatikkhattuṃ caudanāya atiraikachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina vaimatikau  abhinipphādaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikatikkhattuṃ caudanāya atiraikachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina ūnakasañ‍ñī abhinipphādaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ūnakatikkhattuṃ  caudanāya ūnakachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakatikkhattuṃ caudanāya ūnakachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakatikkhattuṃ caudanāya ūnakachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina ūnakasañ‍ñī anāpatti|

541. anāpatti – tikkhattuṃ caudanāya, chakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina, ūnakatikkhattuṃ caudanāya, ūnakachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina, acaudiyamānau daiti, sāmikā  caudaitvā dainti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

rājasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

kathinavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ tīṇi, dhauvanañ‍ca paṭiggahau|

añ‍ñātakāni tīṇaiva, ubhin‍naṃ dūtakaina cāti||

2. kausiyavaggau

1. kausiyasikkhāpadaṃ

542. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kausiyakārakai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘bahū, āvusau, kausakārakai pacatha, amhākampi dassatha, mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kātu’’nti| tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā amhai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhanti – ‘bahū, āvusau, kausakārakai pacatha, amhākampi dassatha, mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kātu’nti! amhākampi alābhā, amhākampi dul‍laddhaṃ, yai mayaṃ ājīvassa haitu puttadārassa kāraṇā bahū khuddakai pāṇai saṅghātaṃ āpādaimā’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kausiyakārakai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhanti – ‘bahū, āvusau, kausakārakai pacatha, amhākampi dassatha, mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ  santhataṃ kātu’’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, kausiyakārakai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaitha – ‘bahū, āvusau, kausakārakai pacatha, amhākampi dassatha, mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ  kātu’’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, kausiyakārakai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhatha – bahū, āvusau, kausakārakai pacatha, amhākampi dassatha, mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kātunti| naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

543. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

544. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

santhataṃ nāma santharitvā kataṃ hauti avāyimaṃ|

kārāpaiyyāti aikainapi kausiyaṃsunā missitvā karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabba…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kārāpitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

545. attanā  vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

546. anāpatti  vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

kausiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. suddhakāḷakasikkhāpadaṃ

547. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpainti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

548. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

549. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ  atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

kāḷakaṃ nāma dvai kāḷakāni – jātiyā kāḷakaṃ vā rajanakāḷakaṃ vā|

santhataṃ nāma santharitvā kataṃ hauti avāyimaṃ|

kārāpaiyyāti  karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa  vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ  …pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

550. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

551. anāpatti vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

suddhakāḷakasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. dvaibhāgasikkhāpadaṃ

552. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaitu’’nti, tai thaukaṃyaiva audātaṃ antai ādiyitvā tathaiva suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thaukaṃyaiva audātaṃ antai ādiyitvā tathaiva suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, thaukaṃyaiva audātaṃ antai ādiyitvā tathaiva suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi  nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, thaukaṃyaiva  audātaṃ antai ādiyitvā tathaiva suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

553. ‘‘navaṃ pana bhikkhunā santhataṃ kārayamānaina dvai bhāgā suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ ādātabbā tatiyaṃ audātānaṃ catutthaṃ gaucariyānaṃ| anādā cai bhikkhu dvai bhāgai suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ tatiyaṃ audātānaṃ catutthaṃ gaucariyānaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

554. navaṃ nāma karaṇaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati|

santhataṃ nāma santharitvā kataṃ hauti avāyimaṃ|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā|

dvai bhāgā suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ ādātabbāti dhārayitvā dvai tulā ādātabbā|

tatiyaṃ audātānanti tulaṃ audātānaṃ|

catutthaṃ gaucariyānanti tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ|

anādā  cai bhikkhu dvai bhāgai suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ tatiyaṃ audātānaṃ catutthaṃ gaucariyānanti| anādiyitvā dvai tulai suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ tulaṃ audātānaṃ tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ karauti vā kārāpaiti vā payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, santhataṃ anādiyitvā dvai tulai suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ tulaṃ audātānaṃ tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ kārāpitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

555. attanā  vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñassatthāya  karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

556. anāpatti tulaṃ audātānaṃ tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ ādiyitvā karauti, bahutaraṃ audātānaṃ bahutaraṃ gaucariyānaṃ ādiyitvā karauti, suddhaṃ audātānaṃ suddhaṃ gaucariyānaṃ ādiyitvā karauti, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

dvaibhāgasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. chabbassasikkhāpadaṃ

557. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpainti| tai yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharanti – ‘‘aiḷakalaumāni daitha| aiḷakalaumaihi atthau’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissanti, yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharissanti – ‘aiḷakalaumāni daitha, aiḷakalaumaihi atthau’’’ti! amhākaṃ pana sakiṃ katāni santhatāni pañ‍capi chapi vassāni haunti, yaisaṃ nau dārakā uhadantipi ummihantipi undūraihipi khaj‍janti| imai pana samaṇā sakyaputtiyā anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpainti, yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharanti – ‘‘aiḷakalaumāni daitha, aiḷakalaumaihi atthau’’ti!

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū  appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissanti, yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharissanti – ‘aiḷakalaumāni daitha, aiḷakalaumaihi atthau’’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ  kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpainti, yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharanti – ‘aiḷakalaumāni daitha, aiḷakalaumaihi  atthau’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau  bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaissanti, yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharissanti – ‘aiḷakalaumāni daitha, aiḷakalaumaihi atthau’ti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

558. ‘‘navaṃ pana bhikkhunā santhataṃ kārāpaitvā chabbassāni dhāraitabbaṃ| auraina cai chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ taṃ santhataṃ vissaj‍jaitvā vā avissaj‍jaitvā vā añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

559. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausambiyaṃ gilānau hauti| ñātakā tassa bhikkhunau santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisuṃ – ‘‘āgacchatu, bhadantau mayaṃ upaṭṭhahissāmā’’ti| bhikkhūpi aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘gacchāvusau, ñātakā taṃ upaṭṭhahissantī’’ti| sau aivamāha – ‘‘bhagavatā, āvusau, sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘navaṃ pana bhikkhunā santhataṃ kārāpaitvā chabbassāni dhāraitabba’nti| ahañ‍camhi gilānau, na sak‍kaumi santhataṃ ādāya pak‍kamituṃ| mayhañ‍ca vinā santhatā na phāsu hauti| nāhaṃ gamissāmī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa bhikkhunau santhatasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| taina gilānaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, gilānau| na sak‍kaumi santhataṃ ādāya pak‍kamituṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ santhatasammutiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

560. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau| na sak‍kauti santhataṃ ādāya pak‍kamituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ santhatasammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau santhatasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau| na sak‍kauti santhataṃ ādāya pak‍kamituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ santhatasammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau santhatasammutiṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau santhatasammutiyā dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau santhatasammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

561. ‘‘navaṃ pana bhikkhunā santhataṃ kārāpaitvā chabbassāni dhāraitabbaṃ| auraina cai chan‍naṃ vassānaṃ taṃ santhataṃ vissaj‍jaitvā vā avissaj‍jaitvā vā añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaiyya, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

562. navaṃ nāma karaṇaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati|

santhataṃ nāma santharitvā kataṃ hauti avāyimaṃ|

kārāpaitvāti karitvā vā kārāpaitvā vā|

chabbassāni dhāraitabbanti chabbassaparamatā dhāraitabbaṃ|

auraina cai chan‍naṃ vassānanti ūnakachabbassāni|

taṃ santhataṃ vissaj‍jaitvāti añ‍ñaisaṃ datvā|

avissaj‍jaitvāti  na kassaci datvā|

añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyāti ṭhapaitvā bhikkhusammutiṃ añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, santhataṃ ūnakachabbassāni kārāpitaṃ, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

563. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

564. anāpatti chabbassāni karauti, atiraikachabbassāni karauti, añ‍ñassatthāya karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, bhikkhusammutiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

chabbassasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. nisīdanasanthatasikkhāpadaṃ

565. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| namhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai,’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā nāssudha kauci bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā saṅghaina katikā katā hauti – ‘‘icchatāvusau, bhagavā taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| na bhagavā kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina| yau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati sau pācittiyaṃ daisāpaitabbau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā upasainau vaṅgantaputtau, saparisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudituṃ| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upasainaṃ vaṅgantaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci vau, upasaina, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍cittha appakilamathaina addhānaṃ āgatā’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ, bhagavā| appakilamathaina ca mayaṃ, bhantai, addhānaṃ āgatā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upasainassa vaṅgantaputtassa saddhivihārikau bhikkhu bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā  taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘manāpāni tai, bhikkhu, paṃsukūlānī’’ti? ‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, manāpāni paṃsukūlānī’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, paṃsukūlikau’’ti? ‘‘upajjhāyau mai, bhantai, paṃsukūlikau| aivaṃ ahampi paṃsukūlikau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upasainaṃ vaṅgantaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pāsādikā khau tyāyaṃ, upasaina, parisā| kathaṃ tvaṃ, upasaina, parisaṃ vinaisī’’ti? ‘‘yau maṃ, bhantai, upasampadaṃ yācati tamahaṃ tāhaṃ (ka॰) aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau piṇḍapātikau paṃsukūlikau| sacai tvampi ārañ‍ñikau bhavissasi piṇḍapātikau paṃsukūlikau, aivāhaṃ taṃ upasampādaissāmī’ti| sacai mai paṭissuṇāti upasampādaimi, nau cai mai paṭissuṇāti na upasampādaimi| yau  maṃ nissayaṃ yācati tamahaṃ tāhaṃ (ka॰) aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ārañ‍ñikau piṇḍapātikau paṃsukūlikau| sacai tvampi ārañ‍ñikau bhavissasi piṇḍapātikau paṃsukūlikau, aivāhaṃ tai nissayaṃ dassāmī’ti| sacai mai paṭissuṇāti nissayaṃ daimi, nau cai mai paṭissuṇāti na nissayaṃ daimi| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, parisaṃ vinaimī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, upasaina| sādhu khau tvaṃ, upasaina, parisaṃ vinaisi| jānāsi pana tvaṃ, upasaina, sāvatthiyā saṅghassa katika’’nti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, jānāmi sāvatthiyā saṅghassa katika’’nti| ‘‘sāvatthiyā khau, upasaina, saṅghaina katikā katā – ‘icchatāvusau, bhagavā taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| na bhagavā kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina| yau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati sau pācittiyaṃ daisāpaitabbau’ti| ‘‘pañ‍ñāyissati, bhantai, sāvatthiyā saṅghau sakāya katikāya, na mayaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāma pañ‍ñattaṃ vā na samucchindissāma, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattissāmā’’ti| ‘‘sādhu  sādhu, upasaina, apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ, pañ‍ñattaṃ vā na samucchinditabbaṃ, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattitabbaṃ| anujānāmi, upasaina, yai tai bhikkhū ārañ‍ñikā piṇḍapātikā paṃsukūlikā yathāsukhaṃ maṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamantū’’ti|

566. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā haunti – ‘‘mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ upasainaṃ vaṅgantaputtaṃ pācittiyaṃ daisāpaissāmā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā upasainau vaṅgantaputtau saparisau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upasainaṃ vaṅgantaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘jānāsi tvaṃ, āvusau upasaina, sāvatthiyā saṅghassa katika’’nti| ‘‘bhagavāpi maṃ, āvusau, aivamāha – ‘jānāsi pana tvaṃ, upasaina, sāvatthiyā saṅghassa  katika’nti? na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, jānāmi sāvatthiyā saṅghassa katika’’nti| ‘‘sāvatthiyā khau, upasaina, saṅghaina katikā katā – icchatāvusau, bhagavā taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| na bhagavā kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina| yau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati sau pācittiyaṃ daisāpaitabbau’’ti| ‘‘pañ‍ñāyissati, bhantai, sāvatthiyā saṅghau sakāya katikāya, na mayaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāma pañ‍ñattaṃ vā na samucchindissāma, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattissāmāti| anuñ‍ñātāvusau, bhagavatā – ‘yai tai bhikkhū ārañ‍ñikā piṇḍapātikā paṃsukūlikā yathāsukhaṃ maṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamantū’’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū – ‘‘sac‍caṃ khau āyasmā upasainau āha – ‘na apañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ, pañ‍ñattaṃ vā na samucchinditabbaṃ, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattitabba’’’nti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū – ‘‘anuñ‍ñātā kira bhagavatā – ‘yai tai bhikkhū ārañ‍ñikā piṇḍapātikā paṃsukūlikā yathāsukhaṃ maṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamantū’’’ti| tai bhagavantaṃ dassanaṃ pihaintā dassanāya pihayamānā (syā॰) santhatāni ujjhitvā ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ  paṃsukūlikaṅgaṃ samādiyiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau addasa santhatāni tahaṃ tahaṃ ujjhitāni| passitvā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘kassimāni, bhikkhavai, santhatāni tahaṃ tahaṃ ujjhitānī’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca  – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya,…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

567. ‘‘nisīdanasanthataṃ  pana bhikkhunā kārayamānaina purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthi ādātabbā dubbaṇṇakaraṇāya, anādā cai bhikkhu purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ navaṃ nisīdanasanthataṃ kārāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

568. nisīdanaṃ nāma sadasaṃ vuc‍cati|

santhataṃ nāma santharitvā kataṃ hauti avāyimaṃ|

kārayamānaināti karauntau vā kārāpaintau vā|

purāṇasanthataṃ nāma sakiṃ nivatthampi sakiṃ pārutampi|

sāmantā sugatavidatthi ādātabbā dubbaṇṇakaraṇāyāti thirabhāvāya vaṭṭaṃ vā caturassaṃ vā chinditvā aikadaisai vā santharitabbaṃ vijaṭaitvā vā santharitabbaṃ|

anādā cai bhikkhu purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthinti anādiyitvā purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ navaṃ  nisīdanasanthataṃ karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, nisīdanasanthataṃ anādiyitvā purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ kārāpitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

569. attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| paraihi vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñassatthāya  karauti vā kārāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

570. anāpatti – purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ ādiyitvā  karauti, alabhantau thaukataraṃ ādiyitvā karauti, alabhantau anādiyitvā karauti, añ‍ñaina kataṃ paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, vitānaṃ vā bhūmattharaṇaṃ vā sāṇipākāraṃ vā bhisiṃ vā bibbauhanaṃ vā karauti, ummattakassa ādikammikassāti|

nisīdanasanthatasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. aiḷakalaumasikkhāpadaṃ

571. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantassa antarāmaggai aiḷakalaumāni uppaj‍jiṃsu| atha khau sau bhikkhu tāni aiḷakalaumāni uttarāsaṅgaina bhaṇḍikaṃ bandhitvā agamāsi| manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā uppaṇḍaisuṃ – ‘‘kittakaina tai, bhantai, kītāni? kittakau udayau bhavissatī’’ti? sau bhikkhu taihi manussaihi uppaṇḍiyamānau maṅku ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu sāvatthiṃ gantvā tāni aiḷakalaumāni ṭhitakauva āsumbhi| bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, imāni aiḷakalaumāni ṭhitakauva āsumbhasī’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi panāhaṃ, āvusau, imaisaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ kāraṇā manussaihi uppaṇḍitau’’ti| ‘‘kīva dūratau pana tvaṃ, āvusau, imāni aiḷakalaumāni āharī’’ti? ‘‘atiraikatiyaujanaṃ, āvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu atiraikatiyaujanaṃ aiḷakalaumāni āharissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, atiraikatiyaujanaṃ aiḷakalaumāni āharīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma  tvaṃ, maughapurisa, atiraikatiyaujanaṃ aiḷakalaumāni āharissasi! naitaṃ maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

572. ‘‘bhikkhunau  panaiva addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nassa aiḷakalaumāni uppaj‍jayyuṃ| ākaṅkhamānaina bhikkhunā paṭiggahaitabbāni| paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaparamaṃ sahatthā haritabbāni hāraitabbāni (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), asantai hārakai| tatau cai uttari haraiyya, asantaipi hārakai, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

573. bhikkhunau  panaiva addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nassāti panthaṃ gacchantassa|

aiḷakalaumāni uppaj‍jaiyyunti uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saṅghatau vā gaṇatau vā ñātitau vā mittatau vā paṃsukūlaṃ vā attanau vā dhanaina|

ākaṅkhamānaināti  icchamānaina paṭiggahaitabbāni|

paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaparamaṃ sahatthā haritabbānīti tiyaujanaparamatā sahatthā haritabbāni|

asantai hārakaiti nāñ‍ñau kauci hārakau hauti itthī vā purisau vā gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā|

tatau cai uttari haraiyya, asantaipi hārakaiti paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyāni haunti (syā॰)| antautiyaujanai ṭhitau bahitiyaujanaṃ pātaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyāni haunti (syā॰)| añ‍ñassa yānai vā bhaṇḍai vā ajānantassa pakkhipitvā tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyāni haunti| nissaj‍jitabbāni saṅghassa vā gaṇassa  vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbāni…pai॰… imāni mai, bhantai, aiḷakalaumāni tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmitāni nissaggiyāni| imānāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

574. atiraikatiyaujanai atiraikasañ‍ñī atik‍kāmaiti tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaiti (syā॰), nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikatiyaujanai vaimatikau atik‍kāmaiti tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaiti (syā॰), nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikatiyaujanai ūnakasañ‍ñī atik‍kāmaiti tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaiti (syā॰), nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ūnakatiyaujanai  atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakatiyaujanai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakatiyaujanai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

575. anāpatti tiyaujanaṃ harati, ūnakatiyaujanaṃ harati, tiyaujanaṃ haratipi, pac‍cāharatipi, tiyaujanaṃ vāsādhippāyau gantvā tatau paraṃ harati, acchin‍naṃ paṭilabhitvā harati, nissaṭṭhaṃ paṭilabhitvā harati, añ‍ñaṃ harāpaiti katabhaṇḍaṃ, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

aiḷakalaumasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aiḷakalaumadhauvāpanasikkhāpadaṃ

576. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaintipi  rajāpaintipi vijaṭāpaintipi| bhikkhuniyau aiḷakalaumāni dhauvantiyau rajantiyau vijaṭaintiyau riñ‍canti uddaisaṃ paripucchaṃ adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ adhipañ‍ñaṃ| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, gautami, bhikkhuniyau appamattā ātāpiniyau pahitattā viharantī’’ti? ‘‘kutau, bhantai, bhikkhunīnaṃ appamādau! ayyā chabbaggiyā bhikkhunīhi aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaintipi rajāpaintipi vijaṭāpaintipi| bhikkhuniyau aiḷakalaumāni dhauvantiyau rajantiyau vijaṭaintiyau riñ‍canti uddaisaṃ paripucchaṃ adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ adhipañ‍ña’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ  nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīhi aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaithapi rajāpaithapi vijaṭāpaithapī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātikāyau tumhākaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātikāyau’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātikāyau, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakā, maughapurisā, añ‍ñātikānaṃ na jānanti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā pāsādikaṃ vā apāsādikaṃ| tattha nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, añ‍ñātikāhi bhikkhunīhi aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaissathapi rajāpaissathapi vijaṭāpaissathapi! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

577. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiyya vā rajāpaiyya vā vijaṭāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

578. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

añ‍ñātikā nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhā|

bhikkhunī nāma ubhatausaṅghai upasampan‍nā|

dhauvāti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dhautāni nissaggiyāni haunti| rajāti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| rattāni nissaggiyāni haunti| vijaṭaihīti āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| vijaṭitāni nissaggiyāni  haunti| nissaj‍jitabbāni saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbāni…pai॰… imāni mai, bhantai, aiḷakalaumāni añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā dhauvāpitāni nissaggiyāni| imānāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

579. añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiti rajāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiti vijaṭāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiti rajāpaiti vijaṭāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

añ‍ñātikāya  añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni rajāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni rajāpaiti vijaṭāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni rajāpaiti dhauvāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni rajāpaiti vijaṭāpaiti dhauvāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni vijaṭāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni vijaṭāpaiti dhauvāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni vijaṭāpaiti rajāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| añ‍ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī aiḷakalaumāni vijaṭāpaiti dhauvāpaiti rajāpaiti, nissaggiyaina āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ|

580. añ‍ñātikāya vaimatikau…pai॰… añ‍ñātikāya ñātikasañ‍ñī…pai॰… añ‍ñassa aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aikatau upasampan‍nāya dhauvāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya añ‍ñātikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātikāya ñātikasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

581. anāpatti ñātikāya dhāvantiyā añ‍ñātikā dutiyā hauti, avuttā dhauvati, aparibhuttaṃ katabhaṇḍaṃ dhauvāpaiti, sikkhamānāya sāmaṇairiyā ummattakassa ādikammikassāti|

aiḷakalaumadhauvāpanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. rūpiyasikkhāpadaṃ

582. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā  upanandau sakyaputtau añ‍ñatarassa kulassa kulūpakau hauti nic‍cabhattikau| yaṃ tasmiṃ kulai uppaj‍jati khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā tatau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa paṭivisau ṭhapiyyati| taina khau pana samayaina sāyaṃ tasmiṃ kulai maṃsaṃ  uppan‍naṃ hauti| tatau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa paṭivisau ṭhapitau hauti| tassa kulassa dārakau rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya raudati – ‘‘maṃsaṃ mai daithā’’ti| atha khau sau purisau pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayyassa paṭivisaṃ dārakassa daihi| añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaitvā ayyassa dassāmā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaraṃ ādāya yaina taṃ kulaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sau purisau yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ  upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘hiyyau khau, bhantai, sāyaṃ maṃsaṃ uppan‍naṃ ahausi| tatau ayyassa paṭivisau ṭhapitau| ayaṃ, bhantai, dārakau rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya raudati – ‘maṃsaṃ mai daithā’ti| ayyassa paṭivisau dārakassa din‍nau| kahāpaṇaina, bhantai, kiṃ āhariyyatū’’ti? ‘‘paric‍cattau mai, āvusau, kahāpaṇau’’ti? ‘‘āma, bhantai, paric‍cattau’’ti| ‘‘tañ‍ñaiva mai, āvusau, kahāpaṇaṃ daihī’’ti|

atha khau sau purisau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa kahāpaṇaṃ datvā ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘tathaiva mayaṃ rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma aivamaivimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhantī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa purisassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahaissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahaisī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāsīti (syā॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahaissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

583. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jātarūparajataṃ uggaṇhaiyya vā uggaṇhāpaiyya vā upanikkhittaṃ vā sādiyaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

584. yau  panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

jātarūpaṃ nāma satthuvaṇṇau vuc‍cati|

rajataṃ nāma kahāpaṇau lauhamāsakau dārumāsakau jatumāsakau yai vauhāraṃ gacchanti|

uggaṇhaiyyāti sayaṃ gaṇhāti nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyaṃ hauti (syā॰)|

uggaṇhāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ gāhāpaiti nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyaṃ hauti (syā॰)|

upanikkhittaṃ  vā sādiyaiyyāti idaṃ ayyassa hautūti upanikkhittaṃ sādiyati, nissaggiyaṃ hauti| saṅghamajjhai nissaj‍jitabbaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ – taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahaisiṃ| idaṃ mai nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā āpatti daisaitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| sacai tattha āgacchati ārāmikau vā upāsakau vā sau vattabbau – ‘‘āvusau, imaṃ jānāhī’’ti| sacai sau bhaṇati – ‘‘iminā kiṃ āhariyyatū’’ti, na vattabbau – ‘‘imaṃ vā imaṃ vā āharā’’ti| kappiyaṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ – sappi vā tailaṃ vā madhu vā phāṇitaṃ vā| sacai sau taina parivattaitvā kappiyaṃ āharati rūpiyappaṭiggāhakaṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbaihaiva paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai labhaitha, sau vattabbau – ‘‘āvusau, imaṃ chaḍḍaihī’’ti| sacai sau chaḍḍaiti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai chaḍḍaiti, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  rūpiyachaḍḍakau samman‍nitabbau – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, chaḍḍitāchaḍḍitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

585. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ rūpiyachaḍḍakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ rūpiyachaḍḍakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau rūpiyachaḍḍakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu rūpiyachaḍḍakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina sammataina bhikkhunā animittaṃ katvā pātaitabbaṃ| sacai nimittaṃ katvā pātaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

586. rūpiyai  rūpiyasañ‍ñī rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| rūpiyai vaimatikau rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| rūpiyai arūpiyasañ‍ñī rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

arūpiyai  rūpiyasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| arūpiyai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| arūpiyai arūpiyasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

anāpatti ajjhārāmai vā ajjhāvasathai vā uggahaitvā vā  uggahāpaitvā vā nikkhipati – yassa bhavissati sau harissatīti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

rūpiyasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. rūpiyasaṃvauhārasikkhāpadaṃ

587. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

588. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

589. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

nānappakārakaṃ  nāma katampi  akatampi katākatampi| kataṃ nāma sīsūpagaṃ gīvūpagaṃ hatthūpagaṃ pādūpagaṃ kaṭūpagaṃ| akataṃ nāma ghanakataṃ vuc‍cati| katākataṃ nāma tadubhayaṃ|

rūpiyaṃ  nāma satthuvaṇṇau kahāpaṇau, lauhamāsakau, dārumāsakau, jatumāsakau yai vauhāraṃ gacchanti|

samāpaj‍jaiyyāti kataina kataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyaṃ hauti (syā॰)| kataina akataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| kataina katākataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| akataina kataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| akataina akataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| akataina katākataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| katākataina kataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| katākataina akataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| katākataina katākataṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| saṅghamajjhai nissaj‍jitabbaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ| taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau  – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jiṃ| idaṃ mai nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā āpatti daisaitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| sacai tattha āgacchati ārāmikau vā upāsakau vā sau vattabbau – ‘‘āvusau, imaṃ jānāhī’’ti| sacai sau bhaṇati – ‘‘iminā kiṃ āhariyyatū’’ti, na vattabbau – ‘‘imaṃ  vā imaṃ vā āharā’’ti| kappiyaṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ – sappi vā tailaṃ vā madhu vā phāṇitaṃ vā| sacai sau taina parivattaitvā kappiyaṃ āharati, rūpiyacaitāpakaṃ ṭhapaitvā, sabbaihaiva paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai labhaitha, sau vattabbau – ‘‘āvusau, imaṃ chaḍḍaihī’’ti| sacai sau chaḍḍaiti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ; nau cai chaḍḍaiti, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu rūpiyachaḍḍakau samman‍nitabbau – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, chaḍḍitāchaḍḍitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

590. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ rūpiyachaḍḍakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ rūpiyachaḍḍakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau rūpiyachaḍḍakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu rūpiyachaḍḍakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina sammataina bhikkhunā animittaṃ katvā pātaitabbaṃ| sacai nimittaṃ katvā pātaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

591. rūpiyai rūpiyasañ‍ñī rūpiyaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| rūpiyai  vaimatikau rūpiyaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| rūpiyai arūpiyasañ‍ñī rūpiyaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| arūpiyai rūpiyasañ‍ñī rūpiyaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| arūpiyai vaimatikau rūpiyaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| arūpiyai arūpiyasañ‍ñī rūpiyaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

arūpiyai rūpiyasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| arūpiyai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| arūpiyai arūpiyasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

592. anāpatti ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

rūpiyasaṃvauhārasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kayavik‍kayasikkhāpadaṃ

593. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau paṭṭau hauti cīvarakammaṃ kātuṃ| sau paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ karitvā surattaṃ suparikammakataṃ katvā pārupi| atha khau añ‍ñatarau paribbājakau mahagghaṃ paṭaṃ pārupitvā yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘sundarā  khau tyāyaṃ, āvusau, saṅghāṭi; daihi mai paṭainā’’ti| ‘‘jānāhi, āvusau’’ti| ‘‘āmāvusau, jānāmī’’ti| ‘‘handāvusau’’ti, adāsi| atha khau sau paribbājakau taṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pārupitvā paribbājakārāmaṃ agamāsi| paribbājakā taṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sundarā khau tyāyaṃ, āvusau, saṅghāṭi; kutau tayā laddhā’’ti? ‘‘taina mai, āvusau, paṭaina parivattitā’’ti| ‘‘katihipi tyāyaṃ, āvusau, saṅghāṭi bhavissati, sauyaiva tai paṭau varau’’ti|

atha khau sau paribbājakau – ‘‘sac‍caṃ khau paribbājakā āhaṃsu – ‘katihipi myāyaṃ saṅghāṭi bhavissati! sauyaiva mai paṭau varau’’’ti yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa tai, āvusau, saṅghāṭi saṅghāṭiṃ (syā॰ ka॰); daihi mai paṭa’’nti| ‘‘nanu tvaṃ, āvusau, mayā vuttau – ‘jānāhi, āvusau’ti! nāha dassāmī’’ti| atha khau sau paribbājakau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘gihīpi naṃ gihissa  vippaṭisārissa dainti, kiṃ pana pabbajitau pabbajitassa na dassatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa paribbājakassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

594. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu nānappakārakaṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

595. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

nānappakārakaṃ  nāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā, antamasau cuṇṇapiṇḍaupi dantakaṭṭhampi dasikasuttampi|

kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyāti iminā  imaṃ daihi, iminā imaṃ āhara, iminā imaṃ parivattaihi, iminā imaṃ caitāpaihīti| ajjhācarati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| yatau kayitañ‍ca hauti vik‍kayitañ‍ca attanau bhaṇḍaṃ parahatthagataṃ parabhaṇḍaṃ attanau hatthagataṃ, nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa  vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… ahaṃ, bhantai, nānappakārakaṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jiṃ| idaṃ mai nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

596. kayavik‍kayai kayavik‍kayasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| kayavik‍kayai vaimatikau, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| kayavik‍kayai nakayavik‍kayasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nakayavik‍kayai kayavik‍kayasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nakayavik‍kayai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| nakayavik‍kayai nakayavik‍kayasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

597. anāpatti – agghaṃ pucchati, kappiyakārakassa ācikkhati, ‘‘idaṃ amhākaṃ atthi, amhākañ‍ca iminā ca iminā ca atthau’’ti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

kayavik‍kayasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

kausiyavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kausiyā  suddhadvaibhāgā, chabbassāni nisīdanaṃ|

dvai ca laumāni uggaṇhai, ubhau nānappakārakāti||

3. pattavaggau

1. pattasikkhāpadaṃ

598. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bahū pattai san‍nicayaṃ karaunti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā bahū pattai san‍nicayaṃ karissanti, pattavāṇij‍jaṃ vā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā karissanti āmattikāpaṇaṃ vā pasāraissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikapattaṃ dhāraissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, atiraikapattaṃ dhāraithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, atiraikapattaṃ dhāraissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya …pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

599. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu atiraikapattaṃ dhāraiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

aivañ‍cidaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti|

600. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa atiraikapattau uppan‍nau  hauti| āyasmā  ca ānandau taṃ pattaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa dātukāmau hauti| āyasmā ca sāriputtau sākaitai viharati| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘na atiraikapattau dhāraitabbau’ti| ayañ‍ca mai atiraikapattau uppan‍nau| ahañ‍cimaṃ pattaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa dātukāmau| āyasmā ca sāriputtau sākaitai viharati| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ panānanda, sāriputtau āgacchissatī’’ti? ‘‘navamaṃ vā, bhagavā, divasaṃ dasamaṃ vā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dasāhaparamaṃ atiraikapattaṃ dhāraituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

601. ‘‘dasāhaparamaṃ atiraikapattau dhāraitabbau| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

602. dasāhaparamanti dasāhaparamatā dhāraitabbau|

atiraikapattau nāma anadhiṭṭhitau avikappitau|

pattau nāma dvai pattā ayaupattau mattikāpattauti|

tayau pattassa vaṇṇā uk‍kaṭṭhau pattau majjhimau pattau aumakau pattau| uk‍kaṭṭhau nāma pattau aḍḍhāḷhakaudanaṃ gaṇhāti catubhāgaṃ khādanaṃ tadupiyaṃ byañ‍janaṃ| majjhimau nāma pattau nāḷikaudanaṃ gaṇhāti catubhāgaṃ khādanaṃ tadupiyaṃ  byañ‍janaṃ| aumakau nāma pattau patthaudanaṃ gaṇhāti catubhāgaṃ khādanaṃ tadupiyaṃ byañ‍janaṃ| tatau uk‍kaṭṭhau apattau, aumakau apattau|

taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyau hautīti aikādasai aruṇuggamanai nissaggiyau hauti| nissaj‍jitabbau saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbau| taina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, bhantai, pattau dasāhātik‍kantau  nissaggiyau| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā  āpatti daisaitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā, nissaṭṭhapattau dātabbau|

603. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ pattau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau nissaggiyau saṅghassa nissaṭṭhau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ pattaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dadaiyyā’’ti|

604. taina bhikkhunā sambahulai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā  añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, bhantai, pattau dasāhātik‍kantau nissaggiyau| imāhaṃ āyasmantānaṃ nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā  āpatti daisaitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā, nissaṭṭhapattau dātabbau|

605. ‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| ayaṃ pattau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau nissaggiyau āyasmantānaṃ nissaṭṭhau| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, āyasmantā imaṃ pattaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dadaiyyu’’nti|

606. taina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, āvusau, pattau dasāhātik‍kantau nissaggiyau| imāhaṃ āyasmatau nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā āpatti daisaitabbā| taina bhikkhunā āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā, nissaṭṭhapattau dātabbau – ‘‘imaṃ pattaṃ āyasmatau dammī’’ti|

607. dasāhātik‍kantai atik‍kantasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| dasāhātik‍kantai vaimatikau, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| dasāhātik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anadhiṭṭhitai adhiṭṭhitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avikappitai vikappitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avissaj‍jitai vissaj‍jitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anaṭṭhai naṭṭhasañ‍ñī nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avinaṭṭhai vinaṭṭhasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ  pācittiyaṃ| abhin‍nai bhin‍nasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aviluttai viluttasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nissaggiyaṃ pattaṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dasāhānatik‍kantai atik‍kantasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dasāhānatik‍kantai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| dasāhānatik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

608. anāpatti antaudasāhaṃ adhiṭṭhaiti, vikappaiti, vissaj‍jaiti, nassati, vinassati, bhij‍jati, acchinditvā gaṇhanti, vissāsaṃ gaṇhanti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nissaṭṭhapattaṃ na dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nissaṭṭhapattau na dātabbau| yau na dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

pattasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. ūnapañ‍cabandhanasikkhāpadaṃ

609. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina kumbhakāraina bhikkhū pavāritā haunti – ‘‘yaisaṃ ayyānaṃ pattaina atthau ahaṃ pattainā’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpainti| yaisaṃ khuddakā pattā tai mahantai pattai viñ‍ñāpainti| yaisaṃ mahantā pattā tai khuddakai pattai viñ‍ñāpainti| atha khau sau kumbhakārau bhikkhūnaṃ bahū pattai karauntau na sak‍kauti añ‍ñaṃ vik‍kāyikaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ kātuṃ, attanāpi na yāpaiti, puttadārāpissa kilamanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaissanti! ayaṃ imaisaṃ bahū pattai karauntau na  sak‍kauti añ‍ñaṃ vik‍kāyikaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ kātuṃ, attanāpi na yāpaiti, puttadārāpissa kilamantī’’ti|

assausuṃ  khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū  pattai viñ‍ñāpaintīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pattau viñ‍ñāpaitabbau| yau viñ‍ñāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

610. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau pattau bhin‍nau hauti| atha khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ pattaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau na viñ‍ñāpaiti| hatthaisu piṇḍāya carati| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā hatthaisu piṇḍāya carissanti, saiyyathāpi titthiyā’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai  aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, naṭṭhapattassa vā bhin‍napattassa vā pattaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti|

611. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ naṭṭhapattassa vā bhin‍napattassa vā pattaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti appamattakainapi bhin‍naina appamattakainapi khaṇḍaina vilikhitamattainapi bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpainti| atha khau sau kumbhakārau bhikkhūnaṃ tathaiva bahū pattai karauntau na sak‍kauti añ‍ñaṃ vik‍kāyikaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ kātuṃ, attanāpi na yāpaiti, puttadārāpissa kilamanti| manussā tathaiva ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaissanti! ayaṃ imaisaṃ bahū pattai karauntau na sak‍kauti añ‍ñaṃ vik‍kāyikaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ kātuṃ, attanāpi na yāpaiti, puttadārāpissa kilamantī’’ti|

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū  appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamattakainapi bhin‍naina  appamattakainapi khaṇḍaina vilikhitamattainapi bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, appamattakainapi bhin‍naina appamattakainapi khaṇḍaina vilikhitamattainapi bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, appamattakainapi bhin‍naina appamattakainapi khaṇḍaina vilikhitamattainapi bahū  pattai viñ‍ñāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

612. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| taina bhikkhunā sau pattau bhikkhuparisāya nissaj‍jitabbau| yau ca tassā bhikkhuparisāya pattapariyantau sau tassa bhikkhunau padātabbau – ‘ayaṃ tai, bhikkhu, pattau yāva bhaidanāya dhāraitabbau’ti| ayaṃ tattha sāmīcī’’ti|

613. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

ūnapañ‍cabandhanau  nāma pattau abandhanau vā aikabandhanau vā dvibandhanau vā tibandhanau vā catubandhanau vā| abandhanaukāsau nāma pattau yassa dvaṅgulā rāji na hauti| bandhanaukāsau nāma pattau yassa dvaṅgulā rāji hauti| navau nāma pattau viñ‍ñattiṃ upādāya vuc‍cati|
