caitāpaiyyāti viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyau hauti| saṅghamajjhai nissaj‍jitabbau| sabbaihaiva adhiṭṭhitapattaṃ gahaitvā san‍nipatitabbaṃ| na lāmakau pattau adhiṭṭhātabbau – ‘‘mahagghaṃ pattaṃ gahaissāmī’’ti| sacai lāmakaṃ pattaṃ adhiṭṭhaiti – ‘‘mahagghaṃ pattaṃ gahaissāmī’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbau| taina  bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, bhantai, pattau ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina caitāpitau nissaggiyau| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmī’’ti| nissaj‍jitvā  āpatti daisaitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu pattaggāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, gāhitāgāhitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

614. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ pattaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ pattaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pattaggāhāpakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu pattaggāhāpakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

615. taina sammataina bhikkhunā pattau gāhaitabbau| thairau vattabbau – ‘‘gaṇhātu, bhantai, thairau patta’’nti| sacai thairau gaṇhāti, thairassa  pattau dutiyassa gāhaitabbau| na ca tassa anuddayatāya na  gahaitabbau| yau na gaṇhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apattakassa na gāhaitabbau| aitainaiva upāyaina yāva saṅghanavakā gāhaitabbā| yau ca tassā bhikkhuparisāya pattapariyantau, sau tassa bhikkhunau padātabbau – ‘‘ayaṃ tai, bhikkhu, pattau yāva bhaidanāya dhāraitabbau’’ti|

taina bhikkhunā sau pattau na adaisai nikkhipitabbau, na abhaugaina bhuñ‍jitabbau, na vissaj‍jaitabbau – ‘‘kathāyaṃ pattau nassaiyya vā vinassaiyya vā bhij‍jaiyya vā’’ti? sacai adaisai vā nikkhipati abhaugaina vā bhuñ‍jati vissaj‍jaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ayaṃ tattha sāmīcīti ayaṃ tattha anudhammatā|

616. abandhanaina  pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| abandhanaina pattaina aikabandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| abandhanaina pattaina dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| abandhanaina pattaina tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| abandhanaina pattaina catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

aikabandhanaina pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aikabandhanaina pattaina aikabandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aikabandhanaina pattaina dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aikabandhanaina pattaina tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aikabandhanaina pattaina catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ  caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dvibandhanaina pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| dvibandhanaina pattaina aikabandhanaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tibandhanaina pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… aikabandhanaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

catubandhanaina pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… aikabandhanaṃ pattaṃ… dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

abandhanaina pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| abandhanaina pattaina aikabandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… dvibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ … tibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaukāsaṃ  pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

aikabandhanaina pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… aikabandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… dvibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

dvibandhanaina pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… catubandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tibandhanaina  pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… catubandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

catubandhanaina pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… aikabandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… dvibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

abandhanaukāsaina  pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| abandhanaukāsaina pattaina aikabandhanaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

catubandhanaukāsaina pattaina abandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| catubandhanaukāsaina pattaina aikabandhanaṃ  pattaṃ…pai॰… dvibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

abandhanaukāsaina pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… aikabandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… dvibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

catubandhanaukāsaina pattaina abandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ…pai॰… aikabandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… dvibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… tibandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ… catubandhanaukāsaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

617. anāpatti  naṭṭhapattassa, bhin‍napattassa, ñātakānaṃ pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

ūnapañ‍cabandhanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. bhaisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ

618. idaṃ vatthu mahāva॰ 270 taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā pilindavacchau rājagahai pabbhāraṃ saudhāpaiti laiṇaṃ kattukāmau| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau yaināyasmā pilindavacchau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, thairau kārāpaitī’’ti? ‘‘pabbhāraṃ mahārāja, saudhāpaimi laiṇaṃ kattukāmau’’ti| ‘‘atthau, bhantai, ayyassa ārāmikainā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, mahārāja, bhagavatā ārāmikau anuñ‍ñātau’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ paṭipucchitvā mama āraucaiyyāthā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ mahārājā’’ti khau āyasmā pilindavacchau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmatā pilindavacchaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

619. atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau bhagavatau santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘rājā, bhantai, māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau ārāmikaṃ dātukāmau| kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ārāmika’’nti| dutiyampi khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau  bimbisārau yaināyasmā pilindivacchau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘anuñ‍ñātau, bhantai, bhagavatā ārāmikau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, ayyassa ārāmikaṃ dammī’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmatau pilindavacchassa ārāmikaṃ  paṭissuṇitvā vissaritvā ciraina satiṃ paṭilabhitvā añ‍ñataraṃ sabbatthakaṃ mahāmattaṃ āmantaisi  – ‘‘yau mayā, bhaṇai, ayyassa ārāmikau paṭissutau, din‍nau sau ārāmikau’’ti? ‘‘na khau, daiva, ayyassa ārāmikau din‍nau’’ti| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ nu khau, bhaṇai, itau hi taṃ hautī’’ti? atha khau sau mahāmattau rattiyau gaṇaitvā vigaṇaitvā (ka॰) rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍ca, daiva, rattisatānī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, ayyassa pañ‍ca ārāmikasatāni daihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sau mahāmattau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissuṇitvā āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pañ‍ca ārāmikasatāni  pādāsi adāsi (syā॰), pāṭiyaik‍kau gāmau nivisi| ārāmikagāmakautipi naṃ āhaṃsu, pilindagāmakautipi naṃ āhaṃsu|

620. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā pilindavacchau tasmiṃ gāmakai kulūpakau hauti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaraṃ ādāya pilindagāmakaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina tasmiṃ gāmakai ussavau hauti| dārakā alaṅkatā mālākitā kīḷanti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau pilindagāmakai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina añ‍ñatarassa ārāmikassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina tassā ārāmikiniyā dhītā añ‍ñai dārakai alaṅkatai mālākitai passitvā raudati – ‘‘mālaṃ mai daitha, alaṅkāraṃ mai daithā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau taṃ ārāmikiniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissāyaṃ dārikā raudatī’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, dārikā añ‍ñai dārakai alaṅkatai mālākitai passitvā raudati – ‘mālaṃ mai daitha, alaṅkāraṃ mai daithā’ti| kutau amhākaṃ duggatānaṃ mālā kutau, alaṅkārau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau añ‍ñataraṃ tiṇaṇḍupakaṃ gahaitvā taṃ ārāmikiniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handimaṃ tiṇaṇḍupakaṃ tassā dārikāya sīsai paṭimuñ‍cā’’ti| atha khau sā  ārāmikinī taṃ tiṇaṇḍupakaṃ gahaitvā tassā dārikāya sīsai paṭimuñ‍ci| sā ahausi suvaṇṇamālā abhirūpā dassanīyā  pāsādikā| natthi tādisā rañ‍ñaupi antaipurai suvaṇṇamālā| manussā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa āraucaisuṃ  – ‘‘amukassa, daiva, ārāmikassa gharai suvaṇṇamālā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā| natthi tādisā daivassāpi antaipurai suvaṇṇamālā| kutau tassa duggatassa! nissaṃsayaṃ caurikāya ābhatā’’ti!! atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau taṃ ārāmikakulaṃ bandhāpaisi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā pilindavacchau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaraṃ ādāya pilindagāmakaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| pilindagāmakai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina tassa ārāmikassa  nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā paṭivissakai pucchi – ‘‘kahaṃ imaṃ ārāmikakulaṃ gata’’nti? ‘‘aitissā, bhantai, suvaṇṇamālāya kāraṇā rañ‍ñā bandhāpita’’nti|

621. atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau yaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau yaināyasmā pilindavacchau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ āyasmā pilindavacchau aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa, mahārāja, ārāmikakulaṃ  bandhāpita’’nti? ‘‘tassa, bhantai, ārāmikassa gharai suvaṇṇamālā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā| natthi tādisā amhākampi antaipurai suvaṇṇamālā| kutau tassa duggatassa! nissaṃsayaṃ caurikāya ābhatā’’ti!! atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pāsādaṃ suvaṇṇanti adhimuc‍ci| sau ahausi sabbasauvaṇṇamayau| ‘‘idaṃ pana tai, mahārāja, tāva bahuṃ suvaṇṇaṃ kutau’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhantai, ayyassaivaisau iddhānubhāvau’’ti| taṃ ārāmikakulaṃ muñ‍cāpaisi| manussā – ‘‘ayyaina kira pilindavacchaina sarājikāya parisāya uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassita’’nti, attamanā abhippasan‍nā āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni abhihariṃsu, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ| pakatiyāpi ca āyasmā pilindavacchau lābhī hauti pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaisaj‍jānaṃ| laddhaṃ laddhaṃ parisāya vissaj‍jaiti| parisā cassa hauti bāhul‍likā| laddhaṃ laddhaṃ kaulambaipi ghaṭaipi pūraitvā paṭisāmaiti, parissāvanānipi thavikāyaupi pūraitvā vātapānaisu laggaiti| tāni aulīnavilīnāni tiṭṭhanti| undūraihipi  vihārā aukiṇṇavikiṇṇā  haunti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘antaukauṭṭhāgārikā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā, saiyyathāpi rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū aivarūpāya bāhul‍lāya caitaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū aivarūpāya bāhul‍lāya caitaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā aivarūpāya bāhul‍lāya caitaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

622. ‘‘yāni  khau pana tāni gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭisāyanīyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ, tāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaparamaṃ san‍nidhikārakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbāni| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

623. yāni khau pana tāni gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭisāyanīyāni bhaissaj‍jānīti sappi nāma gausappi vā ajikāsappi vā mahiṃsasappi mahisasappi (sī॰ syā॰) vā yaisaṃ maṃsaṃ kappati taisaṃ sappi| navanītaṃ nāma taisaṃ yaiva navanītaṃ| tailaṃ nāma tilatailaṃ sāsapatailaṃ madhukatailaṃ airaṇḍatailaṃ vasātailaṃ| madhu nāma makkhikāmadhu| phāṇitaṃ nāma ucchumhā nibbattaṃ|

tāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaparamaṃ san‍nidhikārakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbānīti sattāhaparamatā paribhuñ‍jitabbāni|

taṃ atik‍kāmayatau nissaggiyaṃ  hautīti aṭṭhamai aruṇuggamanai nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, bhaisaj‍jaṃ sattāhātik‍kantaṃ nissaggiyaṃ, imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

624. sattāhātik‍kantai  atik‍kantasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| sattāhātik‍kantai vaimatikau, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| sattāhātik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anadhiṭṭhitai adhiṭṭhitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avissaj‍jitai vissaj‍jitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anaṭṭhai naṭṭhasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| avinaṭṭhai vinaṭṭhasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ  pācittiyaṃ| adaḍḍhai daḍḍhasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aviluttai viluttasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nissaṭṭhaṃ paṭilabhitvā na kāyikaina paribhaugaina paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, na ajjhauharitabbaṃ, padīpai vā kāḷavaṇṇai vā upanaitabbaṃ, añ‍ñaina bhikkhunā kāyikaina paribhaugaina paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, na ajjhauharitabbaṃ|

sattāhānatik‍kantai  atik‍kantasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sattāhānatik‍kantai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sattāhānatik‍kantai anatik‍kantasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

625. anāpatti  antausattāhaṃ adhiṭṭhaiti, vissaj‍jaiti, nassati, vinassati, ḍayhati, acchinditvā gaṇhanti, vissāsaṃ gaṇhanti, anupasampan‍nassa cattaina vantaina muttaina anapaikkhau datvā paṭilabhitvā paribhuñ‍jati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

bhaisaj‍jasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. vassikasāṭikasikkhāpadaṃ

626. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ vassikasāṭikā anuñ‍ñātā hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā vassikasāṭikā anuñ‍ñātā’’ti, paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰) vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisanti, paṭikac‍caiva katvā nivāsainti, jiṇṇāya vassikasāṭikāya naggā kāyaṃ auvassāpainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū paṭikac‍caiva vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisissanti, paṭikac‍caiva katvā nivāsaissanti, jiṇṇāya  vassikasāṭikāya naggā kāyaṃ auvassāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, paṭikac‍caiva vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisatha? paṭikac‍caiva katvā nivāsaitha? jiṇṇāya vassikasāṭikāya naggā kāyaṃ auvassāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai maughapurisā, paṭikac‍caiva vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisissatha, paṭikac‍caiva katvā nivāsaissatha, jiṇṇāya vassikasāṭikāya naggā kāyaṃ auvassāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

627. ‘‘māsau saisau gimhāna’nti bhikkhunā vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ; ‘addhamāsau  saisau gimhāna’nti katvā nivāsaitabbaṃ| ‘auraina cai māsau saisau gimhāna’nti vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisaiyya, ‘aurainaddhamāsau saisau gimhāna’nti katvā nivāsaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

628. ‘māsau saisau gimhāna’nti bhikkhunā vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisitabbanti| yai manussā pubbaipi vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ dainti tai upasaṅkamitvā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘kālau vassikasāṭikāya, samayau vassikasāṭikāya, añ‍ñaipi manussā vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ daintī’’ti| na vattabbā – ‘‘daitha mai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ, āharatha mai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ, parivattaitha mai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ, caitāpaitha mai vassikasāṭikacīvara’’nti|

‘addhamāsau saisau gimhāna’nti katvā nivāsaitabbanti| addhamāsai saisai gimhānai katvā nivāsaitabbaṃ|

‘auraina cai māsau saisau gimhāna’nti atiraikamāsai saisa gimhānai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

‘aurainaddhamāsau saisau gimhāna’nti atiraikaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai katvā nivāsaiti, nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ atiraikamāsai saisai gimhānai pariyiṭṭhaṃ atiraikaddhamāsai  saisai gimhānai katvā paridahitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti|…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

629. atiraikamāsai saisai gimhānai atiraikasañ‍ñī vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikamāsai saisai gimhānai vaimatikau vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikamāsai saisai gimhānai ūnakasañ‍ñī vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

atiraikaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai atiraikasañ‍ñī katvā nivāsaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai vaimatikau katvā nivāsaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai ūnakasañ‍ñī katvā nivāsaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

satiyā vassikasāṭikāya naggau kāyaṃ auvassāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakamāsai saisai gimhānai atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakamāsai saisai gimhānai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakamāsai saisai gimhānai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

ūnakaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakaddhamāsai saisai gimhānai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

630. anāpatti ‘māsau saisau gimhāna’nti vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisati, ‘addhamāsau saisau gimhāna’nti katvā nivāsaiti, ‘ūnakamāsau saisau gimhāna’nti vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisati, ‘ūnakaddhamāsau saisau gimhāna’nti katvā nivāsaiti, pariyiṭṭhāya vassikasāṭikāya vassaṃ uk‍kaḍḍhiyyati, nivatthāya vassikasāṭikāya vassaṃ uk‍kaḍḍhiyyati, dhauvitvā nikkhipitabbaṃ; samayai nivāsaitabbaṃ, acchin‍nacīvarassa, naṭṭhacīvarassa, āpadāsu, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

vassikasāṭikasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. cīvaraacchindanasikkhāpadaṃ

631. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhātunau saddhivihārikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihāvusau, janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, gamissāmi; dubbalacīvaraumhī’’ti| ‘‘aihāvusau, ahaṃ tai cīvaraṃ dassāmī’’ti tassa cīvaraṃ adāsi| assausi khau sau bhikkhu – ‘‘bhagavā kira janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘na dānāhaṃ āyasmatā upanandaina sakyaputtaina saddhiṃ janapadacārikaṃ  pak‍kamissāmi, bhagavatā saddhiṃ janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi dāni, āvusau, janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, tayā saddhiṃ janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissāmi, bhagavatā saddhiṃ janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘yampi tyāhaṃ, āvusau, cīvaraṃ adāsiṃ, mayā saddhiṃ janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti, kupitau anattamanau acchindi|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ  kira tvaṃ, upananda, bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

632. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindaiyya vā acchindāpaiyya vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

633. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

bhikkhussāti  añ‍ñassa bhikkhussa|

sāmanti sayaṃ datvā|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ, vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

kupitau  anattamanauti anabhiraddhau āhatacittau khilajātau|

acchindaiyyāti sayaṃ acchindati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyaṃ hauti (syā॰)|

acchindāpaiyyāti añ‍ñaṃ āṇāpaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sakiṃ āṇattau bahukampi acchindati, nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ bhikkhussa sāmaṃ datvā acchin‍naṃ nissaggiyaṃ imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti  …pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

634. upasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindati vā acchindāpaiti vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| upasampan‍nai vaimatikau cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindati vā acchindāpaiti vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| upasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindati vā acchindāpaiti vā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñaṃ parikkhāraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindati vā acchindāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nassa cīvaraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā parikkhāraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindati vā acchindāpaiti vā, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai upasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anupasampan‍nai anupasampan‍nasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

635. anāpatti – sau vā daiti, tassa vā vissasantau gaṇhāti, ummattakassa ādikammikassāti|

cīvaraacchindanasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. suttaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ

636. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū cīvarakārasamayai  bahuṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ| kataipi cīvarai bahuṃ suttaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti| atha khau chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘handa mayaṃ, āvusau, añ‍ñampi suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaimā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū añ‍ñampi suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaisuṃ| vītaipi cīvarai bahuṃ suttaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti| dutiyampi khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū añ‍ñampi suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaisuṃ| vītaipi cīvarai bahuṃ suttaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ hauti| tatiyampi khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū añ‍ñampi suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaisuṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaissantī’’ti!

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

637. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

638. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… ayaṃ imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

sāmanti  sayaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā|

suttaṃ nāma cha suttāni – khaumaṃ kappāsikaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kambalaṃ sāṇaṃ bhaṅgaṃ|

tantavāyaihīti  paisakāraihi vāyāpaiti, payaugai payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vāyāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ (syā॰)| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi vāyāpitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

639. vāyāpitai vāyāpitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| vāyāpitai vaimatikau, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| vāyāpitai avāyāpitasañ‍ñī, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

avāyāpitai vāyāpitasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| avāyāpitai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| avāyāpitai  avāyāpitasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

640. anāpatti – cīvaraṃ sibbaituṃ, āyaugai, kāyabandhanai, aṃsabandhakai aṃsavaṭṭakai (sī॰), pattatthavikāya, parissāvanai, ñātakānaṃ, pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

suttaviñ‍ñattisikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahāpaisakārasikkhāpadaṃ

641. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau pavāsaṃ gacchantau pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘suttaṃ dhārayitvā amukassa tantavāyassa daihi, cīvaraṃ vāyāpaitvā nikkhipa, āgatau ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| assausi khau añ‍ñatarau piṇḍacārikau bhikkhu tassa purisassa  imaṃ vācaṃ bhāsamānassa| atha khau sau bhikkhu yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahāpuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, āvusau upananda, amukasmiṃ aukāsai añ‍ñatarau purisau pavāsaṃ gacchantau  pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘suttaṃ dhārayitvā amukassa tantavāyassa daihi, cīvaraṃ vāyāpaitvā nikkhipa, āgatau ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘atthāvusau, maṃ sau upaṭṭhākau’’ti| saupi khau tantavāyau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau yaina sau tantavāyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ tantavāyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ khau, āvusau, cīvaraṃ maṃ uddissa viyyati; āyatañ‍ca karauhi vitthatañ‍ca| appitañ‍ca suvītañ‍ca suppavāyitañ‍ca suvilaikhitañ‍ca  suvitacchitañ‍ca karauhī’’ti| ‘‘aitai khau mai, bhantai, suttaṃ dhārayitvā adaṃsu; iminā suttaina cīvaraṃ vināhī’’ti| ‘‘na, bhantai, sak‍kā āyataṃ vā vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā kātuṃ| sak‍kā  ca khau, bhantai, suvītañ‍ca suppavāyitañ‍ca suvilaikhitañ‍ca suvitacchitañ‍ca kātu’’nti| ‘‘iṅgha tvaṃ, āvusau, āyatañ‍ca karauhi vitthatañ‍ca appitañ‍ca| na taina suttaina paṭibaddhaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

atha khau sau tantavāyau yathābhataṃ suttaṃ tantai upanaitvā yaina sā itthī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ itthiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘suttaina, ayyai, atthau’’ti| ‘‘nanu tvaṃ ayyau ayya (syā॰) mayā vuttau – ‘iminā suttaina cīvaraṃ vināhī’’’ti| ‘‘sac‍cāhaṃ, ayyai, tayā vuttau – ‘iminā suttaina cīvaraṃ vināhī’ti| apica, maṃ ayyau upanandau aivamāha – ‘iṅgha tvaṃ, āvusau, āyatañ‍ca karauhi vitthatañ‍ca appitañ‍ca, na taina suttaina paṭibaddhaṃ bhavissatī’’’ti| atha khau sā itthī yattakaṃyaiva suttaṃ paṭhamaṃ adāsi tattakaṃ pacchā adāsi| assausi khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau – ‘‘sau kira purisau pavāsatau āgatau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau yaina tassa purisassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sau purisau yaināyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau  khau sau purisau pajāpatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vītaṃ taṃ cīvara’’nti? ‘‘āmāyya, vītaṃ taṃ cīvara’’nti| ‘‘āhara, ayyaṃ upanandaṃ cīvaraina acchādaissāmī’’ti| atha khau sā itthī taṃ cīvaraṃ nīharitvā sāmikassa datvā aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau sau purisau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa  cīvaraṃ datvā ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘mahicchā imai samaṇā  sakyaputtiyā asantuṭṭhā| nayimai sukarā cīvaraina acchādaituṃ| kathañhi nāma ayyau upanandau mayā pubbai appavāritau tantavāyai gahapatikassa tantavāyai (ka॰) upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti|

assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa purisassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, pubbai appavāritau gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘ñātakau tai, upananda, añ‍ñātakau’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātakau, maughapurisa, añ‍ñātakassa na jānāti patirūpaṃ vā appatirūpaṃ vā santaṃ vā asantaṃ vā| tattha nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pubbai appavāritau  añ‍ñātakassa gahapatikassa  tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jissasi! naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

642. ‘‘bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissa añ‍ñātakau gahapati vā gahapatānī vā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaiyya, tatra cai sau bhikkhu pubbai appavāritau tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya – ‘idaṃ khau, āvusau, cīvaraṃ maṃ uddissa viyyati| āyatañ‍ca karautha vitthatañ‍ca| appitañ‍ca suvītañ‍ca suppavāyitañ‍ca suvilaikhitañ‍ca suvitacchitañ‍ca karautha| appaiva nāma mayampi āyasmantānaṃ kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyyāmā’ti| aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu vatvā kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyya antamasau piṇḍapātamattampi, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

643. bhikkhuṃ panaiva uddissāti bhikkhussatthāya bhikkhuṃ ārammaṇaṃ karitvā bhikkhuṃ acchādaitukāmau|

añ‍ñātakau nāma mātitau vā pititau vā yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā asambaddhau|

gahapati  nāma yau kauci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

gahapatānī nāma yā kāci agāraṃ ajjhāvasati|

tantavāyaihīti paisakāraihi|

cīvaraṃ nāma chan‍naṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ vikappanupagaṃ pacchimaṃ|

vāyāpaiyyāti vināpaiti|

tatra cai sau bhikkhūti yaṃ bhikkhuṃ uddissa cīvaraṃ viyyati sau  bhikkhu|

pubbai appavāritauti pubbai avuttau hauti – ‘‘kīdisaina tai, bhantai, cīvaraina atthau, kīdisaṃ tai cīvaraṃ vāyāpaimī’’ti?

tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvāti gharaṃ gantvā yattha katthaci upasaṅkamitvā|

cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti – ‘‘idaṃ khau, āvusau, cīvaraṃ maṃ uddissa viyyati, āyatañ‍ca karautha vitthatañ‍ca| appitañ‍ca suvītañ‍ca suppavāyitañ‍ca suvilaikhitañ‍ca suvitacchitañ‍ca karautha| appaiva nāma mayampi āyasmantānaṃ kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyyāmā’’ti|

aivañ‍ca sau bhikkhu vatvā kiñ‍cimattaṃ anupadaj‍jaiyya antamasau piṇḍapātamattampīti| piṇḍapātau nāma yāgupi bhattampi khādanīyampi  cuṇṇapiṇḍaupi dantakaṭṭhampi dasikasuttampi, antamasau dhammampi bhaṇati|

tassa vacanaina āyataṃ vā vitthataṃ vā appitaṃ vā karauti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakassa  gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpan‍naṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

644. añ‍ñātakai añ‍ñātakasañ‍ñī pubbai appavāritau gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai vaimatikau pubbai appavāritau gahapatikassa tantavāyai  upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| añ‍ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī pubbai appavāritau gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ñātakai  añātakasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ñātakai ñātakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

645. anāpatti – ñātakānaṃ, pavāritānaṃ, añ‍ñassatthāya, attanau dhanaina, mahagghaṃ vāyāpaitukāmassa appagghaṃ vāyāpaiti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

mahāpaisakārasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ac‍caikacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ

646. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau mahāmattau pavāsaṃ gacchantau bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘āgacchantu bhadantā vassāvāsikaṃ dassāmī’’ti| bhikkhū – ‘vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ bhagavatā vassāvāsikaṃ anuñ‍ñāta’nti, kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nāgamaṃsu| atha khau sau mahāmattau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā mayā dūtai pahitai nāgacchissanti! ahañhi saināya gacchāmi| duj‍jānaṃ jīvitaṃ duj‍jānaṃ maraṇa’’nti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa mahāmattassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā nikkhipitu’’nti|

647. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā nikkhipitu’’nti, ac‍caikacīvarāni paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmainti| tāni cīvarāni cīvaravaṃsai bhaṇḍikābaddhāni tiṭṭhanti| addasa khau āyasmā ānandau saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau tāni cīvarāni cīvaravaṃsai bhaṇḍikābaddhāni| tiṭṭhantai disvā bhikkhū aitadavauca  – ‘‘kassimāni, āvusau, cīvarāni cīvaravaṃsai bhaṇḍikābaddhāni tiṭṭhantī’’ti? ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau, ac‍caikacīvarānī’’ti| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ panāvusau, imāni cīvarāni nikkhittānī’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau  ānandassa yathānikkhittaṃ āraucaisuṃ| āyasmā ānandau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaissantī’’ti! atha khau āyasmā ānandau tai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi…pai॰… – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

648. ‘‘dasāhānāgataṃ kattikataimāsikapuṇṇamaṃ bhikkhunau panaiva ac‍caikacīvaraṃ uppaj‍jaiyya, ac‍caikaṃ mañ‍ñamānaina bhikkhunā paṭiggahaitabbaṃ, paṭiggahaitvā yāva cīvarakālasamayaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari nikkhipaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

649. dasāhānāgatanti dasāhānāgatāya pavāraṇāya|

kattikataimāsikapuṇṇamanti pavāraṇā kattikā vuc‍cati|

ac‍caikacīvaraṃ nāma saināya vā gantukāmau hauti, pavāsaṃ vā gantukāmau hauti, gilānau  vā hauti, gabbhinī vā hauti, assaddhassa vā saddhā uppan‍nā hauti, appasan‍nassa vā  pasādau uppan‍nau hauti, sau cai bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ pahiṇaiyya – ‘‘āgacchantu bhadantā vassāvāsikaṃ dassāmī’’ti, aitaṃ ac‍caikacīvaraṃ nāma|

ac‍caikaṃ mañ‍ñamānaina bhikkhunā paṭiggahaitabbaṃ paṭiggahaitvā yāva cīvarakālasamayaṃ nikkhipitabbanti sañ‍ñāṇaṃ katvā nikkhipitabbaṃ – ‘‘idaṃ ac‍caikacīvara’’nti|

cīvarakālasamayau nāma anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimau māsau, atthatai kathinai pañ‍camāsā|

tatau  cai uttari nikkhipaiyyāti anatthatai kathinai vassānassa pacchimaṃ divasaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ nissaggiyaṃ hauti (syā॰)| atthatai kathinai kathinuddhāradivasaṃ  atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, ac‍caikacīvaraṃ cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

650. ac‍caikacīvarai ac‍caikacīvarasañ‍ñī cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| ac‍caikacīvarai vaimatikau cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| ac‍caikacīvarai anac‍caikacīvarasañ‍ñī cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| anadhiṭṭhitai adhiṭṭhitasañ‍ñī  …pai॰… avikappitai vikappitasañ‍ñī… avissaj‍jitai vissaj‍jitasañ‍ñī… anaṭṭhai naṭṭhasañ‍ñī… avinaṭṭhai vinaṭṭhasañ‍ñī… adaḍḍhai daḍḍhasañ‍ñī… aviluttai viluttasañ‍ñī cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nissaggiyaṃ cīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anac‍caikacīvarai ac‍caikacīvarasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anac‍caikacīvarai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anac‍caikacīvarai anac‍caikacīvarasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

651. anāpatti – antausamayai adhiṭṭhaiti, vikappaiti, vissaj‍jaiti, nassati, vinassati,  ḍayhati, acchinditvā gaṇhanti, vissāsaṃ gaṇhanti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

ac‍caikacīvarasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sāsaṅkasikkhāpadaṃ

652. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū vutthavassā ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu viharanti| kattikacaurakā bhikkhū – ‘‘laddhalābhā’’ti paripātainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu viharantaina tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu viharantaina tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ  añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitu’’nti tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasanti| tāni cīvarāni nassantipi vinassantipi ḍayhantipi undūraihipi khaj‍janti| bhikkhū duc‍cauḷā haunti lūkhacīvarā| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, duc‍cauḷā lūkhacīvarā’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasantīti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasissanti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

653. ‘‘upavassaṃ  khau pana kattikapuṇṇamaṃ yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni sāsaṅkasammatāni sappaṭibhayāni tathārūpaisu bhikkhu saināsanaisu viharantau ākaṅkhamānau tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipaiyya, siyā ca tassa bhikkhunau kaucidaiva pac‍cayau taina cīvaraina vippavāsāya| chārattaparamaṃ taina bhikkhunā taina cīvaraina vippavasitabbaṃ| tatau cai uttari vippavasaiyya, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

654. upavassaṃ khau panāti vuṭṭhavassānaṃ|

kattikapuṇṇamanti kattikacātumāsinī vuc‍cati|

pāci॰ 573 yāni khau pana tāni ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanānīti ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāma saināsanaṃ pañ‍cadhanusatikaṃ pacchimaṃ|

pāci॰ 573 sāsaṅkaṃ nāma ārāmai ārāmūpacārai caurānaṃ niviṭṭhaukāsau dissati, bhuttaukāsau dissati, ṭhitaukāsau dissati, nisin‍naukāsau dissati, nipan‍naukāsau dissati|

pāci॰ 573 sappaṭibhayaṃ  nāma ārāmai ārāmūpacārai cauraihi manussā hatā dissanti, viluttā dissanti, ākauṭitā dissanti|

pāci॰ 573 tathārūpaisu bhikkhu saināsanaisu viharantauti aivarūpaisu bhikkhu saināsanaisu viharantau|

ākaṅkhamānauti  icchamānau|

tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaranti saṅghāṭiṃ vā uttarāsaṅgaṃ vā antaravāsakaṃ vā|

antaragharai nikkhipaiyyāti samantā gaucaragāmai nikkhipaiyya|

siyā ca tassa bhikkhunau kaucidaiva pac‍cayau taina cīvaraina vippavāsāyāti siyā pac‍cayau siyā karaṇīyaṃ|

chārattaparamaṃ  taina bhikkhunā taina cīvaraina vippavasitabbanti chārattaparamatā vippavasitabbaṃ|

añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyāti ṭhapaitvā bhikkhusammutiṃ|

tatau cai uttari vippavasaiyyāti sattamai aruṇuggamanai nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ| ‘‘idaṃ mai, bhantai, cīvaraṃ atiraikachārattaṃ vippavuṭṭhaṃ, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmī’’ti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

655. atiraikachārattai atiraikasañ‍ñī vippavasati, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikachārattai vaimatikau vippavasati, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| atiraikachārattai ūnakasañ‍ñī vippavasati, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ| appac‍cuddhaṭai pac‍cuddhaṭasañ‍ñī…pai॰… avissaj‍jitai vissaj‍jitasañ‍ñī… anaṭṭhai naṭṭhasañ‍ñī… avinaṭṭhai vinaṭṭhasañ‍ñī… adaḍḍhai daḍḍhasañ‍ñī  … aviluttai viluttasañ‍ñī vippavasati, añ‍ñatra bhikkhusammutiyā, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nissaggiyaṃ cīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakachārattai atiraikasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakachārattai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ūnakachārattai ūnakasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

656. anāpatti  – chārattaṃ vippavasati, ūnakachārattaṃ vippavasati, chārattaṃ vippavasitvā puna gāmasīmaṃ auk‍kamitvā vasitvā pak‍kamati, antau chārattaṃ pac‍cuddharati, vissaj‍jaiti, nassati, vinassati, ḍayhati, acchinditvā gaṇhanti, vissāsaṃ gaṇhanti, bhikkhusammutiyā, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

sāsaṅkasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pariṇatasikkhāpadaṃ

657. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghassa sacīvarabhattaṃ paṭiyattaṃ hauti – ‘‘bhaujaitvā cīvaraina acchādaissāmā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yaina sau pūgau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ pūgaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daithāvusau, amhākaṃ imāni cīvarānī’’ti| ‘‘na mayaṃ, bhantai, dassāma| amhākaṃ saṅghassa anuvassaṃ sacīvarabhikkhā pañ‍ñattā’’ti| ‘‘bahū, āvusau, saṅghassa dāyakā, bahū saṅghassa bhattā bhaddā (ka॰)| mayaṃ tumhai nissāya tumhai sampassantā idha viharāma| tumhai cai amhākaṃ na dassatha, atha kau carahi amhākaṃ dassati? daithāvusau, amhākaṃ imāni cīvarānī’’ti| atha khau sau pūgau chabbaggiyaihi bhikkhūhi nippīḷiyamānau yathāpaṭiyattaṃ cīvaraṃ chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ datvā saṅghaṃ bhattaina parivisi| yai tai bhikkhū jānanti saṅghassa sacīvarabhattaṃ paṭiyattaṃ, na ca jānanti chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ din‍nanti, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘auṇaujaithāvusau, saṅghassa cīvara’’nti| ‘‘natthi, bhantai| yathāpaṭiyattaṃ cīvaraṃ ayyā chabbaggiyā attanau pariṇāmaisu’’nti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaithā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau  pariṇāmaissatha! naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, imaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ uddisaiyyātha –

658. ‘‘yau pana bhikkhu jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaiyya, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

659. yau panāti yau yādisau…pai॰… bhikkhūti…pai॰… aya imasmiṃ atthai adhippaitau bhikkhūti|

pāci॰ 491 jānāti  nāma sāmaṃ vā jānāti añ‍ñai vā tassa āraucainti sau vā āraucaiti|

pāci॰ 491 saṅghikaṃ  nāma saṅghassa din‍naṃ hauti paric‍cattaṃ|

pāci॰ 491 lābhau nāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā| antamasau cuṇṇapiṇḍaupi, dantakaṭṭhampi, dasikasuttampi|

pāci॰ 491 pariṇataṃ nāma dassāma karissāmāti vācā bhin‍nā hauti|

attanau pariṇāmaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ| paṭilābhaina nissaggiyaṃ hauti| nissaj‍jitabbaṃ saṅghassa vā gaṇassa vā puggalassa vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nissaj‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ mai, bhantai, jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmitaṃ nissaggiyaṃ| imāhaṃ saṅghassa nissaj‍jāmīti…pai॰… dadaiyyāti…pai॰… dadaiyyunti…pai॰… āyasmatau dammīti|

660. pariṇatai  pariṇatasañ‍ñī attanau pariṇāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pariṇatai vaimatikau attanau pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pariṇatai apariṇatasañ‍ñī attanau pariṇāmaiti, anāpatti| saṅghassa pariṇataṃ añ‍ñasaṅghassa vā caitiyassa vā pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| caitiyassa pariṇataṃ añ‍ñacaitiyassa vā saṅghassa vā puggalassa vā pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| puggalassa pariṇataṃ añ‍ñapuggalassa vā saṅghassa vā caitiyassa vā pariṇāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apariṇatai pariṇatasañ‍ñī, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apariṇatai vaimatikau, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| apariṇatai apariṇatasañ‍ñī, anāpatti|

661. anāpatti  kattha daimāti pucchiyamānau yattha tumhākaṃ daiyyadhammau paribhaugaṃ vā labhaiyya paṭisaṅkhāraṃ vā labhaiyya ciraṭṭhitikau vā assa yattha vā pana tumhākaṃ cittaṃ pasīdati tattha daithāti bhaṇati, ummattakassa, ādikammikassāti|

pariṇatasikkhāpadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

pattavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

dvai ca pattāni bhaisaj‍jaṃ, vassikā dānapañ‍camaṃ|

sāmaṃ vāyāpanac‍caikau, sāsaṅkaṃ saṅghikaina cāti||

662. uddiṭṭhā  khau, āyasmantau, tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā dhammā| tatthāyasmantai pucchāmi – ‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’? dutiyampi pucchāmi – ‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’? tatiyampi pucchāmi – ‘kac‍cittha parisuddhā’? parisuddhaitthāyasmantau, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

nissaggiyakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


pārājikapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
